# Tacet delivery vocabulary — locked

**v1.144 · 2026-09-20 · §195 RETIRES `SHIPS SOON · 现订现发` FROM THE BROWSE BAND AND COMPOSES NOTHING.** Two surfaces said it while five said `PRE-ORDER · 可预订` for the same shelf state, so a shopper tapping a card watched the word change under them. The live pair on the majority wins; no Chinese is minted, and the retired pair keeps its other job as the checkout popup's title. The band also gains the restock window — the same numerals and the same live `天` §194 declared, in the band's upper case. See *A pre-order's restock window* and *One word for a reservable row*. **v1.143 · 2026-09-20 · §194 GIVES THE PRE-ORDER POPUP ITS RESTOCK WINDOW — COMPOSING ONE CLAUSE OUT OF LIVE WORDS AND LEAVING THE SENTENCE AROUND IT VERBATIM.** The popup became the tenant's to edit, and the one thing it could never say was roughly when the item comes back. The sentence it has shipped since `backorder_gz` existed is untouched; a four-character clause is inserted into it, and `预计`, `内` and `补货` are each already live and customer-facing. The window itself is a numeral and the live day counter. See *A pre-order's restock window*. **v1.142 · 2026-09-19 · §193 GIVES `parcel_awaiting_restock` THE SENTENCE IT NEVER HAD — LIFTING TWO LIVE FRAGMENTS AND COMPOSING NOTHING.** §187 minted the error code and registered no pair for it, so a Chinese admin has read the bare code in English ever since; §193 makes the JD label route a third caller, which is the busiest of the three. Both halves are live verbatim and only the join is new. See *A parcel the shortage is holding*.

**v1.141 · 2026-09-19 · §190 NAMES THE BOX IN §188's NOTIFICATION — COMPOSING THREE SENTENCES AND NO NEW WORDS.** *"one of the parcels"* made a split order's customer count boxes to work out which one had landed. Every mode already has a locked pair and two of them are already customer-facing on the printed slip, so each sentence is a live mode word in front of §188's live `包裹已发货`. The generic line survives as the fallback for a mode added later. See *Which box has gone*.

**v1.140 · 2026-09-19 · §189 NAMES THE BOX THAT WENT OUT ON A CANCELLED ORDER — COPYING ONE PAIR AND COMPOSING SEVEN ON IT.** §187 stopped a cancel crediting the shelf with units in a courier's van, which made the count honest and told nobody. `收回` is live and is exactly this verb about exactly this object, so everything here is built on it; `退回` is kept for what the STOCK does, and the two are deliberately not collapsed. See *A box that went out on an order that was cancelled*.

**v1.139 · 2026-09-19 · §188 TELLS THE PARKED ORDER'S CUSTOMER THAT ONE BOX HAS GONE — COMPOSING TWO PAIRS FROM LIVE PARTS.** §187 let a parked order send the parcels its shortage does not touch and said nothing to the customer, because the order keeps its park and the `dispatched` line never fires. A box arriving unannounced days before the rest reads as a short delivery. Two composed pairs, every word in them live; in-app only, no WeChat push, so `ORDER_STATUS_NOTIF` is untouched. See *One box of a parked order has left*.

**v1.138 · 2026-09-18 · §182 SAYS UNDER THE FINANCE REVENUE ROW WHEN A WALLET YUAN IS COUNTED — LIFTING ONE PAIR WHOLE AND COMPOSING ONE.** Ash, with both screens open: *"the 30 days revenue in finance section are not identital to the total revenue in report"*. They were not, they still are not, and neither was wrong — the two answer different questions, and until now neither said so. **THE REPORTS HALF COMPOSES NOTHING.** `incl. ¥X top-ups · 含充值 ¥X` is taken CHARACTER FOR CHARACTER from `public/retail-admin/01-core.js`, where it already sits under the Sales summary's own cash-basis revenue figure disclosing the same fold — the ' · ' join is that line's too. Rule 4's first preference, and here one screen had already said this well; the only change is which card it sits on. **ONE PAIR IS COMPOSED, by Claude, under the fourth amendment's standing delegation** — `仅计订单 —— 储值在消费时计入，充值时不计入。` — because nothing in this product has ever had to say *this figure counts the sale, not the top-up*. The state was real and had no sentence. Every fragment is live: `仅计` from `仅计成本` in the recipe cost lines, `计入` and `不计入` from the six live cost-and-stock sentences that use the pair (`其成本不计入`, `破损数量不计入库存`, `已登记发票，未计入上方`), and `储值` from `储值消费` — the Sales summary's own word for store credit spent, so the two screens spend a wallet with the same character. The `——` is `finCoverageNote`'s own dash, eleven lines up the same file. **THREE CANDIDATES WERE REJECTED AND THE REJECTIONS ARE RECORDED:** `含充值`, live and lifted for the OTHER screen in this same change, which states the opposite fact and on this row would be false; `未计入上方数字`, live in this very file, which reads as a warning that something is missing from the total rather than a statement of which question it answers; and `收入`, which Ruling 1 reserves for Income in the Finance table and which is not this row's word — the row is `营收`. **NOTHING IS RETIRED.** Rules 1 to 7 unchanged. See *When a wallet yuan is counted* at the end.

**v1.137 · 2026-09-18 · §181 PUTS THE SHOP SWITCH ON THE PHONE AND NAMES THE CLOSURE THAT OUTRANKS EVERY CHANNEL — COPYING EIGHT PAIRS, RESTRUCTURING TWO AND COMPOSING FIVE.** Ash: *"in store I believe that's counter till? when I open yuexiu it close automatic"*, then *"I want to have same feature of closing and open shop as I have on PC should work on the phone"*. **MOST OF IT IS LIFTED, AND THAT IS THE DECISION** — the phone already had five of these sentences in the per-channel confirm directly beside the new one, so the shop switch says the same kind of thing about a bigger unit in the same words: `门店` from the In store channel's own name, `门店已关闭` from `04-orders.js`'s reroute chip, `变更排队中` from the desktop's own word for an unapplied `target_open`, plus `重试`, `知道了`, the `X 已开启` / `X 已关闭` toasts and the `开启` / `关闭` labels. **TWO ARE RESTRUCTURED, Rule 4's first preference taken:** the confirm title `在 X 开启 Y？` loses its channel clause because the shop is the whole unit, and the screen footer becomes `一次性关闭整个渠道在电脑端`, from the sheet's live `一次性关闭整个渠道请在电脑端操作。` — the sentence it replaces claimed opening and closing a channel was on desktop at all, which the card beneath it had already stopped being true. **FIVE ARE COMPOSED, by Claude, under the fourth amendment's standing delegation**, four of them because nothing in this product has ever had to say *this shop is shut, so nothing here sells* — the state was real and had no sentence. Each is assembled from live fragments: `所有渠道均不营业` negates the live `所有渠道均已开启` on its own pattern; the blocked-row explainer quotes the section header in 「」 so it names what is on screen; and both shop confirms END on the clause that is the point of them — the till, the pickups and the reroute — because this switch writes every channel at the shop AND the shop-wide fulfilment flag, and *close this shop* must not be read as *close this shop's website*. **ONE MINTED STRING WAS THROWN OUT AND THE REJECTION IS RECORDED:** `销售看板加载失败。` for the live `加载失败，请重试。` — `销售看板` is not a term this product has; the screen is `销售`. Also rejected: `全部关闭`, live but already the whole-channel phrase and therefore the opposite unit; `店铺`, which is not this product's word for a shop anywhere. Nothing is retired except the footer sentence restructured above. See *The shop switch, on the phone* at the end. Rules 1 to 7 unchanged. **v1.136 · 2026-09-18 · §180 GIVES A PARCEL ITS OWN PREPARED STEP, LIFTING ONE PAIR FROM THE SOURCE AND REGISTERING THREE THAT SHIPPED UNRECORDED.** Ash: *"when order has 2 split package one shipped one parked at 'paid'... it create a problem with stock managment."* **`已备货` IS LIFTED FROM `reference/roots_v1.html`, WHICH IS THE SOURCE SYSTEM AND USED IT FOR THIS EXACT STATE** — goods picked and ready, not yet moved (`prepared:'已备货 Prepared'` in its warehouse status labels, and `标记已备货` on its own button). That is the fourth amendment's SECOND preference taken, not its third: nothing is composed here. It is reinforced by two live strings in this product that already use the same act — `备货中` (the order status) and `单待备货` on the dashboard — so the parcel pill and the order status read in one vocabulary. **THE BUTTON IS THE LIVE SIBLING WITH ONE WORD SWAPPED:** `标记此包裹已备货` against the shipping `标记此包裹已发货` eight lines above it in the same card, so an operator reads the two as one pair of acts on one box. **TWO CANDIDATES WERE REJECTED AND THE REJECTIONS ARE RECORDED:** `待发货` — live, but already taken for the order list's *to ship* COUNT (`public/retail-admin/04-orders.js`, `public/m.html`), and it names a queue position rather than the box's own state, so a packed box and an untouched one would read alike; and `已打包`, which is live twice but means the physical wrapping and, in a shop that sells food, reads as takeaway packaging. **THREE PILLS ARE REGISTERED THAT WERE NEVER IN THIS DOCUMENT** — `未发货`, `已发货`, `已送达` as they appear on a PARCEL rather than an order. They have shipped since split packages landed, in `04-orders.js` and `m.html` only, and an unregistered string is indistinguishable from a locked one to whoever comes next; that is the gap this amendment closes alongside the new pair. **NOTHING IS RETIRED** — `未发货` still reads on every parcel that has not been packed, and the restock hold pill still overrides both. See *A parcel is packed* at the end. Rules 1 to 7 unchanged. **v1.135 · 2026-09-18 · THE TWO NEW SETTINGS HOSTS GAIN A HEADING AND A LINE, BOTH LIFTED, NOTHING COMPOSED.** Ash: *"can you fix waste toggle now ?"* — the Waste switch mounted correctly and could not be found, because a host with no `.group-card-head h3` and no `.muted` line is invisible to `indexCards()`, which is what builds Settings search. Each host now carries a card. **BOTH CHINESE LINES ARE LIFTED FROM COPY ALREADY SHIPPING and nothing is minted.** `Waste · 损耗` and `Running low here · 此门店库存偏低` are the two features' own catalog titles, unchanged. The Waste description is **the FIRST SENTENCE of §32's catalog `desc`, taken whole** — `登记损耗会变动库存:库存数量相应减少,并同步至各销售渠道。` — with the two sentences after it dropped rather than reworded, because the switch's own `descOn`/`descOff` already say what each state does and a card that repeats them teaches the operator to stop reading either. The low-stock description is **v1.134's `desc` entire**, unchanged, which is the sentence that pair was written for. **NOTHING IS RETIRED AND NOTHING MOVES**: every source string stays exactly where it was and still reads there; these are second homes, not relocations. Rules 1 to 7 unchanged.**v1.134 · 2026-09-18 · §178 MAKES THE RUNNING-LOW STRIP A TENANT SWITCH, COPYING ITS TITLE, RESTRUCTURING THREE SENTENCES AND COMPOSING THREE.** Ash, on a screenshot of RPO-0046: *"i want this feature to be able to open and close for each tenant. roots doesnt ned it."* **THE SWITCH IS NAMED AFTER THE THING IT SWITCHES, character for character** — `Running low here · 此门店库存偏低`, copied whole from the strip's own kicker in `public/retail-admin/16-purchasing.js`, so an operator turning it off never has to work out which block on the order is meant; a test fails if the strip is re-worded and the switch is not. **THREE PAIRS ARE RESTRUCTURED, NOT COMPOSED**, which is the fourth amendment's first preference taken: the two empty-line sentences and the no-orders card's step 1 each lose the clause that promises a suggestion (`或选择推荐`, `——库存偏低的会自动推荐`) and keep every character of the half that is still true — with the strip off, a sentence pointing at it sends the operator hunting for a control that is not on the screen. **THREE ARE COMPOSED, by Claude, under the fourth amendment's standing delegation**: the settings bar's `desc`, `descOn` and `descOff`, each assembled from live fragments — `采购单`, `顶部`, `库存偏低的…推荐`, `已开启:` / `已关闭:`, `不再`, `自行`, `搜索`, `…即可` and `其余部分不受影响。`, with `可直接添加。` the live `可直接修改。` carrying this screen's own verb. **`descOff` ends by saying the rest of the order is untouched**, because a switch that removes something from a purchase order invites the fear that it removes more. TWO CANDIDATES WERE REJECTED AND THE REJECTIONS ARE RECORDED: `推荐补货`, because `补货` is the Reorder tab's name and §178 deliberately does not gate that tab; and `最缺的四款商品`, which names a count that is an implementation detail. Nothing is retired — both long sentences stay live and are what every tenant sees by default. See *Turning the running-low strip off* at the end. Rules 1 to 7 unchanged. **v1.133 · 2026-09-18 · §177 MOVES A MENU TO ANOTHER LOCATION, LIFTING FOUR PAIRS AND COMPOSING FOUR.** Ash, on a screenshot of Sales → Locations: *"the shop menu should move form tianhe to yuexiu."* Four pairs are verbatim — `Location · 门店`, `Cancel · 取消`, `Saving… · 保存中…` and the success toast `Moved · 已移动`, **taken from `catMoveProduct`, which moves a product between shelves** and is the nearest live act in the product to this one. **The button reads `Move products · 移动商品`, never `Move menu`**: §177 refuses to move the shop record, so a button promising the menu would promise the thing the section refuses and the operator would learn otherwise from a dead QR. **One verb throughout** — 移动 in all four places rather than 迁移 in some, because the toast it ends on is the live `已移动`, and `没有可迁移的角色` is therefore lifted for its 没有可…的 shape and not for its verb. **The one long composition, the cost warning, is assembled from live clauses**: 将被清空 whole from `16-purchasing.js`'s undo-receiving warning, 停用 from this screen's own Inactive pill put in the future tense, and 二维码 / 编码 / 重新打印 all already live — what is composed is the order they come in. **A second live candidate was rejected and the rejection is recorded**: `暂无其他门店开通同城配送` was considered for a "nowhere to move this" line and is not needed at all, because the control is hidden when no destination is eligible — a string with no screen to sit on beats a string explaining why a button did nothing. Three of the four composed pairs are `TD_ERR` refusals; the public QR menu gains no string, since a moved-away shop serves the not-found page it already had. Rules 1 to 7 unchanged. **v1.132 · 2026-09-18 · the replacement is picked line by line and its freight is typed, COPYING FOUR PAIRS AND COMPOSING FIVE.** Ash, against TC000750: *"automatic create an order for all products, maybe the replacement is only for few items?"* and *"didnt ask me for shipping fee"*. §98's `Send replacement` was a browser confirm that sent the whole parcel; it is a sheet now, with a quantity per line and the freight beside them. **THE SHEET IS ALMOST ENTIRELY LIVE WORDS AND THAT IS THE POINT** — `补发`, `数量`, `此包裹内含`, `取消` and the quantity refusal `每行数量需为 1 或以上。` are already shipping, four of them on this very panel, so the sheet reads as the row it opened from. **The body is the live confirm RESTRUCTURED, not re-composed**: `发出同样商品` had to go because the goods are no longer necessarily the same ones. Five are COMPOSED, by Claude, under the fourth amendment's standing delegation, every one assembled from live fragments. `运费` was live and is kept for the FEE a customer pays; `运费成本` is what the freight costs us, which is the figure the case has always been about. See *Picking a replacement — the late-parcel case* at the end. Nothing is retired. Rules 1 to 7 unchanged. **v1.131 · 2026-09-18 · THE BUYER'S WANTED-BY DAY READS `订购`, NOT `截止` — RETIRING ONE LIFT FOR ANOTHER, COMPOSING NOTHING.** Ash, reading the Ask to buy list: *"中文“截止”改成“订购”"*. v1.129 lifted `截止` from `10-extras.js`'s vote row, where it closes a poll; on a purchasing queue that same word reads as a cutoff after which the line lapses, and a kitchen ask does not lapse. `订购` is LIFTED WHOLE from this same file's own receiving column (`Ordered · 订购` in `public/retail-admin/16-purchasing.js`, and `ordered · 订购` in `public/m.html`'s receiving rows), where it already names the buying act. The word order is unchanged and still the live one — day first, word after (`2026-09-30 订购`) — so the cell stays a whole phrase per language against the English `by 30 Sep`. FLAGGED AND NOT CHANGED: the English side still reads `by`, a deadline, while the Chinese now names the act; `2026-09-30 前订购` would carry both, and Ash asked for the single word. `截止` is retired from the purchasing cell only and is untouched everywhere else — the vote row, the coupon end date and the shipping header still carry it. Nothing is composed. Rules 1 to 7 unchanged. **v1.130 · 2026-09-18 · AN EMPTY BLOCK OFFERS `Add`, NOT `Edit` — REUSING A LIVE PAIR, COMPOSING NOTHING.** v1.125's heading shipped beside a button still reading `Edit · 编辑`, which on an empty block names an action that cannot be performed — there is nothing there to edit. A bare Know block now reads `Add · 添加`, **the same pair the tag row's own Add button uses eight lines below it** in `public/retail-admin/08-customers.js`. `编辑` is unchanged and returns the moment a note or tag exists. No new pair, no amendment, nothing retired. Rules 1 to 7 unchanged. **v1.129 · 2026-09-18 · §176 puts urgency and a wanted-by day on every Ask to buy line, LIFTING THREE PAIRS AND COMPOSING THREE.** Ash: *"need to add a mark that mention urgent order so buying department will know to priorities"*, then *"each item add an option to add buying date... this item can be bought later, its not urgent"*. Shape C was chosen from three; §176 carries the reasoning and the objection that survives it. LIFTED WHOLE: `Urgent · 加急` from this file's own `加急订单` in `public/m.html`'s kitchen purpose picker, where it already means a claim on somebody else's queue position; `Pick a date · 选日期` from `public/retail-admin/05-marketing.js`'s end-date chips; and `截止` from `10-extras.js`'s vote row, which prints it AFTER the day (`${votCloses(p)} 截止`) — that word order is the live one and is why the buyer's cell carries a whole phrase per language (`by 30 Sep` / `2026-09-30 截止`) rather than one word glued to a date. THREE ARE COMPOSED, by Claude, under the fourth amendment's standing delegation: `什么时候要` — the buyer's column heading, built from the live `什么时候` (`public/shop.html`'s `什么时候送`, `public/m.html`'s `什么时候发送？`) and `要`; it names a question rather than a field because the column holds either mark, and the candidates rejected for it were the live `截止日期`, which names only one of the two and is empty on an urgent line, and `需要日期`. `需要日期` — the aria-label on the phone's day field, where a heading's question shape reads wrong on a control and the label must name what the field holds. `项加急` — the foot's urgent count, from the live `项` this tally already uses for its item count and the lifted `加急`, so that `2 项 · 1 项加急` reads in one measure word throughout; `1 个加急` was rejected for mixing two. NO CHINESE IS MINTED for the day itself: `9月30日` is a numeral and two characters every calendar in the app already uses. Nothing is retired. Rules 1 to 7 unchanged.

**v1.128 · 2026-09-18 · the Ask to buy picker prints the material's unit in Chinese, LIFTING FIFTEEN PAIRS WHOLE AND COMPOSING NOTHING.** Ash, from the kitchen: *"chinese version need to show chinese unit"*. `ingredients.buy_unit` is stored in English because `lib/ingredient-cost.js` validates it and `firePriceAlertsForPO` matches a purchase-order line's price write-back on it — so the value stays English on the wire and only the PRINTED word changes. Every one of the fifteen is LIFTED verbatim from `PO_UNIT_CN` in `public/retail-admin/16-purchasing.js`, which has carried them since the printed purchase order fixed the same leak for itself: `kg 公斤`, `g 克`, `L 升`, `ml 毫升`, `lb 磅`, `oz 盎司`, `bottle 瓶`, `bag 包`, `box 盒`, `can 罐`, `jar 罐`, `case 箱`, `pack 包`, `tub 桶`, `pcs 件`. Nothing is minted and nothing is retired. THE TWO MAPS ARE NOW A PAIR THAT MUST MOVE TOGETHER: a unit added to `MEASURE_BUY_UNITS` or `CONTAINER_BUY_UNITS` and to only one of them prints English on a Chinese row, so `scripts/test_ask_to_buy_keyboard.js` case 14 reads both lists out of `lib/ingredient-cost.js` and fails on the first unmapped one. An unmapped unit falls through to its English self rather than disappearing — the right failure, and still a wrong-looking row. Rules 1 to 7 unchanged.

**v1.127 · 2026-09-18 · the cook is told what became of the ask, COPYING FOUR PAIRS AND COMPOSING FOUR.** Ash, on §174's own flagged gap: *"the silence is the weak point, not the delete"*. The kitchen's new phone screen reports each line of a sent material request. THE FOUR STATE WORDS ARE COPIES AND THAT IS THE POINT — `待处理`, `已下单`, `已移除` and `已撤回` are the words already shipping for those same four facts, three of them on the buyer's own box, because a line reading `已移除` to the buyer and something else to the cook is two screens disagreeing about one event. Four are COMPOSED, by Claude, under the fourth amendment's standing delegation, all assembled from live fragments: the screen's name, its two empty-state lines and its read failure. `等待` was live, considered and rejected in favour of the `待处理` already on the kitchen's other waiting list one row up. See *What became of what you asked for — the kitchen's phone* at the end. Nothing is retired. Rules 1 to 7 unchanged. **v1.126 · 2026-09-18 · the buyer can strike one kitchen line off, COPYING TWO PAIRS AND COMPOSING FOUR.** Ash, on the From-the-kitchen box: *"cant delete the request directly in this box"*, and, asked at which grain, he chose one line. `Remove · 移除` and `Removed ✓ · 已移除 ✓` are LIFTED WHOLE from the staff-removal control that already shipped them. Four are COMPOSED, by Claude, under the fourth amendment's standing delegation, all four assembled from live fragments rather than minted: the confirm's title and body, and the two line-level refusals `already_sourced` and `line_declined` in the admin's error catalogue. `删除`, `作废` and `撤回` were each live, each considered and each rejected — the last of the three stays reserved for withdrawing the WHOLE request, which is the grain §174 exists to separate this from. See *Striking a kitchen line off — purchasing* at the end. Nothing is retired. Rules 1 to 7 unchanged. **v1.125 · 2026-09-18 · THE DESKTOP KNOW BLOCK TAKES THE HEADING THE PHONE ALREADY HAD, REUSING A LOCKED PAIR AND COMPOSING NOTHING.** Ash, on the customer page with the cursor inches from the control: *“im on customer page wher is it”*. With nothing stored, the desktop's note-and-tags block collapsed to a bare `Edit · 编辑` under the customer's name and named neither of the two things behind it. `What we know · 备注与标签` — **already locked in the customers table below, and live on `public/m.html`'s customer record as the heading of this same block** — now labels it on the desktop too, shown only while the block is empty. **No new pair, no amendment**: this is the existing one arriving on its second surface, which is what a locked pair is FOR. The empty `暂无备注。` was considered and NOT used, and the reason is worth keeping: it names the ABSENCE of a note, which an operator looking at an empty block can already see, where the heading names the CONTROL. Rules 1 to 7 unchanged. **v1.124 · 2026-09-18 · §172 reads the customer's STANDING note on every order, LIFTING ONE PAIR AND COMPOSING NOTHING.** Ash: *“we need a note that will appear for this customer order everytime he place an order”*. `retail_customers.note` already existed and no order read it; §172 reads it on the order drawer (desktop and phone) beside the two per-order notes it must never be confused with. The label is `About this customer · 关于该顾客的备注`, and its Chinese half is **LIFTED WHOLE** from the live aria-label on the customer drawer's own note textarea (`public/retail-admin/08-customers.js`) — the same string on the field where the text is TYPED and the field where it is READ, which is the strongest form of the rule: one string, one meaning, two screens. The English half is new; the Chinese is not, so **nothing is minted and nothing is locked**. Candidates REJECTED, and why: `顾客备注` (live, `Customer note`) — already means the note the BUYER typed at checkout, and reusing it would make the two indistinguishable at a glance, which is the exact failure §172 exists to prevent; `店铺备注` (live, `Shop note`) — same objection, per-order; `顾客长期备注` (COMPOSED, used in the mock-up shown to Ash) — dropped once the live aria-label was found, on the standing preference for a live pair over a composed one. The tag chips carry no vocabulary: tags print exactly as the operator stored them, in whichever language they typed. Nothing is retired. Rules 1 to 7 unchanged. **v1.123 · 2026-09-18 · §170 dates the NEXT BATCH band on every browse surface, LIFTING FOUR FORMS AND COMPOSING NOTHING.** Ash: *“a Shenzhen shopper sees NEXT BATCH with no date attached until they're deep in checkout”*. The band now carries when the row ARRIVES — the make day for the same-day local run, the day after for a couriered order — and every word of it is already live. `TODAY · 今天` and `TOMORROW · 明天` are LIFTED whole from `miniapp/pages/checkout/checkout.wxml:400`, where the slot-day chip has drawn them as a pair since the day picker shipped. The Chinese date form `9月20日` is LIFTED from `miniapp/pages/checkout/checkout.js:1911` (`label_cn: `${m}月${d}日``), the same shape the express slot and every day-only pick already print; its English counterpart is an ordinary month abbreviation and locks nothing. The separator is the live ` · ` the band already uses in `供应商直发 · 单独送达`. `NEXT BATCH · 下批现做` itself is §108’s and is unchanged. **NOTHING IS COMPOSED and nothing is retired**, so the standing delegation of the fourth amendment is not drawn on here — this entry exists so the four lifts can be found from this end rather than only from §170. CANDIDATES REJECTED, recorded because the next session will reach for them: a relative count (`在 3 天内` / `IN 3 DAYS`) — no live pair, and it makes the shopper do the arithmetic the date already did; `送达` appended to the day word (`明天送达`, live only inside longer sentences such as `今天送达,最快配送`) — correct Chinese, but it would have been a composition where a bare lift says the same thing in a band with no room to spare. Rules 1 to 7 unchanged.**



**v1.122 · 2026-09-18 · adds a Channels section to Trend, COMPOSING ONE PAIR AND LIFTING THE REST WHOLE.** Ash: *"sales show revenue only of mini app, why not showing each channel revenue?"*. It never showed only the mini app — the payment share beside it is every channel, split by how the money ARRIVED — but a split by where the order CAME FROM did not exist for a window anywhere. `Channels · 渠道` is LIFTED from `public/retail-admin.html`, and every channel name is lifted from `public/m.html`'s own CHAN_META (`Mini app · 小程序`, `Web store · 网店`, `Meituan · 美团`, `Eleme · 饿了么`, `gzgrocery ERP · gzgrocery`, `In store · 门店`), keyed on the same value the server groups on so a channel reads identically on both screens. ONE pair is COMPOSED, by Claude, under the fourth amendment's standing delegation: `钱包充值（X）计入上方合计，但不属于任何渠道。` — the line saying why the rows cannot add up to the headline, built from the live `钱包充值`, `合计` and `渠道`. Nothing is retired. Rules 1 to 7 unchanged. **v1.121 · 2026-09-18 · RENAMES ONE ROW FOR ITS CHANNEL AND RETIRES THE PAIR THAT NAMED A TRADE, COMPOSING ONE.** Ash, on §169's buyers card: *"bought on gzgrocery I would call it as the channel name. not wholesale"*. Every other row there names WHERE the order came from — the shop, Meituan — and `Bought wholesale · 批发购买` named the kind of trade instead, which is a different question from the two it sits between. RETIRED, and `Bought on gzgrocery · gzgrocery 购买` composed in its place: the channel's own name, lifted from its live pair in `public/m.html`'s CHAN_META (`gzgrocery ERP · gzgrocery`), with the live `购买` it shares with the rows above it. No Chinese is minted — `gzgrocery` is already the Chinese half of that channel's name. **A second change locks no string at all**: the Meituan caveat `；美团自 X 起` now prints only when the id genuinely starts LATER than the window, because `since` equal to the window's own first day restates the range and reads as a limit that is not there. The pair is unchanged; when it says nothing it renders nothing. Rules 1 to 7 unchanged. **v1.120 · 2026-09-17 · adds a section, *Buyers, by the channel that can count them*, COMPOSING SIX PAIRS AND RETIRING NONE.** §169 splits one "People bought" into a per-channel breakdown, because `customer_id` — the column every customer figure counts — is one neither marketplace route writes, so the single number was the storefront's wearing an all-channel label. Composed, by Claude, under the fourth amendment's standing delegation: `自有渠道购买`, `美团购买`, `批发购买`, `N 新客`, the desktop tile's `自有渠道客户`, and the note `分渠道统计 —— 同一个人在不同渠道无法匹配。饿了么不提供顾客信息；美团自 X 起。` Every morpheme is lifted — `自有` from `自有域名`, `分渠道` and `无法匹配` from the admin, `美团`/`批发`/`购买`/`新客` from their own live pairs — and what is composed is the compound. NOTHING IS RETIRED: the old `People bought · 购买人数` pair and its note stay as the fallback a server predating the new block still renders. Rules 1 to 7 unchanged. **v1.119 · 2026-09-17 · adds a section, *Making one, on the phone*, COMPOSING TWO PAIRS.** §167 gives a recipe a *Make it* screen — scale the batch, tick things off as you gather them, work the steps one at a time. Almost all of its words were already live, and the best of them is the step counter: `Step N of M · 第 N 步,共 M 步` is LIFTED WHOLE from the broadcast composer, which has carried that exact pair since v1.x. `Back · 返回`, `Next · 下一步`, `Done · 完成`, `None · 无` and `Log a batch · 登记批次` are the app's own too. Only TWO pairs are COMPOSED, by Claude, under the fourth amendment's standing delegation, and each is two live words joined rather than anything minted: `Make it · 开始制作` and `Start the steps · 开始步骤`. Live candidates considered and rejected are recorded in the section. NO vocabulary was minted for the mockup's per-step ingredient list or its rest timer, because neither is built — `recipe_steps` has no step→ingredient link and no timer column, so both would have been invention dressed as data. Rules 1 to 7 unchanged. **v1.118 · 2026-09-17 · adds a section, *Recipes reach the phone, read-only*, COMPOSING TWO PAIRS.** §164's screen reaches Tacet Mobile as a list and a record — what a thing is made from, in what quantities, in what order, and no money at all for anybody. TWO pairs are COMPOSED, by Claude, under the fourth amendment's standing delegation, and both are one live noun substituted into a live sentence: `Search recipes · 搜索食谱` is the phone's own `Search products · 搜索商品` with `商品` → `食谱`, and `Could not load recipes. · 无法加载食谱。` is its own `Could not load waste records. · 无法加载损耗记录。` with `损耗记录` → `食谱`. Live candidates considered and rejected are recorded in the section. Rules 1 to 7 unchanged. **v1.117 · 2026-09-17 · adds a section, *A required choice is part of what the product costs*, COMPOSING TWO PAIRS.** §166 makes the product editor's margin add what a product's REQUIRED choices cost us, because modelling a chosen dressing as a choice moves that cost out of the recipe and the margin would otherwise read better than the product is. `含必选项 ¥X` and `毛利 — 必选项暂无成本` are COMPOSED, by Claude, under the fourth amendment's standing delegation, and every half is lifted rather than minted: `必选` from the checkbox that sets the very flag being read, on the same screen; `暂无成本` from the waste screen's own words for the identical fact; `含` from `̅3919`'s live `含其中的最近使用`. Only `必选项` is assembled, out of `必选` and `选项` sharing their `选`. `必填` was rejected for not being live in this tree, and `加价` for being the CUSTOMER's word on a screen that shows both figures. Live candidates rejected are recorded in the section. Rules 1 to 7 unchanged. **v1.116 · 2026-09-17 · adds a section, *Three numbers, three populations*, COMPOSING FIVE PAIRS AND RETIRING FOUR.** §165 fixes three labels that described something other than the figure beneath them: a revenue-ordered product ranking called *Top seller · 最热销*, a payment share whose denominator excluded the wholesale ledger and top-up cash that the headline above it included, and three metrics in one chip row drawn from three different channel populations with nothing saying so. Every character is lifted; what is composed is the compound. RETIRES `Top seller · 最热销`, `Top products · 热销商品`, `units · 件数` and `share of revenue · 占营收`. No figure changes value. The new capability needs a row in the role editor and the coin needs a sentence, and neither existed. `See recipe cost & margin · 查看食谱成本与毛利` and `Recipes show batch cost, cost per gram and margin. · 食谱显示整批成本、每克成本与毛利。` are COMPOSED, by Claude, under the fourth amendment's standing delegation — both assembled from live material rather than minted: the first is the live `products:costs` pair with its one noun swapped for the noun `recipes:view` already carries, the second is that same key's own note with the Recipes list's live column headings in it. Live candidates considered and rejected are recorded in the section. `A recipe is a cost. · 食谱本身就是成本。` is RETIRED — it justified a coin that has moved, and the replacement above is about the key it moved to. Rules 1 to 7 are unchanged. **v1.57 · 2026-09-11 · ADDS an amendment section, "Not today" on a waiting fapiao, locking ONE COMPOSED PAIR and recording ONE LIFTED for §99's snooze.** The control that defers a fapiao push to tomorrow, on the phone's Invoices screen. **ONE STRING CARRIES BOTH STATES**, because the control is the app's own `.chip` and its `on` fill already says "set" — a second pair for the set state (`已推迟`, `已暂停`) was drafted and dropped as a new word for what the fill says, neither candidate being live here. **NO DATE IS PRINTED**: the button promises tomorrow and the server writes tomorrow, and a timestamp beside it would be a second source for one fact. `明天提醒我` is an arrangement of `明天` and `提醒我` (`应用关闭时也提醒我`); `提醒已恢复` is lifted from `发送测试提醒` and `整单已恢复待开票`. **`忽略` AND `跳过` WERE REJECTED AND THE REASON IS THE FEATURE**: both describe a dismissal, and a dismissal is exactly what this is not — a swipe writes nothing, and a word that made the two sound alike would undo the distinction §99 rests on. Rules 1 to 7 are unchanged. **v1.56 · 2026-09-11 · ADDS an amendment section, The fapiao that asks again, locking TWO COMPOSED PAIRS and recording ONE LIFTED VERBATIM for §99's fapiao chase.** The Web Push an operator now gets when a 发票 a customer asked for has not been issued, and the switch that mutes it. **THREE PAIRS FOR A WHOLE FEATURE IS THE POINT, NOT AN OVERSIGHT**: the ordinary push title is the live pair Home's row has always printed, the per-order body is an order number, a sum and a name — none of which needs a label in either language — the digest body is a count and an age carrying the live `最久` tail, and Home's month-end escalation adds no string at all because the colour is the message. Rules 1 to 7 are unchanged. **v1.55 · 2026-09-11 · ADDS an amendment section, A carrier's compensation in the ledger, locking TWO COMPOSED PAIRS for §98 stage four.** The refusals the Transactions screen answers with when somebody tries to edit or delete the `income_entries` row a delay case posted — the first projection this ledger has ever had on the money-IN side. **BOTH ARE THE CLAIM REFUSAL WITH ONE NOUN PHRASE SWAPPED**: `费用申请产生的支出不能直接编辑` is live in that same catalogue for the money-OUT projection, so `费用申请` becomes `承运商赔偿` (locked in v1.54) and `支出` becomes `收入` (live in eight files), with `产生的`, `不能直接编辑` and `不能直接删除` carried across untouched — the parallel being the point, since a bookkeeper who has met one refusal should recognise the other as the same rule about a different document. `承运商赔偿` is REUSED, not re-minted: one fact, one pair, now three places. **NOTHING WAS COMPOSED FOR A CATEGORY** — the obvious move was an income category reading *carrier compensation*, and the income list ships empty by §36 so the form cannot offer a wrong option, so the posted row carries none and a pair minted here would have seeded that list by the back door. Composed under Rule 4's standing delegation, by Claude Opus 5. Composed under Rule 4's standing delegation, by Claude Opus 5. Rules 1 to 7 are unchanged. **v1.53 · 2026-09-10 · ADDS an amendment section, Paying with points or the wallet at the counter, locking TWO COMPOSED PAIRS and recording TWO LIFTED VERBATIM for §93's store credit at the till.** The four are the refusals `POST /api/retail-admin/orders` can now answer with when a hand-keyed order tries to spend points or wallet balance, and they render on both order forms — neither of which can actually cause them, the counter till being the caller that can. **`积分不足。` and `余额不足。` ARE NOT COMPOSED** — the first is quoted whole from the admin's own shared error catalogue for this same code, the second is the pair the web storefront and the mini app both already ship for it. The two that are composed are arrangements of words already live in the tree — `积分`, `余额`, `顾客`, `账户`, `金额`, `请选择`, `付款方式`, `剩余`, and `不足` in the shape `积分不足` — checked against the tree rather than assumed. They exist because no screen in this product has ever had to say that a tender fell short of the bill: until §93 no hand-keyed order had a tender that could. Rules 1 to 7 are unchanged. **v1.52 · 2026-09-10 · ADDS an amendment section, Who put the row in the books, locking TWO COMPOSED PAIRS for §92.** `审批人` and `录入人` name the person a ledger row records as having put it there — the approver on a claim-posted row, the typist on a hand-typed one. **BOTH ARE A LIVE WORD PLUS THE SUFFIX OF A LIVE PAIR ON THE SAME FEATURE, which is the cheapest a new pair gets after an outright lift.** `审批` ships on the approvals screen as `待审批`, `审批通过后即记入本月账目。` and `审批费用申请`; `录入` ships in this register as `可录入交易` (the Bookkeeper role, which is this exact act on this exact screen), `仅手动录入` and `可随时录入库存`; and the `人` suffix is lifted from `Asked by · 申请人`, the approvals queue's own column for the other person on the same claim. **THE TWO PAIRS EXIST SEPARATELY BECAUSE THEY NAME TWO DIFFERENT ACTS** — approving somebody else's spending is an authority §87 built a capability around, typing a row is not — and §92 records why one label over both was refused. **NO THIRD PAIR FOR AN UNRESOLVED NAME**: the row renders no meta at all, §1.12 preferring an empty cell to an invented one, and the live `Someone · 未知` stays where it belongs on the approvals queue. Rules 1 to 7 are unchanged. v1.29 · 2026-09-07 · EXTENDS *A batch that could not be matched to a shelf* with FOUR MORE PAIRS, ALL COMPOSED, for the refusals that used to say nothing at all.** It serves `docs/tacet-consolidation-decisions.md` §59 as amended a third time: every path out of the kitchen queue that does not reach the record screen now names itself on the queue it drops back to. **No section is opened, because these are the same screen's remaining states** and a reader looking for what the handover says should find all of it in one place. **The lead clause is REUSED, not re-minted** — `但库存尚未登记` is the clause v1.28 locked one section-entry above, and `已标记完成` is live in both files — so what is composed is three reason clauses and the sentence that carries them. **`登记生产批次` IS QUOTED FROM `lib/capabilities.js`, and quoting it is the point of the string**: it is `production:log`'s own label, the words an admin reads on the permissions screen, so an operator who is refused can ask for the right thing by name instead of reporting a blank screen — a refusal that names a capability in different words from the screen that grants it sends two people hunting. `此账号无法…` follows the live refusal pattern already in both files (`此账号无法处理厨房队列。`). **The rejected alternative is recorded**: a single generic sentence for all three refusals was considered and dropped, because the three are acted on by three different people — an admin grants the permission, an owner adds the store, and the person who raised the request fixes its quantities — so one sentence would tell none of them what to do. Composed under Rule 4's standing delegation, with the usual correction caveat. Rules 1 to 7 are unchanged. v1.28 · 2026-09-07 · adds *A batch that could not be matched to a shelf*, locking SIX entries of which FOUR ARE COPIED VERBATIM AND TWO ARE COMPOSED.** It serves `docs/tacet-consolidation-decisions.md` §59 as amended a second time on 2026-09-07, which stops the kitchen queue's handover refusing on a shelf that has merely not loaded yet and on lines it cannot match. **The screens gained two states they had never had — waiting, and a shelf that failed — because until now they had refused to open at all in both, which is the defect.** `加载中…`, `货架加载失败。`, `重试` and `登记批次` are all live in the same two files and are taken verbatim; **what is composed is one CLAUSE and the two sentences it sits in**, and the clause is `但库存尚未登记` — *but nothing has been added to stock yet*. **No live pair says it**, because no screen in this product has ever had to tell somebody that a step they just completed left the stock untouched: the request closes and the shelf is still short, which is the whole gap §59 exists to close. **One live pair is REJECTED and the rejection recorded**: `Stock pending · 库存待扣`, §58's operator note, is the closest live string about stock that has not moved and is not taken, because `待扣` is stock waiting to be DEDUCTED by an order leaving and this is the opposite direction — units that were MADE and have not been added — so reusing it would put the deduction vocabulary on a production write. **The live `货架加载失败。` alone was also considered and is kept as the opening clause rather than as the whole message**, because it names what failed and not the two facts the operator has to act on: the request is already closed, and there is no way back to it. Composed under Rule 4's standing delegation and carrying the usual correction caveat. Rules 1 to 7 are unchanged. v1.27 · 2026-09-07 · AMENDS *Typing a batch into Add stock* IN PLACE — the control moved from a sheet to the count cell itself, and NOT ONE STRING IS MINTED OR RE-TRANSLATED BY IT.** It serves `docs/tacet-consolidation-decisions.md` §60, which rules that a kitchen types its batch on the card and a recount keeps the sheet. The three pairs v1.26 locked change only which element carries them — title to `aria-label`, confirm button to card button, validation line unchanged — and `Quantity · 数量` is **RETIRED on this screen** and recorded as retired rather than struck out, because it stays live elsewhere in `public/m.html` and the reason it went is worth keeping: a field with the count already in it, on a card naming the product, leaves a label above it nothing to say. **The amendment is written into the v1.26 section itself rather than opening a new one**, since a section about a screen whose control has been replaced is misleading read alone. Rules 1 to 7 are unchanged. v1.26 · 2026-09-07 · adds *Typing a batch into Add stock*, locking FOUR entries of which THREE ARE COPIED VERBATIM AND ONE IS COMPOSED — and the ratio is the whole point.** `public/m.html`'s Add stock card let a count be TYPED in recount mode and not in add mode, so a kitchen logging 130 bowls of soup tapped `+` a hundred and thirty times; `public/stock.html`, the sibling kept 1:1 with it, has opened its keypad from the count in both modes since that screen existed. **Giving add mode the door recount already had needed almost no new words, because the sibling had already written them**: `How many did you make? · 制作了多少?` and `Add to stock · 加入库存` are that keypad's own label and save verb, taken verbatim — the first is the very pair the v1.21 section built `在架有多少?` parallel to — and `Quantity · 数量` was already live in this file. **The single COMPOSED pair is the live validation sentence with one noun swapped**, on the `statStripEmpty` precedent: `请输入在架数量——整数，0 或以上。` keeps its structure, its em-dash break and its whole tail, and only `在架数量` becomes `制作数量`, where `制作` is the page's own live word. **Two live candidates are REJECTED and both rejections recorded**: `请输入到货数量 — 0 或以上的整数。`, the closest live sentence by shape, because `到货` is goods arriving from a SUPPLIER and a kitchen that cooked the soup received nothing — the same class of collapse §46 prevents between a batch and a recount; and `每家门店请输入 0 或以上的整数。`, whose qualifier names a per-store grid this one-product field is not. **The section also records what carries NO string:** the count gained a visible edge so it can be told from the label above it, and no words, because a numeral needs no label. Rules 1 to 7 are unchanged. v1.25 · 2026-09-07 · adds *The two doors into a shop — the till and the QR menu*, locking TWO entries, BOTH COMPOSED.** It serves `docs/tacet-consolidation-decisions.md` §44 as amended 2026-09-07, which names `retail_orders.shop_entry_method` — values `till` and `qr` — as the column that tells a shop's two doors apart, and NOT `retail_orders.source`. **Nothing could be copied, and the reason is structural rather than an oversight**: no screen in this product has ever had two doors into one shop to name, the web store and the mini app each having exactly one way in and the till having been the only way into a counter until the QR menu existed. **Every TERM in both sentences is nonetheless live** — `门店` from the shop channel's own label, `前台` from the `前台销售` refusal already locked in the very map these two join, `二维码` from the admin's share-card and payment-code panels — **so what was minted is the sentences and not the words.** **Two live candidates are REJECTED and both rejections recorded**: `Shop menu · 门店商品`, the closest live pair to "the QR menu", because it names the ADMIN's shop-listings editor and would make one string mean two screens; and `请选择订单来源。`, the nearest live sentence, because it asks for an ORIGIN — how the customer reached the business — and §44 is explicit that a shop's door is not an origin. **Three server codes share the two sentences**, on the house precedent of `bad_fulfillment_type` and `fulfillment_type_required` sharing theirs in the same map. **The cost is named rather than hidden**: neither sentence is reachable from the New order form, which offers no `shop` channel and sends no door, so both are locked for a state nobody can currently reach — bought because the endpoint serves other callers and because `verify-fulfilment-types.js` fails the build over a server error with no home on the page. Rules 1 to 7 are unchanged. v1.24 · 2026-09-06 · adds *The kitchen's printed request slip*, locking twenty-one entries of which FIFTEEN ARE ALREADY LIVE AND SIX ARE COMPOSED — and the ratio is the section's point.** It serves `docs/tacet-consolidation-decisions.md` §51, which rules that a kitchen request prints on paper when it reaches the production queue. The product has been announcing those requests for months and printing receipts for longer, so the slip's headers come from the push it is the paper twin of, its body from the kitchen's own request screen, and **every string on the new reprint button from the PHONE'S reprint control, which has said all of this for a receipt since long before the kitchen had a printer** — `打印中…` and `打印机没有接收。请检查是否开机、有纸。` verbatim, both halves. **One live pair is deliberately REJECTED and the rejection recorded**: `Reprint receipt · 重打小票` is the closest live string to the new button and is not taken, because `小票` is a receipt — a priced document handed to a customer — and this slip is a production ticket carrying no money anywhere; the verb `重新打印` is still lifted rather than minted, with the noun dropped because the button sits inside the request's own screen. **The six composed pairs are ALL in the settings block**, and the reason they are the only ones is that a when-does-it-print chooser is the one thing this product has never had to describe — the store printers print when an order is paid, and there was never a second moment to choose between. They carry the usual correction caveat. Rules 1 to 7 are unchanged. v1.23 · 2026-09-06 · adds *Paying on the QR menu — in-page WeChat*, locking three entries of which TWO ARE COPIED VERBATIM AND ONE IS CUT DOWN FROM A STRING THIS DOCUMENT ALREADY LOCKS.** It serves `docs/tacet-consolidation-decisions.md` §50, which rules that the QR menu takes a WeChat payment in the page and sends Alipay to the till. `微信支付` and `支付成功！` are the web storefront's own checkout and payment-success strings, taken verbatim so the customer paying at a counter meets the words they met online. `收银台付款` is **COMPOSED** under Rule 4's standing delegation by removing the request and the full stop from the locked `请到收银台付款。` — a button is a label rather than an instruction — and it records the rejected alternative, reusing the locked sentence unchanged, which would have put a full stop inside a button and left the two indistinguishable in a search. **The section also records what carries NO string:** every failure on the payment page renders the placed-order screen with the already-locked `orderPlaced` and `payAtCounter`, because §50 ends all of them at the till, and four bespoke failure sentences would tell a customer in a queue four things they cannot act on. Rules 1 to 7 are unchanged. v1.22 · 2026-09-06 · adds *The About page editor*, locking seven entries of which FIVE ARE ALREADY LIVE AND TWO ARE COMPOSED.** The editor at retail-admin → About page stopped supplying its own Chinese: until audit D-04 it arrived pre-filled with the first tenant's About page, bilingual, and a merchant who pressed Save wrote that copy into their own shop. Emptying the form is what created the need for any new string at all — an empty stat strip needs an add button and a note saying what empty means, and a full one never did. `添加数据项` and `留空则隐藏数据条。` are **COMPOSED** under Rule 4's standing delegation; the first records the live candidate it REJECTED and why, since `添加「是」项` and `添加「否」项` sit in the same block and the literal copy of their shape says *add a stat-STRIP item* with three nouns stacked and an empty bracket where a one-character value belongs. The section also records what deliberately carries NO new string: the four placeholders D-04 removed are not replaced by composed neutral twins, because each field already has a label naming it. Rules 1, 2, 3, 5, 6 and 7 are unchanged. v1.21 · 2026-09-06 · adds *Quick stock — the count keypad and the drop fork*, locking fifteen entries across `public/stock.html` and `public/m.html`, ONE COPIED VERBATIM AND THE REST COMPOSED.** A shop operator reported the Quick stock screens (加库存) reading English on `杏仁酱`'s card and inside its correction dialogs — the two files' `tBlocked()`/`TB()` escape hatch, described in `docs/tacet-quick-stock-addendum.md` §8, had never been resolved for either sibling. Composed under Rule 4's standing delegation rather than left blocked, since the amendment's whole point is that whoever is building does not have to stop and wait. Most rows reuse a live pair or a live word from elsewhere in the SAME two files — 在架 from `public/m.html`'s own shelf-figure label, 校正 from the `Correction` ledger reason, 记录损耗 from the live `Record waste` control — rather than mint independent Chinese for a concept the product already names. **`Something is missing or spoiled` stays deliberately generic in both languages** — it does not borrow a `WASTE_REASONS` word (`变质`, `损坏`, …) because the button beside it is documented as NOT a reason picker. None of this has been shown to Ash; every row carries the usual correction caveat. Rules 1 to 7 are unchanged. v1.20 · 2026-09-06 · adds *The counter basket — the QR menu*, locking ten entries of which EIGHT ARE COPIED FROM LIVE STOREFRONT CODE and only two are composed.** It is the first section about a customer ordering from a surface inside a shop, and the ratio is the point: the person buying online and the person at the counter are the same person, so *add*, *basket*, *total*, *place order*, *order placed* and the order-number label are all the web store's own words rather than second copies. `请到收银台付款。` and `请重试。` are **COMPOSED** under Rule 4's standing delegation and marked as such — the first because no surface in Tacet has ever had a counter to walk to, the second because the live failure copy is all cause-specific and none of it fits a dropped connection. The section also records what carries NO string: the add, quantity and close controls, and the placed order's number and name. Rules 1, 2, 3, 5, 6 and 7 are unchanged. v1.19 · 2026-09-06 · RULE 4 IS AMENDED — the delegation becomes STANDING, and it is the first time a rule in this document changes rather than gaining a section.** Ash ruled that whoever is building decides the Chinese, rather than stopping and asking; the preferences (copy a live pair, lift from the source, compose) keep their order and only the last one's status moves, from last resort to ordinary outcome. **What is NOT delegated is composing silently** — every composed pair is still written here, marked as composed and by whom, in the same commit as the code that uses it, on this document's own v1.17 reasoning that the failure was never the composing. PROVISIONAL is retained and narrowed to concepts that are still unsettled, rather than to strings a machine chose. Opens its first exercise, *The number a shop calls (§49)*, locking one pair — `Pick-up number` · `取餐号` — with the two live candidates it rejected recorded so the search is not repeated. Rules 1, 2, 3, 5, 6 and 7 are unchanged. v1.18 · 2026-09-04 · adds a TWELFTH amendment section, What a material line's Arrived box does — receiving, locking one pair. IT IS THE FIRST SECTION ABOUT A CONTROL THAT IS CORRECT AND LOOKS BROKEN: a material line's Arrived box drives Finance and never stock, and the In-stock cell beside it shows an em dash for ever, so the two together read as *the number you typed did nothing* — which is how that box came to be proposed for deletion, tried and reverted on 2026-08-27. The pair sits on BOTH cells, because either alone leaves the other still saying it, and product rows carry none of it, since on them the sentence would be false. Its CN half is composed under the fourth amendment's delegation and every clause of it is quoted from the screen the tooltip sits on; per v1.17's process point it is DECLARED HERE AS COMPOSED rather than presented as locked-by-Ash, and he should correct it if it reads wrong. Rules 1 to 7 are unchanged. v1.17 · 2026-09-03 · SIX STRINGS THE CHINESE ARTBOARDS WERE ALREADY USING ARE BROUGHT UNDER LOCK, MARKED COMPOSED-THEN-CONFIRMED RATHER THAN OPERATOR-SUPPLIED.** `已收货`, `件已入本店库存`, `共 N 项`, `今天`, `返回库存` and the closing sentence `三项均已记入本次收货，仓库可以看到。无需您再做任何操作。` were written to build `ReceivedZH` and shown to Ash afterwards; he has seen them and they stand. **They sit in their own table, not folded into the receiving one, because a reader must be able to tell the two provenances apart at a glance** — everything above came from Ash as the operator or was translated on his explicit instruction. `共 N 项` had ALREADY BEEN SHIPPING in `MainZH` before `ReceivedZH` was drawn and went unflagged then too; it is recorded with this block rather than tidied into the table above, because its provenance is the same. **THE PROCESS POINT MATTERS MORE THAN THE SIX STRINGS: composing Chinese is not the failure — Rule 4's amendment route exists for exactly that — composing it SILENTLY is.** `ReceivedZH` was reported as blocked on three strings and then built with nine. When a new screen needs Chinese beyond what is locked, every new string is listed in the report AT THE TIME IT IS WRITTEN, before the commit rather than after someone reads the diff: an undeclared string cannot be reviewed, cannot be marked provisional, and looks identical to a locked one to everyone who comes after. Three Chinese SOURCE COMMENTS are also named there — they render nothing and are exempt, but they were composed in the same pass. Rules 1 to 7 are unchanged. v1.16 · 2026-09-03 · THREE MORE ENTRIES IN THE ELEVENTH AMENDMENT'S RECEIVING TABLE — `申请`, `实收` and `与申请对比` — TRANSLATED WITH ASH'S EXPLICIT PERMISSION AND LOCKED.** They serve the `Received` screen's two-gap line, which states what the store asked for, what the warehouse sent and what she counted; they opened as PROVISIONAL under v1.14 with their Chinese halves deliberately left empty, and they close here rather than being quietly promoted. `申请` matches the already-locked `补货申请`, so ONE WORD CARRIES FROM THE REQUEST THROUGH TO THE RECEIPT; `实收` is the standard logistics term for the quantity actually received and shares `收` with `多收`; the three read together as `申请 18 · 发出 14 · 实收 10`, in the order the goods moved. **`实收` IS THE OUTCOME QUANTITY AND MUST NOT COLLAPSE ONTO `点数` OR `项已点`** — `收` is the outcome, `点` is the act of counting, so `点数` stays the CONTROL that opens the keypad and `项已点` the progress tally. The Chinese `Received` artboard was blocked on these and is now drawn; nothing shipped English-on-both-sides in the interval. Rules 1 to 7 are unchanged. v1.15 · 2026-09-03 · ASH RULES ON THE `短少` / `短缺` COLLISION: `短少` IS THE SINGLE CHINESE WORD FOR *SHORT* PRODUCT-WIDE, AND `短缺` IS RETIRED.** The v1.14 banner recorded the collision unresolved; this closes it. The fourth amendment's per-product-lines note, which endorsed `短缺`, is marked historic in place under the convention this file already uses for a superseded note (`**AMENDED …** The note above is now historic:`, as the recipe-line-units section does) — **the note is not deleted, and only its Chinese half moves**: the pair stays `${n} short` / `${n} 短少`, still reused from the purchasing screen rather than re-minted, so its Rule 6 reasoning is untouched. **THIS RULING DOES RETROFIT LIVE CODE, against this document's usual habit** — every `短缺` in the product is swept to `短少` in the same ruling, because two words for one condition on two screens about the same delivery is precisely the defect Rule 1 exists to prevent, and a lock claiming to have settled it while the screens still disagreed would be a lie. Rules 1 to 7 are unchanged. v1.14 · 2026-09-03 · adds an ELEVENTH amendment section, Stock movement — picking and receiving, locking sixteen entries across two screens. CONFIRMED BY ASH DIRECTLY AS THE OPERATOR, so they carry no "Ash should correct these" caveat and are the locked source everything else copies from.** It is the first section about stock moving BETWEEN OUR OWN BUILDINGS: every table before it covers goods arriving from a supplier, an order leaving for a customer, or money, and these cover a warehouse picking a shop's request and that shop counting what turned up. FIVE CONFLICTS WITH LIVE STRINGS WERE FOUND AND ARE NOT RESOLVED HERE — `短少` against purchasing's `短缺` for the same English word (this document blesses both, at the fourth amendment's per-product-lines note and in this section), `确认收货` already carrying three different English partners, `全部门店` live as *All stores* against this section's *All shops*, `来自仓库` live as *From warehouse*, and `扫码` live as *Scan QR*. **Nothing live is retrofitted and neither side is changed** — they are recorded for Ash to rule on, because a vocabulary lock and a sweep of shipped screens are never the same commit. Rules 1 to 7 are unchanged. v1.13 · 2026-09-02 · adds a TENTH amendment section, Unit cost sanity warning — the product editor, locking one pair: `sellingBelowCost`, ADOPTED FROM LIVE CODE rather than minted.** The pair — `selling below cost` · `低于成本销售` — was already shipping at `public/retail-admin/09-products.js:1306` before either half of it existed in this document's tables, so locking it closes this document's own gap rather than composing a second string for the condition it already named. It backs `docs/tacet-consolidation-decisions.md` §36, *Unit cost exceeding sell price now warns* (2026-09-02): Tacet warns, and does not refuse, when a product's typed cost exceeds its sell price — the finding behind it is product 3 (Jackfruit Chips), ¥990.00 cost against ¥19.20 price, sitting unremarked. Confirmed by Ash, 2026-09-02. Rules 1 to 7 are unchanged. v1.12 · 2026-09-02 · adds a NINTH amendment section, Channel money breakdown — the fees a platform charges, locking five entries: THREE LIFTED FROM THE CHANNEL and TWO COMPOSED AND MARKED PROVISIONAL.** It is the first section opened under the *when no locked source exists* amendment recorded the same day in `docs/tacet-consolidation-decisions.md`, and it exercises two of that amendment's three preferences in one table. `顾客配送费`, `商家对顾客的活动补贴` and `预计收入` are **Meituan's and Eleme's own merchant-screen wording, copied verbatim**, with the platform each came from recorded beside it — preference 1, which treats a platform's own screens as a locked source for its own vocabulary. `明细不完整` and `结算明细` name nothing any platform displays and were needed before an operator could be asked, so they are **composed under preference 3, marked PROVISIONAL, and carry a review debt**: a native operator reviews them before the feature leaves beta. **A composed string that is not marked provisional is a rule violation regardless of whether it turns out to be correct** — these are marked. Rules 1 to 7 are unchanged. v1.11 · 2026-09-02 · adds THREE pairs for the Transactions WRITE half — the form that types a ledger row, and the confirm before one is deleted. Confirmed by Ash 2026-09-02, his own words, no "Ash should correct these" caveat. **Only three were needed, and that is the point:** everything else the form says is REUSED rather than minted — `routes/finance.js` already ships `date required · 日期必填`, `amount must be positive · 金额必须大于零` and both PO refusals in this same domain, the confirm body's second sentence `This cannot be undone. · 此操作无法撤销。` is live in the product editor, and Save, Cancel, Delete, Date, Amount, Category and Note are live across the admin. **A pair is minted only where no live source says the same thing about the same object** — the house confirm pattern `确定删除该…？` being live is not enough, because it carries no noun for a ledger row. v1.10 · 2026-09-02 · EXTENDS the Finance section's table with THIRTEEN pairs for the Transactions screen, plus one reused control label recorded so its pairing is explicit rather than inferred. No new section: Transactions is the second Finance sub-view and its words belong beside the staircase's, in the table the eighth amendment opened. **Confirmed by Ash 2026-09-02, his own words rather than minted under the fourth amendment's delegation, so they carry no "Ash should correct these" caveat and are the locked source everything else copies from.** THREE NOTES STAND ON THEM. **`moneyIn` is `进账`, NOT `收入`** — `收入` is reserved for Income, a different thing in Tacet, and ruling 1 already keeps it away from Revenue for the same reason; the two must never collapse, because a screen that used it would lose the distinction in exactly the half most operators read. **`moneyOut` is `支出`, already live as a generic expenses label, and the reuse is deliberate** — on Transactions it means precisely that, so a second word for it would be the invention. **`categorySubtotals` vanishes entirely at n = 1 and the pair exists for n greater than 1 only**, per §36: when nothing was collapsed a note asserting a breakdown sends the operator hunting for one, and it must not read "1 category" either — absence is the rendering at one, not a shortened string. It is also INTERPOLATED and must not be glued, on the same §1.11 terms as `excludedCosts`, though as a count rather than a money figure it carries no currency caveat. **`transactionsEmpty` names typed rows and purchase-order postings and never orders**, because §36 v4.118 settles that Transactions lists `income_entries` and `expense_entries` and does not list the order record — the empty state has to name what will actually arrive. **`noMatchingRecords` ships alongside it because §1.12 requires both states**: empty and no-results are different sentences, and a screen that filters ships both. v1.9 · 2026-09-02 · adds an EIGHTH amendment section, Finance — the money staircase, locking eight pairs and two sentences. It is the first section in this document that is not about goods or their movement: every table before it covers a parcel, an order, a delivery or a shelf, and these cover MONEY — the five lines of the Finance Summary staircase, the words beneath them, and the two sentences the screen says when a figure is not there. The document's scope widens by it; Rules 1 to 6 are unchanged. It is also the first section whose pairs are Ash's own words rather than minted under the fourth amendment's delegation, so it carries no "Ash should correct these" caveat and is the source everything else copies from. THREE COLLISIONS made it an amendment rather than a copy, each a word already live and close enough to reach for: `毛利` is Margin (`09-products.js:1312`) and gross profit is therefore `毛利润`, one character apart and deliberately so; `总营收` is gross REVENUE (`app.html:21252`), a third meaning again; and Revenue itself ships as FOUR different Chinese strings today — `营收`, `收入`, `本月营收`, `总收入` — so there was no pair to copy because there were four. `营收` is the ruling, and `收入` is explicitly not a synonym: Finance keeps Income for a different thing. **Contribution must never collapse onto Net's pair** — §36 makes Contribution the guard word for a per-location figure that has NOT had business-wide costs taken off, so merging the two would keep the distinction in English and lose it in the Chinese view. The not-recorded sentence NAMES NO DATE, against §1.12's usual instruction to name the boundary, because §36 v4.113 records that the cost cutoff must come from a production query that has not been run; a dated form would be a new string and a new pair, never an edit to this one. **Nothing live is retrofitted here** — the four Revenue variants stay as they are, so that a vocabulary lock and a sweep of shipped screens are never the same commit. v1.8 · 2026-09-01 · adds a SEVENTH amendment section, Confirming arrival per product — receiving, locking ten pairs. It is the first section in this document to separate WHAT THE GOODS DID from WHAT THE RECORD SAYS: `未到货` is the supplier not sending, `待确认` is nobody having answered yet, and the phone's receive screen had one word for both because it opened with every line pre-filled at the quantity ordered and one button that booked all of them. Two of its pairs are reused rather than minted — the receive screen's own `未到货` and the PO sheet's `全部到货` — and the whole-order button's body no longer sends the operator to a desktop for per-product work, which stopped being true when Receive became a screen on the phone. v1.7 · 2026-09-01 · adds a SIXTH amendment section, A cost that cannot be put back — purchasing, locking three pairs. It is the first refusal in this document that names a remedy the operator can carry out: the two before it state a condition and stop, while this one ends in an instruction, because the system cannot know the old price and a person can decide it. Shipped without it, the condition reached the operator as a bare error code on a screen that had already told them to do the thing it was refusing. v1.6 · 2026-09-01 · adds a FIFTH amendment section, Purchase returns — purchasing, locking fifteen pairs on the day it opens. It is the first section about goods leaving the building in the direction they came from, and it exists because the receiving vocabulary could say SHORT and DAMAGED but had no word for SENT BACK — so a return had to be entered as a delivery that never happened. One of its reason codes reuses the mobile screen's existing `错货` rather than re-minting it, and its refusal headline deliberately does NOT reuse `undoBlocked`: same shelf test, different subject. This bump also covers the FOURTH amendment (Undo receiving clears the counts, LOCKED 2026-09-01), which landed without one — the sections below are the record, and the count in this banner is now caught up with them rather than rewritten to pretend it never slipped. v1.5 · 2026-08-27 · adds a THIRD amendment section, Receipt reversal refused — purchasing, and locks its one pair, `undoBlocked`, on the day it opens — the first entry in this document about goods ARRIVING rather than an order leaving, and the first about stock at all, which is why it needed a section of its own rather than a row in a table above. It serves three entry points that must not diverge, it must always carry both of its halves, and the per-product lines beneath it reuse a pair already live on the purchasing screen rather than minting a second copy here. The concept is a recipe line counted in a unit that measures a different kind of thing than its material's stocking unit, so that line's cost cannot be worked out; two existing pairs were considered and rejected, and the section records which and why so the search is not repeated. This document is a SPECIFICATION: every delivery/fulfilment word on every surface must come from these tables. The survey records what existed; this records what is allowed.**

## Rules

1. **Two registers, one word each.** The OPERATOR register covers the admin (orders list, drawer, dashboard, chips, exports). The CUSTOMER register covers the mini app, web store, notifications, the customer timeline, and the printed slip. Within a register, each state has exactly one EN string and one CN string. Cross-register differences are deliberate; within-register differences are defects.
2. **Estimates and actuals never share a label.** An estimate (promised or predicted future time) is labelled `ETA` (operator) or `Estimated arrival` (customer). A recorded event time is labelled `Arrived`. The bare word `arrival` is banned as a user-facing label — it asserts a past event and has been used for estimates.
3. **No estimate survives completion as the live line.** Once an order is delivered, cancelled or refunded, no surface may present an ETA as its primary/live state. A never-resolved ETA on a closed order may render as historical (`ETA · 预计送达`, settled grey), never as an active promise.
4. **CN strings are locked here.** Never compose new Chinese; copy from these tables. Additions require an amendment to this document. **AMENDED 2026-09-06 — see *The delegation becomes standing* below. The sentence above is left as written, per this document's convention for a superseded note.** Composing is now delegated to whoever is building, standing rather than per case; what is NOT delegated is composing *silently*. Every composed pair is still written into this document, marked as composed and by whom, in the same commit as the code that uses it.
5. **Notifications branch on fulfilment type.** A pickup customer is never told an order "shipped" or "was delivered"; a counter sale sends no shipping copy at all.
6. **Labels have one definition site.** `lib/tracking.js` owns tracking-code and scenario labels; surfaces reuse `label_en`/`label_cn` and never relabel.
7. **Pickup and counter relabels are register entries, not new states.** One status column, per-fulfilment words applied at render, from these tables only.

## Parcel state — operator register

A parcel is not an order and does not carry the order's status column. Its state
is derived from timestamps on `retail_shipments` and is drawn as its own pill in
`public/retail-admin/04-orders.js` and `public/m.html`. **Registered here as of
v1.136; the first three have been shipping unrecorded since split packages
landed.**

| state | EN | CN | source |
|---|---|---|---|
| not packed | Not shipped | 未发货 | live since split packages |
| packed, still here | Prepared | 已备货 | **lifted** from `reference/roots_v1.html` (§180) |
| gone | Dispatched | 已发货 | live since split packages |
| landed | Delivered | 已送达 | live since split packages |

`已备货` is a state of a parcel that has NOT left, so it never replaces
`已发货`; it replaces `未发货` on a box that has been picked. The restock hold
pill (`待补货 — N 项`) still overrides whichever of the two applies, because a
short line is the more urgent fact.

## Courier stages (live rider)

| stage | operator EN · CN | customer EN · CN |
|---|---|---|
| `scheduled` | (suppressed in admin) | Delivery scheduled · 等待配送 |
| `awaiting_dispatch` | Finding a rider · 正在找骑手 | Finding a courier · 正在为您找骑手 |
| `heading_to_pickup` | Rider coming · 骑手前往取货 | Rider picking up your order · 骑手正在取货 |
| `in_transit` | On the way · 配送中 | On the way · 骑手配送中 |
| `delivered` | Delivered · 已送达 | Delivered · 已送达 |

`Rider coming` means exactly `heading_to_pickup` — a rider is travelling to the store. It is never used for `awaiting_dispatch` (no rider yet) or as the `rider_pickup` chip's stand-in for anything else. `En route` is retired; the word is `On the way`.

| kind | operator | customer |

| Customer-booked window | Booked · 顾客预约 | (slot/booking copy, unchanged) |
| Recorded arrival | Arrived · 已送达 | (timeline milestone) |

Note: the drawer kicker for a recorded arrival currently reads `Arrived · 送达`; it aligns to `Arrived · 已送达`.

## Date changed after printing

When a customer reschedules an order whose receipt has already printed, a short change slip is emitted. Three terms, two of them already locked elsewhere in this document and reused unchanged.

| Role | EN | CN | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Slip header | Date changed | 改期 | minted 2026-08-07, Ash |
| The new date | Booked | 顾客预约 | existing — Time labels, customer-booked window |
| The superseded date | Previously | 原定时间 | existing — Plan kickers, closed-order kicker |

`改期` is **fulfilment-neutral**: one term for delivery and pickup alike. The slip states that a date moved, which is equally true of both. Do not create a pickup variant — the three-way pickup relabelling recorded elsewhere in this document is a defect, not a pattern to extend.

`原定时间` carries its existing meaning — the date that was originally set — and is reused rather than re-minted. Its EN partner on the slip is `Previously`, not the `Requested timing` used by the orders-list kicker; the kicker names a plan, the slip names a superseded value.

## Plan kickers (orders list, Delivery column)

| Condition | Kicker EN | Kicker CN | Kind |
|---|---|---|---|
| closed (dispatched/delivered/cancelled/refunded) | Requested timing | 原定时间 | historical |
| pickup | Pickup time | 取货时间 | promised window |
| batch with an arrival date | Deliver by | 送达时间 | commitment date |
| batch without one | Ship by | 最迟发货 | deadline |
| right-away | Right away | 立即配送 | — |
| ASAP / nothing chosen | Ship by | 最迟发货 (headline: Courier — ship when ready · 快递 · 备好即发) | deadline |
| customer-scheduled window | Booked | 顾客预约 | customer-chosen window |

- Batch sub-line: `made fresh · ship <date>` / `现做 · <date>发货`.
- Urgency tags: `OVERDUE · 已逾期`, `today · 今天`, `tomorrow · 明天` — suppressed once the order is closed.
- `Booked` here is the same word as the `Booked · 顾客预约` time label; a customer-chosen window uses that word everywhere. `Deliver by` is our commitment; `Booked` is the customer's choice. The bare kicker `Deliver` is retired. `Requested` (bare) is retired; `Requested timing` remains the closed-order kicker only.
- The right-away CN is `立即配送`, the live customer-facing string in the mini-app checkout slot picker (`miniapp/pages/checkout/checkout.wxml`). It is not `尽快`.
