# AMENDMENT — Operations in eight groups, and the three pills

**§121, decided by Ash 2026-09-13.** The last open part of option A. Eight headings replace
the single `Operations · 运营`, and three pills carry what a row label cannot.

**Not one string here is composed.** The headings name jobs this app has always done, so the
words for them were already live; the pills are row labels or their stems. Where a job needed
two words, the heading is two live labels joined by `与` rather than a new phrase.

## The eight headings

| English | Chinese | Lifted from |
|---|---|---|
| Take an order | `接单` | `New orders can arrive at this store again. · 这家门店可以重新接单。` |
| Money and numbers | `营收与数据` | `营收` off `Revenue (all channels) · 营收（全渠道）`; `数据` is live in 43 places (`实时数据`, `数据未变动`) |
| Send it out | `拣货与配送` | Its own two rows: `Pick · 拣货` and `Delivery settings · 配送设置`. The heading names its contents |
| Stock coming in | `入库` | `in · 入库`, and `到货时清点入库` on the transfer screen |
| Making things | `生产` | `Record what was made · 登记生产批次` |
| Ask someone else | `发起申请` | `发起` off `This account cannot raise purchase requests. · 此账号无法发起采购申请。`; `申请` off the three request rows |
| Record and review | `登记与审批` | `登记` (36 uses, `登记批次`, `登记损耗`) and `审批` off the `Approvals · 审批` row |
| Messages and channels | `消息与渠道` | Its own two rows: `Inbox · 消息` and `Channel sync · 渠道同步` |

**`出货` was considered for *Send it out* and rejected: it appears in no live UI pair** in this
product — only in a comment — so it would have been a new word for a screen that already has
two. `拣货与配送` says the same thing in vocabulary an operator has already read.

## The three pills

| English | Chinese | Lifted from |
|---|---|---|
| Ask | `申请` | The stem of all three rows it sits on: `申请补货`, `采购申请`, and `费用申请` one group down |
| Approve | `审批` | The `Approvals · 审批` row itself — the pill is the row's own word, on the row |
| Settings | `设置` | The `Settings · 设置` row at the foot of More |

**There is no fourth value, and that is the design.** A row the operator simply does wears
nothing. `Ready from the kitchen` is the row that most invites one — it is the answer to an
ask — and it deliberately has none: a value meaning *you get told* would spend the scarcity
that makes the other three legible.

## Rejected

- **`出货`** for *Send it out*. See above: not live as a UI string.
- **`报表`** for *Money and numbers*. Zero uses in this product. `数据` is live 43 times.
- **A pill reading `只读` (read-only) on Sales and Trend.** A fourth value, on rows nobody
  confuses, to say something the screens already say by having no save button.
- **`运营` kept as a ninth heading above the eight.** A heading over headings, restating the
  section the operator already navigated to.
- **Composing a single phrase for a two-word job** (e.g. one new word for *Record and review*
  instead of `登记与审批`). Joining two live labels with `与` keeps every character traceable
  to a screen, which is what Rule 4 is for.

Ash should correct any of this if it reads wrong to a Chinese operator; nothing depends on the
exact phrasing.

## The switch on the row it changes (2026-09-13)

§122. Ash, against the shipped §116 record: *"this part need to redesign. not
clear."* The foot of the customer record was five centred sentences; four of
them moved onto the rows whose values they change, and the labels went from
clauses to verbs. **Almost every verb here is a trim of a string already live**
— the long form survives on the confirm sheet each one opens, so nothing is
orphaned and the short label and its confirm cannot disagree.

### Lifted verbatim — recorded, not composed

| Chinese | English | Where it comes from |
|---|---|---|
| `重发` | Reissue | `08-customers.js`'s own Reissue button, which is already `Reissue · 重发` |

### Trims of live strings — recorded, not composed

Each is the verb out of the sentence the control's own confirm still says in
full. A label that is a clause is the fault being fixed; the clause is not lost,
it is one tap away.

| Short (the row) | The live sentence it comes from (the confirm) |
|---|---|
| `停止` | `停止推荐资格` · `停止复购分成` |
| `允许` | `允许获得复购分成` |
| `退出` | `退出所有设备` · `退出登录` |
| `选定于` | `上次选定于`, `renderLifetimeEligibility`'s own — the row states the date rather than qualifying it, so `上次` goes |

### Composed — declared, by Claude Opus 5, under Rule 4's 2026-09-06 delegation

| Chinese | English | Why it is here |
|---|---|---|
| `有效` | Active | The state of a referral eligibility that has NOT been stopped. §116 never rendered this — the row appeared only once referring had been stopped — so there was no word for the ordinary case. `有效` is the live `长期有效`'s own term, and it is the right register: the eligibility is in force, which is a fact about the permission rather than about the person. Rejected: `正常` (says nothing is wrong, which is not the same as the code working), and `可推荐` (describes the customer, where the row's subject is the permission) |

### Rejected

**Keeping the long labels on the rows — rejected, and it is the whole point.**
`停止推荐资格` beside a value reading `有效` is the row saying its subject twice.
The label names the thing, the value gives its state, the verb acts on it: three
jobs, three words, one line.

**A toggle instead of a verb — rejected.** A switch would be smaller, and it
would also throw away the confirm. Every one of these four costs a customer
something — their code, their rewards, their session — and §116's confirms state
what survives each. A verb opens that; a toggle would fire it.


## The late-parcel panel reads its own hierarchy (2026-09-14)

§98's checklist, redrawn on both surfaces. Ash, from the phone, against the
shipped panel: *"can make a better design?"*

The fault was weight, not words. The panel was one brick block — brick tint,
brick ink, all four steps open at full strength **including the ones already
finished** — so a phone call already made shouted as loudly as the claim nobody
had filed, and brick meant nothing because everything was brick. Three rules
replace that: the alarm colour lives in the header strip and the body is an
ordinary card; a done step is one quiet line; exactly one primary button exists
at a time. Nothing in `lib/delay-case.js` moved.

**Almost every string here was already on this screen.** The panel needed a
kicker, a footer label and two shorter step labels, and the product had all four
— which is Rule 4's preference order working as intended rather than a
coincidence.

### Lifted verbatim — recorded, not composed

| Chinese | English | Where it comes from |
|---|---|---|
| `下一步` | Next | `m.html`'s own kicker — live on the batch keypad and twice on the setup checklist. New to `04-orders.js`, not to the product |
| `待办` | to do | `m.html`'s setup checklist, `to do · 待办`, which sits in the same row as `下一步` and means the same thing there as here: what is left |
| `添加` | Add | `m.html`'s own Add button. Replaces `添加照片` only where a photo is already attached, so the label shortens as the row fills |

### Trims of live strings — recorded, not composed

| Short (the new label) | The live string it is cut from |
|---|---|
| `联系客户` | `联系客户 — 生鲜可能已不安全` — this panel's own step 1. The warning is not lost: it moves to the line directly beneath the label, where it is read rather than skimmed as part of a heading |
| `晚到 N 天` | `配送延误 — 晚到 N 天` — the desktop's own heading since §98. The phone printed the carrier's `已签收` directly above a panel headed `配送延误` and said the lateness nowhere, so this half now sits beside the status as a pill |

### Composed — declared, by Claude Opus 5, under Rule 4's 2026-09-06 delegation

| Chinese | English | Why it is here |
|---|---|---|
| `申请赔偿后` | after the claim | What the fourth step waits on while nothing has been filed. §98 drew that state as a row with three settlement buttons that could not mean anything yet; the row now says what has to happen first. Built from this panel's own `向承运商申请延误赔偿` and the `后` of its own `以上步骤全部完成后方可关闭。`, so every character on the line is already in service two rows above it. **Rejected:** `等待承运商` (that is the state AFTER filing, and it is live as `等待承运商 · N 天` on the very same step — reusing it would make two different states read identically), and `未申请` (states the claim's own state where this line is about what this step is waiting for) |

### Not a string at all

The progress figure in the header is `✓ 2/4`. A tick and two numerals read the
same in both registers, so the panel gained a figure and this document gained
nothing to lock. **Rejected: `已完成 2/4` / `2 of 4 done`** — a word that has to
be translated, maintained and kept in agreement with a count that is already
unambiguous without it.

### Rejected

**Keeping the body in brick — rejected, and it is the whole change.** A tint
that covers everything cannot mark anything. The design system's own rule is
that brick tint means error; four steps, two of them finished, all sitting in it
made the finished ones look unfinished and the outstanding ones look ordinary.

**`不申请赔偿` as a button beside `已申请延误赔偿` — rejected.** Identical weight
for the main road and the permanent opt-out, two taps apart, on a phone. It is
now an underlined text action, which is this app's own `.rowact` pattern from
§122 and says plainly that it is the exception.

**Hiding the later steps behind a disclosure — rejected.** A step nobody can see
is a step nobody does, and the carrier can ring while the remedy is still open.
The later steps keep their controls; what they lose is the shouting.


## Nothing was owed, and that is an answer (2026-09-14)

§125. Ash, against a live case — a parcel a day late, the customer rung, the
carrier claim waived — *"客人确认食品状态良好，无需补发，也无需退款"*.

The make-whole step offered `补发` and `已退款` and nothing else, so that case
had no honest exit. The three the operator actually had: raise a no-charge
replacement order nobody wanted, tick a refund that never happened, or leave the
step alone and let `待办: 补发或退款` sit under the case for ever. **Two of those
put a false fact in the record and the third leaves finished work on the alert
card**, which is how a card whose whole job is to list outstanding work stops
being read.

The shape was already solved one track up. `不申请赔偿` is the carrier track's
third answer: a quiet underlined action, not a button; a typed reason; and it
finishes the track in one tap. The same screenshot shows it doing exactly that,
with the reason `客人收到包裹确认食品状态良好` beside it. The make-whole track now
has its own.

### Composed — declared, by Claude Opus 5, under Rule 4's 2026-09-06 delegation

| Chinese | English | Why it is here |
|---|---|---|
| `无需补发退款` | Nothing needed | The make-whole step's third answer, and the label on that answer once taken. Every character is already on the row it sits on: `补发` and `退款` are this panel's own two buttons, and `无需` is live in more than twenty files (`无需处理`, `无需补货`, `无需更改`). It introduces no concept — it names the two things and says neither. **Rejected:** `无需处理` (live, so admissible, and it is the product's own words for *nothing to do here* — but it is an EMPTY-STATE label, what a screen says when it has no rows, and using it as the record of a decision an operator took and typed a reason for would make a deliberate act read as an absence); `无需赔付` (introduces `赔付` to a panel whose settled vocabulary is `补发`/`退款`/`赔偿`, which is the objection §98 already recorded against `补偿客户`); `不补发不退款` (a correct sentence, and one word longer than the row it labels, for a shade of meaning the `无需` version does not lose) |

### Lifted verbatim — recorded, not composed

| Chinese | English | Where it comes from |
|---|---|---|
| `原因` | Reason | The phone's own field label on `不申请赔偿`. The desktop's claim step needs `不申请的原因` because it draws three fields at once and they have to be told apart; this step draws one, under a heading that already says what is being decided, so the bare word is enough |
| `必填` | Required | the same sheet's placeholder, unchanged |
| `请先填写原因。` | Give a reason first. | `01-core.js`'s `reason_required`, which this step's server-side refusal now returns as well — **reused, not re-minted**, exactly as §98 recorded for the claim track |

### Rejected

**A third button beside `补发` and `已退款` — rejected.** §98's panel allows one
primary and quiet alternatives beside it; an opt-out that permanently ends a
track is neither. It is drawn as `不申请赔偿` is, and for the identical reason:
weight should say which acts are the main road.

**Letting `无需补发退款` go in without a reason — rejected.** The other two
answers evidence themselves: there is a replacement order or a refund behind
each. This one leaves nothing at all, and *nothing was owed* read back in six
months with no sentence beside it cannot be told from a mis-tap. The reason is
required by `lib/delay-case.js`, not by the screen, so both surfaces and any
future one inherit it.

**A new blocker word for the footer — rejected, nothing was needed.** The
footer's `待办: 补发或退款` is unchanged and now simply stops appearing, which is
the point. The progress figure is unchanged too: an answered step counts as
answered whether or not anything was given.

## The run is opened by name (2026-09-14)

§128. Ash, on the product editor's *When out of stock* control — *"right now for
there is only 'pre order outside guangzhou' i would like to add an option of pre
order for everyone"*.

The control had two answers, `全国售罄停售` and `广州外可预订（现订现发）`, and the
gap between them is a region: an out-of-city parcel leaves after the next
restock, so a pre-order is safe, while a Guangzhou same-day order is picked off
the shelf and leaves on today's run, where there is nothing to put in the box.
The third answer closes that gap by **changing what the order is** rather than by
pretending the shelf is full — it parks as `awaiting_restock`, holding nothing,
with no delivery window and no same-day fee — so the wording has to carry two
facts at once: everyone may order, and the whole order waits.

**Every fragment used below is already live.** `全国` is the sibling option's own
first two characters. `可预订` is the pill the shop grid, the PDP, the size sheet
and the favourites page all draw. `补货后发出` is lifted whole out of the
storefront's existing out-of-stock note (`当前缺货，外地可预订，下批/补货后发出。`).
`整单` appears in twenty-odd files and `一并发货` is the checkout pre-order
notice's own verb. What is composed is the assembly, and that is what is declared.

### Composed — declared, by Claude, under Rule 4's 2026-09-06 delegation

| Chinese | English | Why it is here |
|---|---|---|
| `全国可预订（补货后发出）` | Pre-order everywhere | The option itself, built to the sibling's exact shape — `[区域][可预订]（[履约方式]）`, matching `广州外可预订（现订现发）` character position for character position, so an operator reads the two as one pair rather than as two unrelated settings. The bracket had to change with the region: `现订现发` promises a parcel that leaves on the next run, which is true out of city and false for the shopper this option is being added for, so it becomes `补货后发出` — the same document's own words for the honest version. **Rejected:** `所有顾客可预订` (closest to Ash's own phrasing, and `所有顾客` is not live in the product editor — it would be the one minted phrase in a set of lifts, for no gain over `全国`, which the option beside it already uses for exactly this contrast); `全场可预订` (`全场` is a promotions word — it means *everything in the shop* — and this setting is per product, so it would say the opposite of what it does); `不限地区可预订` (accurate and three characters longer, and it names what is NOT restricted rather than who may order, which reads as a caveat instead of an offer) |
| `“全国可预订”不拦截广州当日:顾客在缺货时仍可下单预订,整单等补货后一并发货。` | *(help text)* | The second half of the control's tooltip. It is written as the **continuation of the sentence already there** — the existing half ends `仅广州当日配送会被拦截。`, so this one opens by lifting that verb back (`不拦截广州当日`) and the clause after it is the first half's own `顾客在缺货时仍可下单预订` with `广州外` dropped. The closing clause is the part an operator cannot discover any other way: the order stops being a same-day order, and in a mixed basket the in-stock items wait too. **Rejected:** ending at `…仍可下单预订。` (every character live, and it hides the cost — an operator who sets this and then hears from a customer whose whole lunch was delayed was not told, which is the §1.7 failure in reverse); `广州当日也不例外` (compact, but `例外` is not this document's word for the gate and the sentence stops before saying what happens instead) |
| `当前缺货，可预订，整单等补货后一并发货。` | *(product-page note)* | The storefront's note under the pill, and a **trim plus a tail** rather than a new sentence: `当前缺货，外地可预订，下批/补货后发出。` loses `外地` (no longer true — that is the whole point) and loses `下批/` (there is no batch behind a grocery restock), then gains the clause the shopper is owed for the same reason the operator is. The `backorder_gz` note is untouched and still ships beside it. **Rejected:** keeping the live string unchanged for both modes (it would tell a Guangzhou shopper the item is orderable *外地* — outside their own city — on a page where they can in fact order it); `当前缺货，可预订。` (a clean trim, and it drops the one fact that makes the offer honest) |

### Lifted verbatim — recorded, not composed

| Chinese | English | Where it comes from |
|---|---|---|
| `预订` / `现订现发` | Pre-order / Ships soon | `computePreorderNotice`'s kicker and title, reused for this mode with no edit. The notice's own body — `您订单中的部分商品目前缺货。此为预订订单，我们将在补货后尽快为您一并发货。` — **already described this policy exactly**, having been written for the out-of-city case that behaves the same way, so §128 widened who sees it and changed not one character of it |
| `可预订` / `售罄` | PRE-ORDER / SOLD OUT | the pills, unchanged on every surface. A row under this policy simply stops being drawn as the second one for a shopper on the local run |

## The shortfall behind a cost figure (2026-09-14)

§56 as amended. Ash, choosing between two designs after
`scripts/diagnose_cost_coverage.js` measured the live database: *"version a"* —
show the figure, say next to the number that it is not accurate, and drop the
note once the catalogue is fixed.

The Finance Summary's cost-of-goods and gross-profit rows were blank from §56
(2026-09-07) until that amendment. They now carry a figure, **on one condition:
the figure never appears without the share of the period it rests on.** On the
measured window that share was 29.1%, and a gross margin of 91.6% printed alone
off it is exactly the flattering fiction §56 refused. This sentence is the
condition, so it is not a caption on the number — it is what the number ships
under.

**The shape was already solved one level down.** `public/retail-admin/09-products.js`'s
`_paMarginWarn` says this about a single product's margin drawn from an
incomplete cost, and it was locked on 2026-09-01: *"Marked 'cost is not
complete' — this percentage is drawn from a cost that is missing something, so
the real margin is lower."* This is the same fact at period scale, and its
operative half is that string's, character for character.

**It says the DIRECTION, not just the fraction.** A percentage alone leaves a
reader to work out which way the error runs. Cost missing from a period can only
push the margin UP — a shortfall always flatters — and that is the half worth a
sentence.

### Composed — declared, by Claude Opus 5, under Rule 4's 2026-09-06 delegation

| Chinese | English | Why it is here |
|---|---|---|
| `此期间 {n}% 的营收已记录成本 —— 实际毛利更低。` | Cost recorded for {n}% of this period's revenue — the real margin is lower. | The condition under which §56 as amended lets the two rows carry a figure. **Assembled from four live pieces and introduces no word.** `此期间` is this very card's own `noCostRecorded` (`此期间未记录成本数据。`); `营收` is this card's own `revenue` label, and naming that row is deliberate — the fraction is computed against it, so the sentence points at the line it qualifies rather than at sales in general; `已记录成本` is the positive of the same card's `未记录成本数据`; and `—— 实际毛利更低。` is `09-products.js:1647` verbatim, dash included. **Rejected:** `销售额` for `营收` (live only in `public/full-circle.html`, the marketing page, and never in this admin — it would name a quantity this card does not show, where `营收` names the row directly above); a bare `此期间 {n}% 的营收已记录成本。` with no second clause (true and useless — it leaves the reader to derive which way the error runs, which is the one thing they need and the one thing §56 was amended to guarantee); reusing `noCostRecorded` itself for both states (it explains an ABSENCE and this qualifies a PRESENCE — one sentence covering both would make a period with some cost indistinguishable from one with none, which is the distinction the amendment turns on) |

### Rejected

**A dated form — rejected, and for §36 v4.113's reason rather than a new one.**
The capture cutoff is now measured (31 August 2026 — §36, *The cost capture
cutoff is 31 August 2026, measured*), so a dated sentence is no longer
impossible. It is still wrong here: the fraction is a
property of the window the operator chose, not of a fixed boundary, and a window
wholly after the cutoff can still be short. **The coverage number is the honest
statement and the date would be a second, weaker one beside it.**

**A percentage that rounds — rejected.** A window at 99.6% rounds to `100%` and
prints a sentence arguing with its own number. It floors, so the figure can
never claim more coverage than there is.

**A word for the full-coverage state — rejected, nothing is needed.** At 100%
the sentence is simply not drawn. Ash's *"once we fix manually everything you
will remove that note"* is built as a consequence of the data rather than a task
anyone has to remember: the note cannot outlive the shortfall it describes,
because the shortfall is the only thing that draws it.

**A word for the zero-coverage state — rejected, `noCostRecorded` already
holds it.** A period with no costed line at all keeps the seventh state it has
had since §56, unchanged. The amendment does not reach that case: a `¥0.00` cost
of goods would claim the goods were free, which §36 refused and §56 kept.


## The bread is chosen by name (2026-09-15)

§129. Ash, after the fault was reported to him three times while §129 was
built: *"fix"*.

Garlic Dill Sour Cream Toast asks **Choose your bread** in English and
`加点配料?` — *"Add extras?"* — in Chinese, copied from the Apple Pie Overnight
Oats question. Its two answers carried `label_cn = NULL`, so a Chinese customer
read *"whole wheat rye"* in Latin script under a question about something else
entirely. The group was also typed `multi`, which is what let a customer tick
both breads for one toast — that half is data, not vocabulary, and is repaired
by `scripts/oneoff_bread_question.js` in the same run.

**THE TWO ANSWERS NEEDED NOTHING.** This is the part worth recording, because
the first instinct was to translate them. `scripts/diagnose_bread_question_strings.js`
read the live catalogue and found both breads already named:

| Answer | Chinese | Where it is already live |
|---|---|---|
| whole wheat rye | `全麦黑麦面包` | Product #352 *Hearty Rye & Whole Wheat Bread*, and option answers #1334, #1795, #2240, #2545, #2953 |
| gluten free buckwheat | `无麸 荒麦面包` | Products #255 and #353 *Buckwheat Bread (GF, Sliced & Frozen)*, and option answers #1332, #1793, #2238, #2543, #2951 |

A customer who has bought the loaf now reads the same words choosing it on a
toast. **The spacing in `无麸 荒麦面包` is the catalogue's own and is kept.**
Closing it up would be tidier and would also be a second spelling of a name this
shop already prints — which is the drift Rule 4 exists to stop, arriving as a
tidy-up rather than as a translation.

### Composed — declared, by Claude Opus 5, under Rule 4's 2026-09-06 delegation

| Chinese | English | Why it is here |
|---|---|---|
| `选择面包` | Choose your bread | The question itself. **Two live pieces and no new character.** `选择` is the picker verb this shop already uses on both its other surfaces — `选择选项` / Choose your options and `选择规格` / Choose a size, both in `miniapp/pages/product/product.js` and `public/shop.html`, where the mini app literally builds `选择${noun}` at runtime; `面包` is live in five product names (#185, #228, #255, #352, #353) and in a dozen option answers. What is composed is only the pairing, which is why it is declared rather than treated as a lift. **Rejected:** `加点配料?` (the value being replaced — it is live, and it is live as the WRONG question; a string being real somewhere else is not a reason to leave it asking about extras under a heading about bread); `选择选项` (live, and the exact generic this picker already puts in its own heading — using it here would tell the customer they are choosing "options" when the screen can tell them they are choosing bread, and would make two different questions read identically); `选择规格` (live, but `规格` is a size or specification — wrong noun); `选择你的面包` (closer to the English possessive, rejected because both live siblings drop the possessive and Chinese does not need it — matching the shop's own pattern beats matching the English word for word); `面包选择` (noun first — reads as a label on a shelf rather than a question being asked, and breaks the `选择X` shape the other two share) |

### Rejected

**Translating the two answers — rejected, and it would have been the ordinary
mistake.** Both breads are products this shop sells and has already named. Any
new wording, however correct, would have been a second name for a thing with
one, discoverable only by a customer who owned both the loaf and the toast.
**The rule that caught it is Rule 4's first preference, and the thing that made
it followable was reading the live catalogue before writing anything.**

**Leaving the answers in English — rejected.** It is what shipped, and the
fallback that produces it (`label_cn || label_en`) is a safety net for a missing
string, not a decision. A Chinese customer choosing between two Latin-script
phrases under a Chinese question is being asked to read a language the rest of
the screen does not use.

**Fixing the English too — rejected, nothing is wrong with it.** *"whole wheat
rye"* and *"gluten free buckwheat"* are lower-case where the catalogue's product
names are title-case, and that is a house-style difference on a customer-facing
answer rather than a fault. It is the operator's to change in the product
editor if they want it, and not something a repair script should decide on their
behalf while it is in there for something else.


## The purchase order is dated when it was placed (2026-09-15)

**Ash, 2026-09-15: "我想这里可以更改为下单日期，而不是到货日期。因为物品到货的时间都不一样。"**
Said twice, the second time after a session had answered around it. Register:
**OPERATOR** (Rule 1) — this is the back office's 新建采购单 wizard, not a customer
surface.

**WHAT WAS THERE.** Step one of the wizard asked for `预计到货` / *Expected
arrival* and carried the hint `超过该日期后,订单会被标记逾期。` — we will flag the
order once this date passes. One date, for the whole order.

**WHY IT HAD TO GO, and it is Ash's reason rather than a style judgement.** A
retail purchase order here routinely carries many suppliers — RPO-0037 carries
seventeen — and their goods arrive on different days. A single expected-arrival
date is therefore not a fact about the order; it is a fact about at most one
supplier in it. Worse, `isLate()` (`public/retail-admin/16-purchasing.js`) built
the `逾期` badge on exactly that date, so an order with sixteen suppliers on time
and one date passed was labelled late in full. **Rule 2 is the rule already in
this document that says why this is a defect rather than a rough edge:** an
estimate and an actual never share a label, and `到货` is the word this product
uses for goods that HAVE arrived — the list column, and the receiving screen's
own count. Hanging it on a promise was the drift.

**WHAT REPLACES IT.** The order date — `purchase_orders.date` — which is the one
date that IS a single value for the whole order, which the server has always
stored, and which the orders list has always printed under the order number. No
new column, no migration; the wizard simply stops asking the wrong question.

### Composed — declared, by Claude Opus 5, under Rule 4's 2026-09-06 delegation

| Chinese | English | Why it is here |
|---|---|---|
| `下单日期` | Order date | The field label. **Two live pieces, no new character.** `下单` is this very file's own word for the event, live on the order page's subheading (`T('placed', '下单')`, `16-purchasing.js`) and in nine other admin files; `日期` is live in eleven (`10-extras.js` table head, `20-waste.js`, `22-finance.js`, `26-print.js`, and `开票日期` in this same purchasing file). Only the pairing is composed, which is why it is declared rather than treated as a lift. **Rejected:** `采购日期` (reads as "procurement date" — a category of activity rather than the moment this order was placed, and `采购` is already carrying the screen's name, 采购单, so the label would echo the page title instead of naming the field); `订单日期` (live-adjacent — `订单` is the CUSTOMER order word throughout `04-orders.js`, and Rule 1 keeps the two registers apart: a buyer reading `订单日期` on a purchasing screen is being handed the sales vocabulary); `日期` alone (live, and the honest minimum — rejected because this card now shows exactly one date where it used to show a different one, and an unqualified label is precisely what lets a reader carry the old meaning across); `制单日期` (the term on some Chinese ERP screens for when a document was raised — accurate, but it names the paperwork rather than the purchase, and nothing else in this product uses 制单) |
| `每件商品的到货时间可能不同。` | Items can arrive on different days. | The hint under the box, replacing the overdue promise. **Every piece is live** — `商品` in twenty-two files, `到货` in six (including this file's own `到货` column head and `在到货并勾选收货之前` on the review step), `时间` in fourteen, `每件` in two. What is composed is the sentence. It is here because removing the old hint without replacing it leaves an operator wondering where the arrival date went; this says why there isn't one. **Rejected:** saying nothing at all (the ordinary right answer under this document's own guidance — *a numeral or a name often needs no label* — and rejected only because the field did not gain a hint, it CHANGED one, and a silently vanished promise reads as a bug); `到货时间以实际收货为准。` (composed, and it asserts a procedure — "actual receipt governs" — which is true but is the receiving screen's business, not this card's); `商品到货时间不同。` (drops `每件` and `可能`, and with them the two things that make it true: it is per item, and it is a possibility rather than a rule) |

### Reused verbatim — no new string, recorded so the search is not repeated

The wizard's two recap strips — step two's summary chip row and step three's
review line — both printed the delivery date. Left alone they would have read
`预计 —` on every new order, which is the dead-UI failure that makes a removed
field look like a broken one. Both now print the order date under the live pair
already used for it on the order page: **`下单` / *Placed*** and **`下单` /
*placed*** (`T('Placed', '下单')` and `T('placed', '下单')`). Nothing was minted
for either.

### Not done, and deliberately

**`delivery_date` is NOT deleted.** Orders already carrying one keep it: the
list's `到货` column still prints it, the order page still shows `· 预计 …` when
there is one, kitchen copies still bring theirs across, and reopening such a
draft does not blank it. What changed is that the wizard no longer MINTS one.

**`isLate()` is untouched, and it will now fire less.** An order raised from
this screen carries no delivery date, so it can never be flagged `逾期`. That is
the correct consequence of removing a date that was not true — a badge derived
from a meaningless field is worse than no badge. **The honest replacement is a
per-line expected arrival**, one per supplier, with `逾期` meaning "this line is
late". That is a larger change than the one asked for and is recorded here as
the next step rather than smuggled into it.

## Setting a count over a debt (2026-09-15)

**Reported from the floor, 2026-09-15, as a screenshot of the Products grid with
a red toast across it and the question "what this error means?".** The toast read
`库存保存失败 — HTTP 400 · recount_clears_debt`. Register: **OPERATOR** (Rule 1) —
the back office Products grid and the product editor's store matrix, both
staff-facing.

**WHAT THE OPERATOR HAD DONE, AND THE SERVER WAS RIGHT.** The shelf was below
zero. Since §84 that is deliberate: −18 is how this system says eighteen units
were sold that were never held, and it is the ONLY record they are owed — the
kitchen reads it in the morning and cooks against it. The operator stood at an
empty box, typed 0, and `routes/retail-inventory.js` refused, because 0 is a true
statement about the room that silently discards eighteen customers' claim on the
next batch. The refusal is correct and is not what changed.

**WHAT WAS WRONG WAS THAT IT COULD NOT BE ANSWERED.** The route accepts one word
— `recount_reason: 'clears_debt'`, meaning *yes, I know what I am clearing* — and
the admin had no way to send it. So that cell could not be saved from that screen
by ANY value: the box refuses a negative and the server refuses everything above
it. What the operator got instead was the raw error code, because
`paSaveStoreStock` is a hand-rolled `fetch` that built its toast from `j.error`
and threw the server's own `message` and `owed` figure away — the audit A-09 gap,
in a call site that never went through `api()`. The editor matrix was worse: it
said `库存保存失败` and nothing else, whatever the server had answered.

**WHAT SHIPS.** Both grids now put the question to the operator in words and
resend with the acknowledgement if they say yes. §46's own refusal
(`recount_reason_required` — a kitchen-mode count that has gone DOWN) is answered
through the same dialog, since a kitchen-role operator reaching this screen met
the identical dead end.

### Reused verbatim — no new string, recorded so the search is not repeated

Six of the strings needed already exist for the same two questions on the phone,
and are lifted whole, punctuation included. Lifting rather than minting is the
point: the kitchen and the back office now name the same number with the same
words.

| Chinese | English | Lifted from |
|---|---|---|
| `有客户订单等待` | Customer orders waiting | `public/m.html` `kqOwed()` — the §84 owed block's heading, itself locked at v1.41. The dialog's title is this pair plus the numeral and nothing else |
| `数量变少了` | The count has gone down | `public/m.html:10429` — the §46 fork sheet, locked in this file's *Quick stock* table (2026-09-06) |
| `在架 {a}，你数的是 {b}。` | Shelf says {a}, you counted {b}. | `public/m.html:10430`, same table. **Full-width comma and full stop kept as they are there** — a lift that re-punctuates is not a lift |
| `数错了——保存 {n}` | I counted wrong — save {n} | `public/m.html:10431`, same table |
| `少了或坏了` | Something is missing or spoiled | `public/m.html:10432`, same table. Still deliberately generic: it is not a reason picker (§46) |
| `数量保持不变。请告诉负责记录损耗的人。` | The count stays as it is. Tell whoever records waste. | `public/m.html:10438`, same table |
| `请输入货架上的数量 — 0 或以上的整数。` | Enter the number on the shelf — a whole number, 0 or more. | `public/retail-admin/09-products.js`'s own list-grid validator, now also used by the editor matrix, which had no validation at all |

### Composed — declared, by Claude Opus 5, under Rule 4's 2026-09-06 delegation

Three strings, all for the §84 arm, which has nothing on the phone to copy: the
Quick stock fork sheet predates a shelf being allowed to sit below zero.

| Chinese | English | Why it is here |
|---|---|---|
| `在架 {q},因为有 {n} 件已售出但从未制作。这个数字是厨房的待制作依据,在此保存数量会将其清零。若要增加库存,请改用“加库存”。` | This shelf is at {q} because {n} were sold that were never made. That number is what the kitchen cooks against, and saving a count here sets it to zero. To add stock, record a batch instead. | The body of the dialog — the whole of why the save is being questioned. **Every piece is live:** `在架` (the *Quick stock* table above, and `public/m.html`), `件` (`库存将减少 N 件`, `public/m.html`), `售出` (`Items sold · 售出件数` in four admin files; `确认售出` on the Gzgrocery pickup card), `制作` / `待制作` (`public/m.html` queue tab, `lib/roles.js`), `厨房` (six admin files), `依据` (`匹配依据`, `排名依据`), `清零` (`使用次数清零`, `05-marketing.js`), `加库存` (`lib/roles.js`, `lib/capabilities.js` — the app's own name), `若要` (`02-settings-ops.js`), `请改用` (`04-orders.js`). What is composed is the sentence. **Rejected:** the server's own English `message`, shown as-is (what the code does everywhere else through `tdErrorText`, and rejected here because this is the one string in the exchange a Guangzhou shop operator has to act on, and the route's message is English-only); `库存为负,无法直接设置数量。` (accurate and useless — it restates the refusal as a rule and never says that somebody is waiting for those units, which is the only fact that makes the choice a choice); `有 {n} 件待制作,保存后将清零。` (shorter, and it was the draft — rejected because `待制作` alone reads as a production plan the kitchen chose, not as a debt that was incurred by a sale; the clause `已售出但从未制作` is doing the work) |
| `清零并保存` | Clear it and save | The destructive button. **Both halves live:** `清零` (`05-marketing.js`) and `保存` (everywhere). It names what happens to the owed figure FIRST and the save second, because the save is the part the operator already intended and the clearing is the part they did not. **Rejected:** `确认` (live, and the house default for a confirm button — rejected because a generic yes on a destructive dialog is exactly where a misread happens); `仍要保存` (*save anyway* — true, and it hides the consequence behind a shrug); `清零` alone (accurate about the debt, silent about the count being written) |
| `保留` | Keep it | The cancel button. **Live as a verb** — `保留已保存的值` (`15-integrations.js`), `留空即保留` (same file), `配置保留` (`public/cms-mobile-v2.html`), `保留哪部分` (`public/cms.html`) — the bare form is what is composed. Paired against `清零并保存` it can only mean the owed figure, which is the thing the operator is being asked about. **Rejected:** `取消` (live, and the house default — rejected because *cancel* answers "do you want to do this?" when the question is "which of these two numbers survives?"); `保留欠数` (`欠` appears once in this product, in `欠款`, which is money a customer owes; borrowing it for units owed to a customer points the debt the wrong way) |

### Not done, and deliberately

**The route's `message` is still English.** It is good English and the right
thing in a log or a support ticket, and every other refusal in this admin reaches
the operator through `tdErrorText` the same way. What changed is that the two
strings a shop operator must ACT on are bilingual at the call site. Making the
route bilingual is a larger change than the one reported and is not smuggled in
here.

**Nothing is added to `TD_ERR`.** A dictionary entry for either code would be
dead: `tdErrorText` prefers the server's `message`, which these refusals always
send, and the only two surfaces that can reach them now answer with the dialog
above.

## A shelf that went below zero (2026-09-15)

**Found by survey, 2026-09-15, following the §84 refusal shipped earlier the same
day.** Meituan and Eleme have alerted the merchant when an order line exceeds
on-hand since 2026-09-08 — the section *The alert a channel order raises* above
is that wording. The mini app, the web store and the counter till reach the same
condition through `lib/inventory.js`'s two commits and **raised nothing at all**.
Register: **OPERATOR** (Rule 1) — a WeCom alert to the shop, same surface as the
channel alerts.

**IT IS ONLY FOR THE PRODUCTS NOBODY COOKS, AND THAT IS WHY IT IS QUIET.** A
batch-made product's debt already has a surface, and a better one:
`lib/batch-owed.js` reads `physical_qty < 0 AND is_batch_made = 1` and puts it in
front of the people who cook, on the screen they open at the start of a shift.
For those, selling past zero is the DESIGNED behaviour, so alerting on them would
fire on the ordinary case. A bought-in product — a drink, a packaged snack — is
outside that filter by design, is `strict` by default, and reached only a row
inside the dashboard's out-of-stock panel, sitting among the plain zeroes at
`<= 0`. Eighteen customers could be owed a case of something nobody had thought
to re-order, and the only place saying so was a panel nobody had opened. The two
surfaces now split the problem exactly: the owed block covers what this kitchen
cooks, this alert covers what it does not.

**THIS IS NOT THE PLATFORM SENTENCE AND MUST NOT BORROW IT.** `chStockOversold`
says `已按 0 计` / *clamped at 0* because the platform ingest REFUSES the shelf:
the line exceeded on-hand and nothing moved. Our own commit does not clamp and
must not — §84's negative is the honest record that units were sold that were
never held, and it is the only record the kitchen has. The shelf DID move, the
write was correct, and what is being reported is that somebody is now owed
something. Reporting a completed deduction as a refused one would be false.

### Composed — declared, by Claude Opus 5, under Rule 4's 2026-09-06 delegation

One string. Everything else in the alert is lifted.

| key | EN | CN |
|---|---|---|
| `ownShelfBelowZero` | N line(s) took the count below zero and are now owed | N 项商品库存已为负数,有客户订单等待 |

**Three pieces, all live, and the seam between them is what is composed.**
`N 项商品` is `chStockUnmatched` verbatim, two rows up this very file.
`库存已为负数` is this product's own sentence for the condition, from the
admin error dictionary's `would_go_negative` (`该操作会使库存变为负数。`,
`public/retail-admin/01-core.js`) — the same words, moved from a refusal in the
future tense to a fact in the past. `有客户订单等待` is the kitchen owed
block's heading verbatim (`public/m.html` `kqOwed()`, locked at v1.41), which is
what makes this alert and the kitchen's own screen name the same debt the same
way. The English half mirrors `would_go_negative`'s English (*That would take the
count below zero*) for the same reason.

**Rejected:** `超卖` (oversold) — rejected once already for `chStockOversold`, on
the same grounds: it is the natural term, it appears nowhere in this product, and
it would be a new word for a reader who has one. **Rejected:** reusing
`chStockOversold` outright — it is one string less to explain and it says the
shelf was refused, which is the opposite of what happened. **Rejected:** `库存不足`
(the low-stock pair, live) — true of a shelf at 2 as well as one at −18, and the
whole point of the alert is that this one is past zero. **Rejected:** saying only
the number and letting `剩余 -3` carry it — the honest minimum, and rejected
because an alert arrives with no screen around it: the reader is holding a phone
and needs the sentence, not the column.

### Reused verbatim — no new string

| Chinese | English | Lifted from |
|---|---|---|
| `剩余` | (the balance, unlabelled) | `chStockWant`'s own half, this file |
| `订单` · `请检查` · the `🟠 {where} …\n{English}` shape | order · Please check | the channel alert above, and the daemons it took the shape from |
| `前台收银` | Counter till | `lib/capabilities.js` / `lib/roles.js` — the till's own capability label |
| `网店` · `微信` · `门店` · `手动` | Web store · WeChat · Shop · Manual | `public/retail-admin/03-dashboard.js` `DASH_CHAN_SHORT`, the dashboard's own channel chips |

**A walk-in is named by the till, not by its channel.** `fulfillment_type =
'counter'` wins over whatever `channel` the row carries, because a cashier
reading this needs to know it happened at their counter, and the channel column
on a counter sale says `shop` or `web` depending on how the till was opened.

### Not done, and deliberately

**No clamp, and no new gate.** The counter till does not check stock before a
sale and must not: a walk-in holding the item can never be refused because a
database row disagrees with the room. This adds a voice, not a lock.

**No new log table.** The platforms write a sync-log row because their ingest
has one. Ours already writes `retail_stock_ledger` with the negative
`balance_after`, the order it belongs to and the channel that caused it. That is
the durable record; only the saying-it-out-loud half was missing.

**The owed block still cannot tell a debt from a miscount.** A counter sale of
three units that were physically on the shelf but not in the record leaves the
same −3 as three units genuinely owed, and `lib/batch-owed.js` reads both as
units to cook. That is a REAL defect and a larger one — it needs a decision
about what a counter-sale negative means before any code — so it is recorded
here as the next step rather than smuggled into this change.

## A negative has two causes, and they have opposite remedies (2026-09-15)

**Ash, the same day the alert above shipped: "why i need that wecom messgae i
dont understand we give permission to sell products that are out of stock."**
The question was about permission and the answer to that half is that products
given permission are not alerted — `is_batch_made` is filtered out. But working
the question exposed a real defect in the string locked hours earlier. Register:
**OPERATOR** (Rule 1), the same WeCom alert.

**WHAT WAS WRONG.** `ownShelfBelowZero` ends `有客户订单等待` — *customer
orders waiting* — on every path. That is true of a DELIVERY order that outran
the shelf: the customer is waiting and somebody has to make or re-order the
units. It is false of a COUNTER SALE, and the counter is the path that actually
fires.

A walk-in rings up three colas. They are in the customer's hand, the money is in
the till, the customer leaves. The shelf goes to −3 because the record said 0
and there were three. **Nobody is owed anything.** What the negative says is that
the count was wrong.

**AND THE TILL IS ALMOST THE ONLY PATH.** A bought-in product is `strict` by
default, and the hard out-of-stock gate in `POST /orders`
(`routes/retail.js`) refuses a strict line at checkout — so the mini app and the
web store cannot ordinarily reach this alert at all. The counter till has no such
gate, correctly: a walk-in holding the item can never be refused because a
database row disagrees with the room. So the wording that was wrong was the
wording nearly every alert would carry.

**WHY IT IS WORTH A SEPARATE STRING RATHER THAN A SOFTER SHARED ONE.** The two
readings have OPPOSITE remedies. *Units are owed* sends somebody to re-order
stock that is not missing. *The count was wrong* sends them to recount a shelf,
which is the actual repair. A sentence vague enough to cover both would send
them nowhere.

### Composed — declared, by Claude Opus 5, under Rule 4's 2026-09-06 delegation

| key | EN | CN |
|---|---|---|
| `ownShelfMiscount` | , so it was likely counted wrong | ,可能是数错了 |

**One clause, swapped for `有客户订单等待` when the fulfilment is `counter`.
Everything around it is unchanged**, which is the point: one alert, one shape,
and the clause that names the cause.

`数错了` is a VERBATIM lift — `public/m.html:10431`, the §46 fork sheet's own
answer (`I counted wrong — save {n}` / `数错了——保存 {n}`), locked in this file's
*Quick stock* table (2026-09-06) and lifted again for the admin dialog at v1.85.
It is the phrase this product already uses for exactly this fact, and reusing it
means the till alert and the phone's recount fork say the same thing the same
way. `可能` is live in seven files (`public/m.html`, `public/cms.html`,
`lib/product-blocks-read.js`, and the purchase-order hint locked at v1.84:
`每件商品的到货时间可能不同。`). Only the seam is composed.

**`可能` is load-bearing, not hedging.** The system cannot know which of the two
it was — it sees a negative, not a shelf. A bare `数错了` would assert a
diagnosis, and an alert that asserts wrongly once is an alert nobody reads twice.
The English `likely` carries the same weight.

**Rejected:** `库存记录有误` (the record is in error) — accurate, and it names a
database rather than a shelf; the operator's next action is to go and count, so
the sentence should say counting. **Rejected:** `请盘点` (please stocktake) —
`盘点` appears nowhere in this product, and the alert already ends `请检查`.
**Rejected:** dropping the counter path from the alert entirely — considered
seriously, and rejected because a till sale that outruns the record is exactly
how a product comes to read sold-out on the web shop while sitting on the shelf.
**Rejected:** one sentence covering both causes (`库存与记录不符`, *stock does not
match the record*) — true of both and useful for neither, per the remedies above.

### Not done, and deliberately

**The delivery clause is unchanged.** `有客户订单等待` stays exactly as locked
at v1.86 for every non-counter path. It was never wrong there; it was wrong
everywhere else.
