## Holding a paid order for a restock (2026-09-15)

**Ash, told that §134 fixes the next order and can do nothing for the one that
prompted it: "build all and automatically deploy to live."** Register:
**OPERATOR** (Rule 1) — the order drawer's transition row, seen by office and
counter staff, never by a customer.

**NO STRING IS MINTED HERE, and that is the finding worth recording.** The new
control moves a settled order into the restock queue. The obvious label followed
the row's own `Mark …` pattern — `标记等待补货`, compounding 标记 (live in
`标记已发货`, `标记已送达`, `标记可取货`, `标记已取货`) with 等待补货. That
compound is not live anywhere, so it would have been a composition, declarable
under the 2026-09-06 amendment.

**It was not needed.** `public/retail-admin/04-orders.js` already carries the
exact pair on the card this button produces:

> `cuT('Waiting on stock', '等待补货')` — `awaitingRestockCard`

and 等待补货 is the same word behind the `awaiting_restock` status chip
(`retail-admin.html`), the mini app's order list and order page
(`miniapp/pages/orders`, `miniapp/pages/order`) and the web store
(`public/shop.html`). Five live surfaces, one word.

**LOCKED — lifted verbatim, nothing new:**

| English | 中文 | Lifted from |
|---|---|---|
| Hold for restock | 等待补货 | `04-orders.js` `awaitingRestockCard` — `cuT('Waiting on stock', '等待补货')` |

**THE ENGLISH IS RESTRUCTURED AND THE CHINESE IS NOT, which is the preference
order working as written.** *Waiting on stock* reads as a state; in a row beside
*Start preparing* and *Cancel + refund* the control needed to read as an act, so
the English became *Hold for restock*. The rule locks the Chinese, and 等待补货
serves both readings — it is what the queue is called — so restructuring the half
that is free to move removed the reason to mint the half that is not. **This is
the case CLAUDE.md describes:** *"prefer restructuring a label so an existing live
string fits it over minting a new one."*

**REJECTED, recorded so the next session does not re-open it:**

* **标记等待补货** — a composition; unnecessary once the button stopped needing to
  echo the `Mark …` pattern, which three of the row's five controls
  (`开始备货`, `退款`, `取消并退款`) do not follow either.
* **暂缓备货** (*hold off preparing*) — invented, and it describes what the
  operator stops doing rather than what the order is now waiting for. The queue is
  named for the wait, not for the pause.
* **待补货** — live, but as the SHORT form on the packing badge and the parcel
  pill (`待补货 — N 项`), where space is tight. The drawer's transition row has
  room for the full word the card uses, and matching the card is the point.

**The three refusal messages the route returns are English-only**, which is the
existing convention on `/api/retail-admin/orders/:id/status` — every message it
already returns (`A refund is currently being processed…`) is English. No register
question arises and nothing is locked for them here.

## The order says what it was told (2026-09-15)

§138. The order drawer gains two lines recording what the customer was shown at
checkout: that a line was sold as a pre-order, and that the order moved shop
because one was shut. Register: **OPERATOR** (Rule 1) — the back office order
drawer, beside `rerouteShortNote`, which answers the same question.

### Reused verbatim — no new string

| Chinese | English | Lifted from |
|---|---|---|
| `预订` | Pre-order | `computePreorderNotice`'s own `kicker_cn` / `kicker_en` (`routes/retail.js`), so the drawer and the screen the customer read name it identically. Also live in the product editor's policy picker (`广州外可预订`, `预订开始`) |

### Composed — declared, by Claude Opus 5, under Rule 4's 2026-09-06 delegation

| Chinese | English | Why it is here |
|---|---|---|
| `改派 · 门店已关闭` | Rerouted, shop was closed | The move line. **Every piece is live:** `改派` is this admin's own word for an order changing shop, live as `改派 · 缺货` (*rerouted · out of stock*) and `手动改派` (*manually routed*) in `public/m.html` and `04-orders.js`; `门店` is the dashboard's own channel chip (`shop: ['Shop', '门店']`); `已` and `关闭` are live throughout (`关闭。没有订单需要等待制作。`, `06-delivery.js`). The `改派 · <reason>` SHAPE is lifted too — it is exactly how the live reroute note is built, so this reads as one more reason rather than a new construct. What is composed is `门店已关闭` and its pairing with that shape. **Rejected:** `原门店` (*the original shop*) — neither `原` nor `原门店` appears anywhere in this product, and it names the wrong half: the reader is looking at an order and wants to know why it moved, not what to call the shop it left. **Rejected:** `改派 · 门店休息` (*shop resting*) — `休息` is softer and is not the word the closure switch uses. **Rejected:** reusing the customer-facing store-move notice copy — it is the operator's own text from `storefront_config`, may be blank, and is written for a shopper rather than for the office. **Rejected:** no label at all, printing only the shop code — the honest minimum elsewhere in this file, and rejected here because a bare code beside an order reads as the shop that FILLED it, which is the opposite of what the field holds |

### Not done

**The store is printed as its CODE, not its name.** The drawer shows `moved_from_store`
verbatim. A code is what the column holds and what ops quote to each other; looking
the name up would be a second query per drawer for a line that appears on a small
minority of orders.

## The bind page stops asking for an address (2026-09-16)

The bind-profile page (`miniapp/pages/profile`) loses its address field. It was
a single `wx.chooseAddress` shortcut, and a customer with nothing saved in
WeChat does not get a list to pick from — WeChat opens its own create-address
form, whose chrome follows the customer's WeChat language while its region list
is Chinese-only. An English-speaking first-timer got readable buttons over an
unreadable list, at the moment before they had bought anything. The address is
collected at checkout instead, where our own bilingual picker already lives.
Register: **CUSTOMER** (Rule 1) — the mini app.

### Nothing composed, nothing lifted

The four EN/CN pairs the field used (`ADDRESS · NAME · PHONE · 地址 · 姓名 · 电话`,
`Import from WeChat · 从微信一键导入`, `Loading… · 加载中…`, and the hint
`WeChat gives us your name, phone, and full address in a single tap. ·
一键导入姓名、手机号与完整地址。`) are deleted with the control they labelled.
They are recorded here as removed, not banned: if the shortcut ever returns as a
secondary control beside a manual form, these are the strings it takes back.

### Subtracted — declared, by Claude Opus 5, under Rule 4's 2026-09-06 delegation

| Before (live) | After | Why |
|---|---|---|
| `微信不会自动告诉我们您的昵称、头像和地址，请点击下方手动绑定。` | `微信不会自动告诉我们您的昵称和头像，请点击下方手动绑定。` | The page's own subtitle. It listed the three things the page bound; one of them is no longer on the page, so the list is two items and the enumerating `、` collapses into `和`. **Nothing is minted** — every character survives from the live sentence, in its original order. This is declared rather than treated as a silent edit because the resulting sentence is a string nobody locked, and an undeclared one cannot be told from a locked one by whoever comes after. **Rejected:** leaving the sentence as written — it would promise an address binding the page no longer performs. **Rejected:** rewriting it around the two remaining items (`微信不会自动提供您的昵称和头像…`) — `不会自动告诉我们` is the live phrasing and carries the page's voice; swapping it would be minting to no purpose |

The English half changes the same way and for the same reason:
`WeChat keeps your nickname, avatar and address private.` →
`WeChat keeps your nickname and avatar private.`

### Not done

**The `scope.address` shortcut is untouched on `miniapp/pages/addresses`.** There
it sits beside our own bilingual city→district picker and the manual fields, so a
customer who cannot read the WeChat list has somewhere to go. Removing it from the
bind page removes it from the one screen where it was the only option, which is
the whole of this change.


## The spec strip picks its words from what the catalogue already says (2026-09-16)

**§145. Nothing here is composed.** Every pair below was read out of the live
catalogue by `scripts/diagnose_spec_strip_labels.js`, over the 724 spec-strip
cells carried by 242 products, and is recorded with the count that earned it a
place. This is the preference order in `CLAUDE.md` — *copy a live pair, then
lift from the source, then compose* — resolving at the first step, because the
office had already written every one of these words.

**Why they are being locked at all, given that they are lifts.** The list is
what the editor will offer from now on, so a pair that is wrong here becomes
wrong on every product written after today. A lift can be as wrong as a
composition if the wrong twin is lifted — and there were twins to choose
between in almost every row. The choices are recorded so they can be read and
corrected rather than re-derived.

### The labels — ten pairs

| EN | 中文 | EN cells | 中文 cells | the twin NOT taken |
|---|---|---:|---:|---|
| Product Type | 产品类型 | 64 | 83 | `Type` / 类型 (66 / 40) — see below |
| Net Wt | 净含量 | 163 | 200 | `Net Weight` (29), 净重 (2) |
| Origin | 产地 | 156 | 150 | 原产地 (4), 原料产地 (3) |
| Shelf Life | 保质期 | 57 | 57 | — |
| Diet | 饮食 | 29 | 29 | — |
| Form | 形态 | 28 | 28 | — |
| Use | 用法 | 26 | 26 | — |
| Category | 类别 | 18 | 14 | 品类 (6), 分类 (5) |
| Certification | 认证 | 13 across three spellings | 14 | `Certified` (4), `Certifications` (4) |
| Storage | 储存方式 | 5 | 5 | — |

Four of these — Shelf Life / 保质期, Diet / 饮食, Form / 形态, Use / 用法 —
match exactly on both sides, which is as strong as pairing evidence gets from a
count.

**`Type` / 类型 is the one rejection that is not about spelling.** It is a
second label for the same idea as `Product Type`, holding the same answers.
Offering both would continue a 130-cell split, so one had to go; the Chinese
decided it, 产品类型 (83) over 类型 (40). **The 66 cells that already say `Type`
are not touched** — this is a choice about what the editor offers next, not
about what the catalogue has said before.

### The values — sixteen pairs, each under its own label

**Origin** — `Guangzhou, China` / 中国广州 (109) · `China` / 中国 (15) ·
`Yunnan, China` / 中国云南 (5 / 3) · `Israel` / 以色列 (3) ·
`Northeast China` / 中国东北 (2) · `Tunisia, North Africa` / 突尼斯（北非）(2)

**Shelf Life** — `See packaging` / 见包装标示 (46 / 38) ·
`Up to 6 months frozen` / 冷冻最长保存6个月 (5 / 3)

*Rejected here:* 见包装 (8) and 请见包装标注 (2). Three Chinese sentences for
one English one; 见包装标示 is the plurality and the most complete.

**Diet** — `100% plant` / 纯植物 (28) · `Vegan` / 纯素 (1)

**Form** — `Raw · whole` / 生 · 整粒 (6) · `Dried · raw` / 干豆 · 生 (4) ·
`Raw seeds` / 生 · 籽 (4) · `Whole rolled` / 整粒压片 (2) ·
`Organic powder` / 有机粉 (2)

**Use** — `Snack · bake · blend` / 直接吃·烘焙·打浆 (6) ·
`Sprinkle · blend · soak` / 撒·打浆·泡发 (4) · `No soak needed` / 无需浸泡 (2) ·
`Rinse & cook` / 冲洗后烹煮 (2) · `Soak & cook` / 泡发后烹煮 (2) ·
`Cook in minutes` / 几分钟煮好 (2) · `Season anything` / 万物可调味 (2)

**Category** — `Home & Body` / 家居护理 (6 / 4) · `Fresh & Deli` / 鲜食与熟食
(3 / 2) · `Protein Powder` / 蛋白粉 (2) · `Dry & Pantry` / 干货 & 储备食材
(2 / 1) · `Chili Paste` / 辣椒酱 (2)

*Rejected here:* 家居护肤 (1) and 家居与身体护理 (1) — the same English category
translated three separate times. 家居护理 is the plurality.

**Product Type** — `Ready-to-eat spread` / 即食抹酱 (9) ·
`Ready-to-eat nut butter` / 即食坚果酱 (8) · `Dried Fruit` / 干果 (4 / 3) ·
`Ready-to-eat refrigerated food` / 即食冷藏食品 (3) ·
`Frozen · heat to eat` / 冷冻 · 加热食用 (3) · `Fresh Food` / 鲜食 (2) ·
`Glass Cleaner` / 玻璃清洁液 (2) · `Laundry Liquid` / 洗衣液 (2)

### What deliberately has no list

**Net Wt** — 67 distinct weights across 163 cells. A dropdown there is a
dropdown nobody can find their product in.

**Certification** (13 cells) and **Storage** (5) — too thin to draw a list from
without inventing one, which is the thing this document exists to prevent.

**`hero_intro`, all of it** — the kicker is 172 distinct answers across 248
fills, the highlight phrase 207 across 256, the body 217 across 258. That is the
catalogue's voice and it stays typed.

## The approver files the claim (2026-09-16)

§146. A staff expense claim submitted without a category can be filed by the
approver on the row being decided, and re-filed afterwards if it was already
approved — until now the only way to correct one was to void the claim and ask
the employee to send it again. Register: **OPERATOR** (Rule 1) — the approval
queue on both the admin Finance screen and the phone, and two server refusals.

### Reused verbatim — no new string, recorded so the search is not repeated

| Chinese | English | Lifted from |
|---|---|---|
| `未记录` | Not recorded | `public/retail-admin/22-finance.js`, where it is already Finance's own word for a category column holding nothing. The approval row draws a blank category with the SAME words the screen two clicks away uses for the same value — a second phrasing here would be a second name for one state |
| `— 选择分类 —` | — pick a category — | `public/retail-admin/09-products.js`'s own empty option, with the same em-dash framing. Also live as `选择分类` in `05-marketing.js` and `07-content-billing.js` |
| `已保存` | Saved | `public/retail-admin/01-core.js`. The toast after a re-filing is the product's ordinary save toast, because that is what it is |
| `更改` | Change | `public/retail-admin/04-orders.js`, where it already opens a picker over a value that is already set. The ledger row's link, and it reads correctly over a blank too — the category cell beside it says `Not recorded`, so the link changes it from nothing |
| `仅审批通过的申请` | only an approved claim | This route file's own `ERR.notApproved` (`routes/staff-expenses.js`), verbatim as the opening of the new refusal below |

### Composed — declared, by Claude Opus 5, under Rule 4's 2026-09-06 delegation

| Chinese | English | Why it is here |
|---|---|---|
| `仅审批通过的申请可修改分类` | only an approved claim can be re-categorised | The refusal when something with no ledger row behind it is sent to the re-filing route. **Only `可修改分类` is new, and none of its characters is:** the sentence opens with `仅审批通过的申请` lifted whole from `ERR.notApproved` one line above it in the same object, `修改` is live across this admin (`修改记录`, `如需可修改`, `修改营业`), and `分类` is this product's own word for the thing (`选择分类`, `分类标签`). The `仅…可…` shape is `ERR.notApproved`'s own. **Rejected:** `重新分类` (*re-classify*) — `重新` appears nowhere in this vocabulary and reads as re-doing an act, while what is offered is changing a value. **Rejected:** `仅已审批的申请…` — `审批通过` is the live wording for this status; shortening it would put a second name on one state |
| `该笔已记入采购单 — 请在采购单上修改分类` | this claim is on a purchase order — set the category on the order | The refusal for a claim whose goods have not arrived: the order it raised owns the cost, under the LINE's category, so re-filing the claim would move nothing. **The first half and the shape are `ERR.onOrder`'s, verbatim** — `该笔已记入采购单 — 请` — which is the live refusal for voiding the same kind of claim, so the two read as one rule with two exits. What is composed is `在采购单上修改分类`; `采购单` is live throughout Purchasing, `上` and `修改` are live, `分类` as above. **Rejected:** merely reusing `ERR.onOrder` whole (*cancel that order instead*) — it names the wrong remedy: nobody re-filing a category wants the order cancelled. **Rejected:** a refusal that does not name a way out at all — `ERR.onOrder`'s own comment gives the house rule, that the refusal names the way out rather than merely refusing |
| `此账号无法修改费用分类` | this account cannot change an expense category | The refusal for an account holding neither `expenses:approve` nor `expenses:cancel`. **The shape and every piece are live:** `此账号无法审批费用申请` and `此账号无法作废费用申请` sit in the same object (`ERR.forbidden` / `ERR.forbiddenVoid`), so this is the third of a set rather than a new sentence — `此账号无法` verbatim, `费用` verbatim from both, `修改` and `分类` as above. **Rejected:** a generic `无权限` (*no permission*) — the two refusals beside it name the act being refused, and a reader who is told only "forbidden" goes looking for the wrong thing |
| `选择分类` | pick a category | The refusal for an EMPTY value sent to the re-filing route. **Both halves are live** — the Chinese verbatim from `05-marketing.js` / `07-content-billing.js` / `09-products.js`, the English from `09-products.js`'s own `— pick a category —` with the dashes dropped because this is a sentence rather than a menu row. Listed here rather than under *Reused* only because that pairing, as one refusal string, is assembled here for the first time |

### Not done, and deliberately

**No string was minted for the picker's LABEL, because it has none.** The control
is a `<select>` whose blank row reads `— pick a category —` and whose filled
state reads the category itself. A label above it would say *Category* over a
list of categories.

**The claim form is untouched.** Making the category compulsory there was
rejected in §145 — somebody standing beside a taxi does not know the chart of
accounts — so no new sentence was needed to insist on one.

## The storage box picks its words too (2026-09-16)

**§147. Nothing composed.** Every pair was read out of the live catalogue by
`scripts/diagnose_block_field_values.js` over the 218 storage boxes, and is
recorded with the count that earned it a place — and, where there was a twin,
with the twin that lost.

**Ash set the rule before seeing the data:** *"you pick, most-used wins."* So
these are not judgements about which wording is better. They are counts.

### An icon is a string, and two of them are lies

`❄️` is U+2744 followed by U+FE0F. `❄` is U+2744 alone. **They render
identically and they are not equal.** The catalogue carries 138 of the first
and 5 of the second; 🌡️ (18) and 🌡 (9) split the same way.

This document has never had to lock a character that cannot be seen before.
Recorded here because the next person to read the icon list will see two
identical-looking glyphs in the rejected column and assume it is a mistake.

**Locked:** `❄️` `🌬️` `🫙` `🧊` `🌡️` `🏠` (left) · `❄️` `📅` `🔒` `📦` `🧊`
`🫧` `⏱️` (right), each with its variation selector where it has one.
**Rejected:** the bare `❄` and `🌡`.

### The heads — eleven pairs

**Left card** — `Refrigerate` / 冷藏保存 (52 / 59) · `Cool & Dry` / 阴凉干燥
(55 / 34) · `Keep it dry` / 干燥存放 (19 / 17) · `Keep Frozen` / 冷冻保存
(16 / 19) · `Room Temperature` / 常温保存 (12 / 13) · `Unopened` / 未开封
(11 / 17)

*Rejected:* `Keep refrigerated` (13) and `Keep Refrigerated`; `Cool & dry`;
`Keep frozen`; `Before Opening` (6); 冷藏 (12); 置于阴凉干燥处 (16).

**Right card** — `Shelf Life` / 保质期 (57 / 57, exact) · `After Opening` /
开封后 (41 / 44) · `To keep longer` / 延长保鲜 (13 / 13, exact) ·
`Refrigerated` / 冷藏保存 (12 / 13) · `Freeze for longer` / 长期保存请冷冻
(9 / 9, exact)

*Rejected:* `Shelf life`; `After opening`; `Use After Opening` (16); `Freeze`
(8); `Freezing` (7); 开封后尽快食用 (16).

### The units — fourteen pairs

**Left** — `°C at all times` / °C 全程冷藏 (49 / 42) · `°C away from sunlight` /
°C 避免阳光直射 (17 / 16) · `Away from direct sunlight` / 避免阳光直射 (15 / 13) ·
`Store away from heat and moisture` / 远离高温和潮湿 (14 / 6) ·
`Airtight, away from heat & light` / 密封,避免高温与阳光 (12 / 11) · `months` /
个月 (12 / 12) · `days — eat fresh` / 天内尝鲜 (7 / 7, exact)

**Right** — `See packaging` / 见包装标示 (37 / 31) · `days for peak potency` /
天内风味与效力最佳 (16 / 17) · `months, sealed` / 个月（密封）(15 / 15, exact) ·
`Refrigerate or freeze — the oils stay fresh` / 冷藏或冷冻——油脂更不易氧化
(13 / 12) · `months` / 个月 (12 / 13) · `Up to 6 months` / 最长可保存6个月
(9 / 9, exact) · `days in the fridge` / 天内（冷藏）(8 / 8, exact)

*Rejected:* `°C  全程冷藏` with a double space (a twin of the first, invisible
in the same way the snowflake is).

### The note — two pairs, and why only two

`Shelf life: see packaging.` / 保质期：见包装标示。 (27 / 21) ·
`Reseal tightly after opening.` / 开封后请密封保存。 (17 / 17, exact)

**The field has 77 distinct answers and the usage report truncated the long
ones.** Pairing a long English sentence to its Chinese would have meant guessing
which 中文 belongs to which — and a guessed pair is indistinguishable from a
locked one to everyone who comes after, which is the failure this document
exists to prevent.

These two were printed whole and read as plainly the same sentence. Everything
else stays typed. `scripts/test_storage_box_picklists.js` refuses a note list
longer than four entries, so the next person to add one has to re-measure rather
than remember.

## Three more things a product can be free of (2026-09-16)

**§148. One composed string, two lifts.** The Free-of checkboxes have been
`无坚果 · 无大豆 · 无麸质 · 无五香` and nothing else. Reading the free-text tail
on production shows what the office kept typing beside them: `animal products`
on 75 products, `dairy` on 15, `egg` on 14.

**The tail is why this entry can exist at all.** A list without a free-text
escape hatch teaches people to pick the nearest wrong answer, and the list never
learns it was short. This one kept what it did not recognise, and three years of
hand-typing is now a measurement.

### Lifted — 无动物成分

Verbatim from `miniapp/pages/faq/faq.js`, where the mini app already answers
this question for customers. The English `Animal products` follows the sibling
pattern (`Nuts`, `Soy`, `Gluten`, `5 Spice`): the thing itself, not the claim.

### Lifted — 无乳制品

Verbatim from `miniapp/pages/product/product.js`, and also live in
`public/shop/_gallery.fixtures.js`. 乳制品 is the standard term and was already
the shop's.

### Composed — 无蛋

**The only composed string here.** Nothing live said "free of egg" — `无蛋` is
absent from the committed tree, and `鸡蛋` appears once, unrelated.

**Built on the live shape**, which is the whole basis: its five siblings are all
`无` + the thing (无坚果, 无大豆, 无麸质, 无五香, 无乳制品), so `无蛋` is the
only form consistent with the row it joins.

**Rejected:**

- **无鸡蛋** — "no chicken egg". More specific than the shop needs, and narrower
  than the sibling terms, which name a category rather than a species.
- **不含蛋** — "does not contain egg". A different shape from every sibling in
  the row; mixing `无X` and `不含X` in one line of checkboxes reads as though the
  two mean different things.

### Not locked — the typo fold

`5spice` (16 products) folds to the existing `5 spice` checkbox on read. That is
a spelling correction, not a vocabulary entry: no new string, and nothing is
written until somebody opens and saves the product.

## The allergens this catalogue names (2026-09-16)

**§149. Nothing composed.** Eight terms, each half verified live in the
committed tree before being offered.

| EN | 中文 | 中文 already live in |
|---|---|---|
| Tree nuts | 坚果 | `lib/category-tree.js` |
| Peanuts | 花生 | `lib/feie.js` |
| Soy | 大豆 | `miniapp/pages/product/product.js` |
| Gluten | 麸质 | `miniapp/pages/product/product.js` |
| Sesame | 芝麻 | `lib/review-seeder.js` |
| Coconut | 椰子 | `lib/review-seeder.js` |
| Legumes | 豆类 | `lib/category-tree.js` |
| Gluten-containing cereals | 含麸质谷物 | `public/shop/_gallery.fixtures.js` |

### The word NOT offered, and it is the most-used one

**`Nuts` — 55 cards — is deliberately absent**, and so are `Other nuts` (14)
and `Other tree nuts` (15).

Every other list in §145 to §148 was decided by most-used-wins. This one was
not, because the counts are answering the wrong question: peanuts and tree nuts
are **different allergens**, and a customer reading `Nuts` cannot tell which
they are being warned about. Ash was asked rather than shown a count.

`Other …` only means anything beside a named nut — *"contains hazelnuts, may
contain other tree nuts"* — so it belongs in free text, where the sentence that
gives it meaning also lives.

### 未声明 is the shop's word for "nothing to declare" — not 未声明常见致敏原

**This is a correction to what was nearly locked.** Three hand-typed sentences
were put to Ash as the candidates for *nothing to declare*, and he chose
`None declared` / 未声明常见致敏原 (50 cards).

**There is a fourth, and it was already in the code.** When the contains row is
blank, three renderers each print their own copy of `None declared` / **未声明**:

- `lib/product-blocks-read.js` — the counter menu
- `public/shop/product-blocks.js` — the web shop
- `miniapp/components/product-blocks/product-blocks.js` — the mini app

So the hand-typed 未声明常见致敏原 was never what a blank card printed, and the
66 cards carrying a typed variant are producing a chip that duplicates the empty
state.

**Locked: 未声明**, on the strength of being the one three surfaces already
agree on — changing it would mean editing all three plus a WeChat release, to
replace a working string with a longer one.

**Recorded as rejected:** 未声明常见致敏原 (50 cards, hand-typed) and
本产品不含已知致敏原 (11 cards) — the second of which is a stronger claim, saying
the product *has* no allergens rather than that none were declared. That
difference was put to Ash in those words before he chose.

`scripts/test_allergen_options.js` holds the three renderers in agreement, and
refuses to let any of the four typed variants be offered as a term.


## What a busy system says (2026-09-16)

**§151. One composed string.** The counter and the admin used to answer a
locked database with `401 Not authenticated`, which sends the operator to the
login page. It now answers `503` with the code `service_busy`, and the admin's
own error table turns that code into a sentence.

### Composed — 系统繁忙,请稍后重试。

**English:** `The system is busy right now — try again in a moment.`

**Both halves have a live parent, which is why this is composed rather than
invented.** 请稍后重试 is live in five pairs in `public/retail-admin/01-core.js`
alone (`too_soon`, `too_many_attempts`, `refund_already_processing`, and two
more), and 系统繁忙 is WeChat's own wording for exactly this transient state —
it is already in the tree at `routes/retail.js`, naming the WeChat error code
this string describes.

**Rejected:**

- **操作过于频繁,请稍后重试。** — live, and the closest thing to a ready-made
  answer. Refused because it says the OPERATOR is going too fast. They are not:
  a queue they cannot see is busy, and blaming them for it would send somebody
  looking for a mistake they did not make.
- **尝试次数过多,请稍后重试。** — same objection, more strongly. It counts the
  operator's attempts, and the first attempt can meet this.
- **加载失败,请重试。** — live, and accurate for a read. This gate covers writes
  too, and "loading failed" on a save is the wrong word for the most frightening
  case: a save whose outcome the operator cannot see.
## Signing a customer out says what it actually does (2026-09-16)

**§152. Six composed strings, seven verbatim lifts.** The control is
`cuForceLogout` in `public/retail-admin/08-customers.js` and `reg('forceLogout')`
in `public/m.html`; both post to
`POST /api/retail-admin/customers/:id/force-logout`, which deletes every
`retail_auth_tokens` row for the customer and nothing else.

### Why the old copy had to go

It said *"She can sign back in normally"*. True, and misleading by omission.
`miniapp/utils/api.js` catches the 401 this revoke causes, runs a fresh silent
`wx.login` and replays the request — so in the WeChat mini app there is no
sign-in screen and the customer notices nothing at all. On the web storefront
(`public/shop.html`) there is no silent re-login, so the sign-out is real and
visible on the next request. An operator reaching for this because a phone was
stolen has to know which of those two they are getting. The copy now says so,
and points at Block, which `requireRetailAuth` refuses even on a valid token.

The phone's zero branch also promised to end sessions when there were none.

### The lifts

| 中文 | Lifted verbatim from |
|---|---|
| 当前有 N 台设备已登录。 | `public/m.html` — the same control's phone sheet |
| 退出所有设备？ | `public/m.html` — the same control's phone sheet |
| 网页商店 | `public/retail-admin/17-setup-guide.js` |
| 微信小程序 | `public/retail-admin/15-integrations.js`, `17-setup-guide.js` |
| 重新登录 | `public/retail-admin/11-meituan.js`, `01-core.js`, `public/m.html` |
| 活跃会话 | the string this amendment replaces, in `08-customers.js` |
| 已退出登录 | `public/m.html` — the same control's success toast |

`退出失败` — the desktop handler's `errLabelCn`, printed when the POST
itself fails — is untouched by this amendment and recorded here only so the
guard in `scripts/test_force_logout_truth.js` can insist that EVERY Chinese
sentence in the handler is accounted for, rather than only the ones a list
happens to name.

`如需锁定，请改用封禁。` is the old desktop string's own
closing clause, carried over. Its comma is the only edit: the original typed a
half-width `,` mid-sentence, which the rest of this document does not do.

### The six composed, by Claude, 2026-09-16

| 中文 | EN it pairs with | Built from |
|---|---|---|
| 撤销可以清除泄露的令牌，但不能锁定账号。 | Revoking clears a leaked token — it does not lock them out. | 撤销 + 令牌可能泄露 + 锁定 + 账号, all four live |
| 网页商店会立即退出； | On the web store they are signed out at once; | 网页商店 (lift) + 立即 (from 将立即撤销) |
| 微信小程序会自动重新登录，不会出现登录页。 | in the WeChat Mini Program they are signed back in automatically, with no sign-in screen. | 微信小程序 + 重新登录 (both lifts); 会自动 and 不会出现登录页 are new |
| 当前没有活跃会话，无可撤销内容。 | No active sessions — there is nothing to revoke. | 活跃会话 (lift) + 撤销 (lift) |
| 已退出登录，已撤销 N 个会话 | Signed out — N sessions revoked | 已退出登录 (lift) + 已撤销 (live, `04-orders.js`) + N 个会话 (from the toast this replaces). The EN half joins on an em dash, NOT on ` · `, so the walker has no pair to find in either language |
| 当前没有活跃会话，未撤销任何内容 | No active sessions — nothing was revoked | same parts as the fourth row, past tense |

**Candidates rejected.** 退出登录 alone (live, `m.html`) was the obvious
reuse for the toast and was dropped because it cannot carry the count, which is
the only thing the toast exists to say. 她可正常重新登录 — the
old desktop clause — was rejected outright: in the mini app she does not
*可* sign back in, she simply is, without being asked.

### The mechanical fault this fixes alongside the copy

The old toast was authored as an `EN · CN` pair with the count in the middle:

    Signed out all devices · N sessions revoked · 已退出所有设备(N 个会话)

`processNode` in `public/retail-admin/10-extras.js` splits a text node on its
**first** `' · '`, so that resolved to `en = "Signed out all devices"` and
`cn = "N sessions revoked · 已退出所有设备(N 个会话)"`. English
operators were shown **no number at all**; Chinese ones were shown the English
half. `toast()` assigns `textContent`, which replaces the text node and is
therefore a childList mutation the walker's `MutationObserver` does reach — a
toast is not exempt from the pair rule for being transient. Every string in this
section is composed through `cuT` / `T` so the walker never sees a pair.

This is the same trap `cuOpenAdjust` documents a few lines further down the same
file, which is the second time it has been live in this one function.


## How many a pick-any question takes (2026-09-16)

§153 gives a "Pick any" option group a floor and a ceiling. Two registers need
words for it: the CUSTOMER sheets print how many are wanted, and the OPERATOR
editor labels the two boxes that set it.

**FOUR COMPOSED PAIRS and ONE composed operator line, composed by Claude under
Rule 4 as amended.** Every one is a live shape with a numeral swapped in; the
numerals do most of the work, which is why there are five new strings and not a
table of them.

### The counter word is 项, and 款 was rejected

`至少选一项` is already live in `miniapp/pages/add-to-order/add-to-order.js`
(`copy.pickRequired`, the 中文 half). It is this codebase's own sentence for
*pick at least one of these answers*, and it settles the counter: an option
answer is a 项.

**Rejected: 款**, from Ash's own product label `爽脆配菜（选2款）`. It reads well
and it is the phrasing that prompted this feature — but it is ONE operator-typed
label on ONE product, while 项 is the word the software already uses for the
same thing wherever it speaks for itself. Recorded rather than dropped because
the cost of changing it is now one line: `limHint` in `miniapp/utils/options.js`
and its twin in `public/shop.html` are the only places either word is printed.

### Customer register — under the question, in the sheet

| Case | EN | CN |
|---|---|---|
| Exactly N | `Choose 2` | `选 2 项` |
| A range | `Choose 1–2` | `选 1–2 项` |
| Ceiling only | `Up to 2` | `最多 2 项` |
| Floor only | `At least 2` | `至少 2 项` |

**COMPOSED**, all four. Built on `至少选一项` (选…项) and on `最多 ` / `至少 `,
both live across the admin and the mini app. Nothing is printed when a group has
no limit, which is every group authored before §151.

**Reused verbatim, not composed:** `请选择 ` — already the prefix of the
unanswered-question toast on both storefronts, and of `请选择地址` /
`请选择配送时间` at checkout. Under a floor of 2 or more the toast appends the
hint in brackets: `请选择 爽脆配菜（选 2 项）`.

### Operator register — the two boxes in the product editor

| Where | EN | CN |
|---|---|---|
| Around the boxes | `Pick` … | `选` … `项` |
| Fewest tooltip | `Fewest they must pick (0 = no limit)` | `至少选几项(0 = 不限)` |
| Most tooltip | `Most they can pick (0 = no limit)` | `最多选几项(0 = 不限)` |
| On the control | `0 in a box means no limit` | `填 0 表示不限` |

**`不限` is a VERBATIM LIFT** — live in the admin in `不限`, `不限量`,
`不限订单数`, `每人不限次`. It is the shop's word for *no ceiling* and it was not
re-invented here.

**`至少选几项` / `最多选几项` / `填 0 表示不限` are COMPOSED**, on the same 选…项
shape as the customer strings so the two registers do not disagree about what an
answer is called.

### Nothing was composed for the refusals

`too_few_answers` and `too_many_answers` are server backstops behind a sheet that
blocks first — a customer reaches them only by posting at the endpoint. They get
no new customer-facing string, and the storefront's existing `coErrMsg` fallback
prints the code rather than a sentence nobody wrote.

`scripts/test_option_limits.js` holds the numbers the hints are built from; the
strings themselves are asserted nowhere, which is a gap worth naming rather than
one to paper over with a snapshot test of a numeral.

## Reset to a new customer, and the word it makes you type (2026-09-16)

**§154. Eight composed strings, nine verbatim lifts.** The control is
`cuResetTestCustomer` in `public/retail-admin/08-customers.js`, posting to
`POST /api/retail-admin/customers/:id/reset-test`. The endpoint is old; the
button is new, and so is the gate in front of it.

### What the words have to carry

The endpoint archives the customer's openid by prefixing it, so their next
silent `wx.login` cannot match it and `POST /api/retail/auth` mints them a new
customer row. Their orders, points, balance and addresses stay exactly where
they are and their phone can never reach any of it again. The row is NOT
deleted — several child tables are `ON DELETE RESTRICT` — which is why
重置 is the honest verb and 删除 would be a lie.

The copy has to say three things a shorter label cannot: that the customer
comes back as somebody new, that the old record survives but is unreachable
from their phone, and that this is a testing tool. Leaving any of the three to
inference is how it gets used on a real customer once.

### The lifts

| 中文 | Lifted verbatim from |
|---|---|
| 重置 | `public/retail-admin/02-settings-ops.js` (重置密码) |
| 新顾客 | `public/retail-admin/04-orders.js`, `05-marketing.js` |
| 测试 | `public/retail-admin/02-settings-ops.js`, `04-orders.js` |
| 输入 | `public/retail-admin/02-settings-ops.js`, `04-orders.js` |
| 保留 | `public/retail-admin/01-core.js`, `02-settings-ops.js` |
| 无法 | `public/retail-admin/01-core.js`, `02-settings-ops.js` |
| 订单 / 积分 / 余额 / 地址 | throughout the admin |
| 真实顾客 | `public/retail-admin/03-dashboard.js` |
| 已重置 | built from 重置 on the live 已X shape (已退出登录, 已撤销) |

### The eight composed, by Claude, 2026-09-16

| 中文 | EN it pairs with | Note |
|---|---|---|
| 重置为新顾客 | Reset to a new customer | the menu label and the confirm button; 重置 + 新顾客, both lifts |
| 将 N 重置为新顾客？ | Reset N to a new customer? | N is the customer's own name, which is why this is `cuT` and not a pair |
| 将其微信登录与此记录解除关联。 | Their WeChat sign-in is detached from this record. | 微信 is live everywhere; 解除关联 is the new part |
| 下次打开小程序时，将以新顾客身份进入，没有订单、积分、余额和地址。 | Next time they open the mini app they arrive as a new customer with no orders, points, balance or addresses. | 小程序 and the four nouns are lifts |
| 此记录会保留，不会删除，但其手机将无法再次访问。 | This record is kept here and is not deleted, but their phone can never reach it again. | the sentence that stops 重置 reading as 删除 |
| 仅用于在自己手机上测试注册流程，请勿用于真实顾客。 | For testing signup on your own phone. Not for a real customer. | 测试 and 真实顾客 are lifts |
| 输入 RESET 以继续 | Type RESET to continue | the typed gate; see below |
| 该顾客已重置，正等待以新顾客身份进入。 | That customer has already been reset — they are waiting to arrive as a new customer. | the `already_reset` row in `01-core.js`, joining the live `already_*` family |

Two toasts are composed from parts already in this document: 已重置，下次进入将是新顾客
and the same with §152's 已撤销 N 个会话 spliced in, because a reset revokes
the customer's tokens on its way through and the count is worth the same as it
was there.

### What it says once it has run, and when it refuses

Recorded verbatim so the guard in `scripts/test_reset_test_control.js` can
insist that EVERY Chinese sentence the control ships is accounted for, rather
than only the ones a list happens to name.

| 中文 | EN it pairs with | Where |
|---|---|---|
| 已重置，已撤销 N 个会话，下次进入将是新顾客 | Reset — N sessions revoked, they arrive as a new customer next time | success toast; 已撤销 N 个会话 is §152's, spliced in |
| 已重置，下次进入将是新顾客 | Reset — they arrive as a new customer next time | the same toast when there were no sessions to revoke |
| 该顾客已重置。 | That customer has already been reset. | the client's own refusal, before the round trip |
| 重置失败 | Reset failed | `errLabelCn`, the shape every write in this file uses |
| 已重置 | already reset | the spent-state hint on the menu item |
| 测试 | testing | the hint on the menu item before it is used; 测试 is a lift |

**Rejected:** 删除顾客 — shorter, and what an operator might expect the
control to do. Refused because it is false: nothing is deleted, and an operator
who believes it is will not go looking for the record afterwards.
清空 — same objection, and it reads as emptying the row rather than
detaching the sign-in from it.

### 输入 RESET 以继续 — the first caller of a gate that was never used

`askConfirm` in `public/tacet-dialogs.js` has documented a `matchWord`
typed-confirmation gate since it was written, and until this control **nothing
had ever passed one**. Its label was therefore still English-only — it had
never been printed at an operator reading Chinese, so nobody had seen it.

It is now a pair the page's own walker splits, exactly as `pick()` beside it
relies on. That puts one constraint on every future caller, and it is recorded
in the function's API header: **`matchWord` must be ASCII and must not contain
`' · '`.** The word is repeated on BOTH sides of the label, so a Chinese
`matchWord` would put Chinese on the English side and `processNode` would
refuse the split, printing both languages at once. `RESET` is the only
caller so far.

### The capability moved first

`reset-test` matched none of the words in `lib/cap-routes.js`'s
`(block|unblock|logout|force-logout)` rule, so it answered `customers:edit` —
the weakest customer capability, the one most staff hold — for the most
destructive route in the file. That was survivable only while no button
existed. It now answers `customers:block`, and the menu item carries a matching
`data-cap` so it is not offered to somebody the server will only refuse.

This is deliberately NOT the same call as the affiliate-block gap pinned in
`scripts/test_capability_gaps.js` §4, which was left alone because narrowing it
would take a live tool away from operators who use it today. This route had no
users to take it from.

## The bind page offers a phone, optionally (2026-09-16)

The bind-profile page (`miniapp/pages/profile`) had a WeChat phone button behind
a `showPhone: false` flag and a handler that toasted *Coming soon*. The flag's
own comment gave the reason — *needs real AppID* — and that reason has expired:
the same button has been live on `pages/me` and `pages/wallet` against the real
AppID for months. The flag is removed and the handler now does what those two
pages do. Register: **CUSTOMER** (Rule 1) — the mini app.

**Why it belongs on this screen and the address did not.** WeChat's phone sheet
shows masked numbers and three buttons; a number is a number in any language. Its
region picker shows a province list that exists only in Chinese. The first is
readable by a customer who reads no Chinese, the second is not — which is the
whole difference, and the reason this field is added to the screen the address
was removed from. Binding also claims OUT1N migration credit and reconciles the
signup voucher, so a returning customer's balance and points appear before they
have bought anything.

### Reused verbatim — seven pairs, all from `pages/me`

The bind outcome is the same event on both screens, so it gets one sentence.

| Chinese | English | Lifted from |
|---|---|---|
| `已取消` | Cancelled | `me.js` `restoreDenied` |
| `已完成!` | All set! | `me.js` `restoreDone` |
| `找回失败,请重试` | Couldn't restore — please try again | `me.js` `restoreFailed` |
| `欢迎回来!` | Welcome back! | `me.js` `restoreWelcome` |
| `好的` | Great | `me.js` `restoreGotIt` |
| `已为您找回 ` | We've restored | `me.js` `restoredLead` |
| `。` | to your wallet. | `me.js` `restoredTail` |

The field hint is an eighth lift: `以前在我们这买过?` / *Shopped with us before?*
is `me.js`'s `restoreTitle`, which already asks exactly the question this hint
needs to ask. It replaces `可选，上线后可用。` (*optional, available once we go
live*), whose second clause stopped being true the moment the handler was wired.

### Composed — declared, by Claude Opus 5, under Rule 4's 2026-09-06 delegation

| Chinese | English | Why it is here |
|---|---|---|
| `手机号 (可选)` | PHONE (OPTIONAL) | The field label. **Both pieces are live and so is the shape:** `手机号` is this page's own existing label, and `<thing> (可选)` is how the mini app already marks an optional field — `楼层 / 单元 / 公寓 (可选)` and `给厨房的备注 (可选)` in `checkout.js`, `说明一下情况 (可选)` in `order.js`. What is composed is only the join. It carries the optionality that moved off the hint when the hint was replaced, and it carries it on the label, where a customer deciding whether to tap sees it. **Rejected:** leaving `手机号` bare and putting the optional marker back in the hint — the hint is one line and it is now doing the more useful job of saying why to bind at all. **Rejected:** `手机号（选填）` — `选填` does not appear anywhere in this mini app, and the full-width brackets do not match the three live examples above |

### Not done

**The phone is not part of the Save gate.** `save()` still requires a nickname and
nothing else, and the phone binds on tap rather than on Save — the same contract
as `pages/me` and `pages/wallet`. WeChat's sheet offers *Not Allowed* as one of
its three buttons and customers press it; a required field would strand every one
of them on the first screen of the app, which is the failure this page was just
edited to stop having.

## Air on a land parcel's label, marked rather than hidden (2026-09-16)

The JD label dialog (`public/retail-admin/04-orders.js`, `createJdLabel`) built a
ground parcel's radio list as `JD_SERVICES.filter(s => s.code !== 'LL-SD-M')` —
the air product was not merely un-preselected, it was absent. That made a
DEFAULT into a LOCK on the one screen where the operator knows something the
shipment plan does not. The waybill route has always honoured an explicit
product on any parcel (`const product = reqProduct || …`), so the server already
allowed what the screen was hiding. Air now appears on every ground and local
parcel's list, last. Register: **OPERATOR** (Rule 1) — the order drawer.

**What is marked, and what is not.** `planShipments` puts an item in the land
bucket for two different reasons, and only one of them is an airline rejection:
an item whose `ship_modes` is land-only is refused by the airline, while an item
that allows both modes is in land simply because land is cheaper. Marking the
second as "cannot ship air" would be false, and a warning shown on every parcel
is a warning nobody reads. So the badge and the question appear only where the
parcel actually carries a land-only item, and those items are named on the row.

**The parcel's mode is still never written.** Same rule as the Fujian / Guangxi
ground default two sections of this document above: a `land` parcel booked on
`LL-SD-M` stays a `land` parcel and only the product printed on its waybill
changes. `scripts/test_jd_label_air_option.js` holds all of it, and
`scripts/test_jd_waybill_land_default.js` still holds the mode.

### Reused verbatim — four, all already on this operator's screen

The badge and its reason are `renderPackBadges`' own, so the words the drawer
uses for "the airline will not take this" are the words the label dialog uses.
Nothing new is minted to say a thing the screen already says.

| Chinese | English | Lifted from |
|---|---|---|
| `仅陆运` | Land only | `04-orders.js` `renderPackBadges`, the land-only pack badge |
| `仅陆运 — 航空拒运` | Land shipping only — airline rejected | `04-orders.js` `renderPackBadges`, that badge's `title` — now the row's visible reason line and the confirmation's title |
| `航空拒运` | airline rejected | the right half of the pair above, standing alone in the confirmation body after the item names |
| `暂不` | Not yet | `04-orders.js` `transition()`, the early-dispatch confirmation's `cancelCn` — the same shape of question, declined |

`生鲜 · 空运` and `生鲜特快 · LL-SD-M` are not lifts: they are the `JD_SERVICES`
row's own labels, unchanged, and were always there. The dialog's subtitle stays
`陆运包裹 — 生鲜(冰袋)还是普通?` — fresh vs ambient is still the question being put,
and air is still the exception rather than a third answer to it.

### Composed — declared, by Claude Opus 5, under Rule 4's 2026-09-06 delegation

| Chinese | English | Why it is here |
|---|---|---|
| `仍要生成空运运单吗?` | Create the air waybill anyway? | The confirmation's body question. **The shape is live in this same file:** `仍要标记已发货吗?` ends both of `earlyDispatchWarning`'s bodies, and `仍要删除吗？` is in `07-content-billing.js` — `仍要` + verb + `吗?` is how this admin asks "do it anyway". The verb is the dialog's own `生成` (from `生成京东运单` and `生成并发货`), and `空运运单` is `空运` (live, `空运包裹`) on `运单` (live, `修改运单`). What is composed is the join. **Rejected:** `仍要空运发货吗?` — it says ship by air, and this books a waybill; the parcel's own mode is untouched and the words must not claim otherwise |
| `仍要生成空运运单` | Create air waybill anyway | The confirm button, the same string without `吗?`. **That pairing is live:** `transition()` puts `仍要标记已发货吗?` in the body and `仍要标记已发货` on the button, and this follows it exactly. **Rejected:** reusing `生成并发货` (the dialog's own Create & ship) — on the override it would read as the ordinary action and lose that this button is the exception being taken |

### Not done

**Nothing is written to the parcel, and nothing is logged as an override.** The
activity log records `jd_waybill_created` with the product, which is enough to
see afterwards that a land parcel went out on `LL-SD-M`, but not that the
operator was warned first. Recording the override would need the server to read
the parcel's item ship modes, which is a change to the route rather than to the
screen. Left out deliberately, and noted here so the gap is on the record rather
than discovered later.

## The parcel asks, not the product (2026-09-16)

Supersedes the trigger described one section above. That section is left as
written, per this document's convention for a superseded note — its vocabulary
still stands, only the condition that puts it on screen has changed.

**What was wrong with it.** v1.102 warned only where an item carried a land-only
`ship_modes` flag. `planShipments` puts an item in the land bucket for two
reasons and that flag is only one of them, so the ORDINARY case — a parcel the
system planned as land, holding goods nobody has flagged — booked air in
silence. The only way to be warned about a parcel was to go and edit a product,
which is a catalogue change made to get a dialog to behave. Ash, asked directly:
*"i dont want any change on the product."*

**The ruling (Ash, 2026-09-16).** The parcel already says it is a land shipment,
and that is the whole condition. Every land and local parcel asks when air is
picked. The one list where air is chosen without a question is an air parcel's
own — the Fujian / Guangxi ground-default list — where air is what the plan
asked for. In code this is `!isAir` and nothing else. Register: **OPERATOR**
(Rule 1) — the order drawer.

**And the moment is the click.** Also Ash's, chosen against the alternative of
asking at Create & ship: the question lands the instant the air row is chosen.
Declining restores the row the operator was on, so backing out of a mis-click
costs nothing, and there is deliberately no second question on submit — two
dialogs for one decision is how an operator learns to click through both
without reading either.

**The badge on the row is NOT the trigger and stays.** *Land only — airline
rejected* says the airline REFUSES these goods, which is a stronger and
different claim from *the plan said land*. It keeps naming the flagged items on
the air row, as extra information beside the question rather than the cause of
it.

### Reused verbatim — four, and no new Chinese at all

| Chinese | English | Lifted from |
|---|---|---|
| `仅陆运` | Land only | `04-orders.js` `renderPackBadges` — now the question's TITLE, and its predicate in both bodies |
| `此包裹` | This parcel | `04-orders.js` `cancelJdPickup`, `拦截此包裹?` — the subject of the fallback body |
| `仍要生成空运运单吗?` | Create the air waybill anyway? | locked in v1.102 above, unchanged |
| `暂不` | Not yet | `04-orders.js` `transition()`, the early-dispatch `cancelCn` |

The button keeps v1.102's `仍要生成空运运单` / *Create air waybill anyway*.
The English `land shipping only` is the left half of the live pair
`Land shipping only — airline rejected`, carried without the second clause
because the second clause would now be false on most parcels that see it.

The two sentences, in full:

| | Chinese | English |
|---|---|---|
| items known | `<names> 仅陆运。仍要生成空运运单吗?` | `<names> — land shipping only. Create the air waybill anyway?` |
| items not loaded | `此包裹仅陆运。仍要生成空运运单吗?` | `This parcel is land shipping only. Create the air waybill anyway?` |

Composed: nothing. Both sentences are two locked pieces and a full stop, which
is the point — declaring `仍要生成空运运单吗?` properly in v1.102 is what let a
behaviour change this size ship without minting a character.

### `<names>` is the parcel's contents, capped

The question is about the parcel, so it names the parcel: every item in it,
deduped, first three, then ` +N` for the rest. The count needs no word in either
language, and a ten-line dialog is one nobody reads to the end. When the drawer
has no items yet (a dialog opened before `openOrder` lands) the fallback row
above is used, rather than a bold empty string.

### Not done

The activity log still records `jd_waybill_created` with the product but not
that a question was put — unchanged from v1.102's *Not done*, and still a route
change rather than a screen one.

## The phone can stop a line selling (2026-09-16)

**What this is.** The phone's product sheet edited a price and a per-store count
and nothing else. The three decisions that STOP a line selling — take it off the
shop, the supplier has none, what a shopper sees once the count is zero — were
desktop-only, and the sheet said so in three places. One of those places,
`Resume on desktop` on a paused supplier item, was a sentence telling the
operator to go and find a computer in order to flip a flag they were already
looking at.

Register: **OPERATOR** (Rule 1) — the phone's Products sheet.

**No control here is new vocabulary.** The switch, the command behind it and its
roll-back already existed on the catalogue CARD one screen up; this puts the
same control on the sheet as well, calling the same command on the same family
of sizes. The desktop drawer has carried it in the same position, as a tappable
state tag, since `pdRenderHeadTags`. Three surfaces, one gesture.

### Reused verbatim — five, and one truncation

| Chinese | English | Lifted from |
|---|---|---|
| `上架` | In shop | `m.html` `productCard` — the label already under this switch on the card |
| `未上架` | Hidden | `09-products.js` `pdRenderHeadTags` — the off half of the desktop's own tag |
| `供应商缺货,暂停销售` | Supplier out — pause selling | `retail-admin.html` `#pd-supplier-paused` — the desktop's label for this exact flag |
| `缺货时的处理方式` | When it runs out | `retail-admin.html` `#pd-oos-policy` `aria-label` — the desktop's own name for this setting |
| `全国售罄停售` · `广州外可预订(现订现发)` · `全国可预订(补货后发出)` | Sold out everywhere · Pre-order outside Guangzhou · Pre-order everywhere | `retail-admin.html` `#pd-oos-policy` options, already locked in this document |

The English `When it runs out` is a SHORTER label over the live Chinese; the
desktop's own English for it (`What happens when this product is out of stock`)
wraps to three lines in a 390px row. No Chinese is composed by that — the pair's
Chinese half is the desktop's, unchanged.

**The truncation.** `供应商已暂停 — 顾客无法下单。请在电脑端恢复。` loses its last
sentence in both languages and becomes `供应商已暂停 — 顾客无法下单。` /
*Supplier paused — customers cannot order it.* The cut is at the full stop and
takes the same sentence out of both halves. What is dropped is the instruction to
go to a desktop, which the switch beside it has just made false; what stays is
the consequence of being paused, which `test_mobile_products` asserts and is
right to.

### Composed — one

| Chinese | English | Built from | Rejected |
|---|---|---|---|
| `快改上架、库存与价格 · 完整编辑在电脑端` | Quick-edit selling, stock and price · full editing on desktop | the live note bar `快改库存与价格 · 完整编辑在电脑端` with the live `上架` inserted at the head of its list | Leaving the bar as it was — it would now understate the sheet by one control, and a note bar that lists what you can change here is the one line in the sheet that must not be short. Also rejected: `销售` for the inserted term, which names selling in general rather than the shelf state this switch sets. |

Composed by Claude under the 2026-09-06 amendment. `上架` is the term this app
already uses for the state the switch controls, on the card directly above this
sheet, so the list reads in the same word the operator just tapped.

### Values, not words

The picker offers the three policies the desktop's select offers and no others.
The PATCH route accepts a fourth, legacy `always`, which that select has never
shown — a phone able to set a value the desktop cannot display is a value nobody
can undo from a desktop.

### Not done

The catalogue card shows no mark for a pre-order policy, so a line selling on
back-order looks like any other line in the list until it is opened. That is a
card change, not a vocabulary one.

## One switch is enough (2026-09-16)

Supersedes the shop-switch half of the section above. That section is left as
written, per this document's convention for a superseded note — its other two
controls, the supplier flag and the out-of-stock policy, are untouched and still
ship.

**The ruling (Ash, 2026-09-16).** *"in shop no need 2 of them. the one outside
the product edit page is enough."* The sheet opens from a card that already
carries the switch, a thumb's width above it. Putting a second one in the
sheet's head did not make the decision faster; it made the same fact answerable
in two places on one screen, which is the exact duplication the catalogue card's
own note argued against when it dropped the Live pill from beside its switch.

**What comes out.** `sheetShopStrip` and its call. With it goes the reason the
pill beside the sheet's title had been narrowed to Low / Out / Paused, so the
pill draws for every state again: a hidden product opened in the sheet has to
say so somewhere, and with the switch gone the pill is the only thing left that
can.

### Withdrawn — one composed string

| Chinese | English | Status |
|---|---|---|
| `快改上架、库存与价格 · 完整编辑在电脑端` | Quick-edit selling, stock and price · full editing on desktop | **WITHDRAWN.** Never reached production in a released build. The note bar reverts to the locked `快改库存与价格 · 完整编辑在电脑端`. |

The bar named selling because the switch was in the sheet. What is left there is
a stock policy and a supplier flag, and the locked sentence already covers the
first; a second composition to name the second would be minting a character to
describe a control the operator can see. v1.104's composed count is therefore
zero, not one — this document's running total moves DOWN.

### Withdrawn — two lifts

`上架` / *In shop* and `未上架` / *Hidden* are no longer used by the sheet. Both
remain live where they were lifted from — the catalogue card and the desktop
drawer's own state tag — and are unaffected as entries; only this section's
claim on them is released.

### Unchanged

`供应商缺货,暂停销售`, `缺货时的处理方式`, the three out-of-stock policy pairs
and the truncation of `供应商已暂停 — 顾客无法下单。` all stand exactly as
v1.104 recorded them. Those controls exist nowhere else on the phone, which is
the test this one failed.

## The voting page, rebuilt — the admin's Voting tab (2026-09-17)

The retail-admin Voting tab was rebuilt from scratch against the C · Stage
mockup Ash picked from three directions. Register: **OPERATOR** (Rule 1) —
every string here is read by whoever runs the shop, in the admin. The one
exception is the phone preview inside the editor, which draws the CUSTOMER
register's words (`你说了算`, the intro tagline) because it is a picture of the
mini app, not a screen of its own; it mints nothing.

**96 pairs, 38 lifted whole and 58 composed** under Rule 4's standing
delegation, composed by Claude (Opus 5) on 2026-09-17.

**The ratio is the point, and it is the other way round from how it looks.**
Every noun and every verb on this page is lifted — 投票, 草稿, 进行中, 已结束,
精选, 发布, 关闭, 重新开启, 编辑, 删除, 排序, 获胜选项, 你说了算. **Nothing that
names a thing or an action was composed.** What was composed is almost entirely
*consequence copy*: the sentence beside a control saying what will happen if you
use it. There was no live source for those because **the old page did not have
them** — it carried one dense bilingual paragraph per region and let the rest of
the screen go unexplained, so there was nothing to copy from. Composing them is
what the rebuild is; leaving them out would have shipped an English-only
explanation of a Chinese screen.

**Three word choices were contested, and the rejected candidate is recorded so
the search is not repeated:**

- **Open (a poll's state) → `进行中`, not `开启`.** `开启` is live in this very
  file as the *button* that opens a poll — a verb the operator presses. The pill
  beside a question is a state, not an action, and using one word for both makes
  the pill read as a control. `进行中` is already how `lib/eleme-browser.js` and
  the mini app say *running*.
- **Closed → `已结束`, not `已关闭`.** `已关闭` reads as *switched off* — what
  you do to a shop or a channel. A poll that closes is *finished*, with a result
  to show, and `已结束` is what the mini app's own block renderer already calls
  it. The verb stays `关闭`, which is correct for the action.
- **Featured → `精选`, kept from the old page.** `推荐` (recommended) was
  considered and dropped: the merchant is not recommending the poll to anyone,
  they are choosing which one sits on top.

**One numeral rule, applied throughout.** Chinese counts take a measure word and
the English plural has none, so `N votes` is `N 票`, `N options` is `N 个选项`,
`N drafts` is `N 个草稿`. English keeps its `${n === 1 ? '' : 's'}`; Chinese
never needs it, and no string was bent to make the two symmetrical.

**Ash should correct anything that reads wrong to a Chinese operator; nothing in
the code depends on the exact phrasing.**

## The voting page, said plainly — an amendment (2026-09-17)

Ash, on the page shipped hours earlier: *"i want you to review the explnation
of each feature, it need to be super clear for the tenant."* This is that pass.
It replaces twelve operator-facing explanations and the three headings above
them; it locks **12 new composed pairs** and retires the same number. Register:
OPERATOR (Rule 1). Composed by Claude (Opus 5), 2026-09-17.

**WHAT WAS WRONG WITH THE OLD ONES, since every one of them was already
grammatical Chinese and grammatical English.** They described the *control*
instead of the *consequence*. `向顾客显示为剩余天数` says what the field is; it
does not say the thing an operator actually needs to know, which is that setting
a date closes nothing and the poll keeps taking votes until they press the
button. The rewrite states the consequence first and the mechanism second, in
both languages, and where a control can be misread as doing something it does
not do, the sentence says so outright.

**THE DISPLAY FLOOR GETS THE LONGEST SENTENCE ON THE PAGE, deliberately.**
It is the one control here that changes what a customer believes, and its old
caption (*"所有选项按同一比例放大"*) explained the arithmetic without ever saying
that the operator's own screen keeps showing the real number. Both halves now
say it twice — once about the customer, once about this page.

**RICH COPY IS NOT A PAIR — see `docs/tacet-consolidation-decisions.md` §155.**
The two settings hints carry `<code>{pts}</code>` inside them, so they are no
longer written as `English · 中文`; they are two blocks, one per language,
marked `data-lang-block`. That is a mechanism change, not a vocabulary one, but
it is recorded here too because it is why those two strings appear in this
document as prose rather than as table rows.
