| The option customers see marked as the winner under past results. Leave it alone and whichever option took the most votes is marked. | 顾客在“往期结果”中看到的获胜选项。留空则票数最高的选项自动标为获胜。 |
| Customers see at least this many votes. If real turnout is lower, every option is multiplied by the same number — the leader and every percentage stay exactly as they are, only the size of the numbers changes. 0 shows the real count. This page always shows you the real count. | 顾客至少会看到这么多票。若真实票数低于此值,所有选项按同一倍数放大 —— 领先项与每个百分比完全不变,变的只是数字的大小。填 0 即显示真实票数。本页始终向你显示真实票数。 |

**Two more headings changed in `public/retail-admin.html` and are locked with
them:** *The line under “Your say” · “你说了算”下方的一行字* (was *Line under the
headline · 标题下方的文字*) and *The numbers customers see · 顾客看到的数字* (was
*Vote counts · 票数*). The page-settings description became *Two settings for the
whole Your say page in the mini app — not for one poll. · 小程序“你说了算”整页的两项设置
—— 不针对单个投票。*, because an operator opening a card headed only *Page
settings* has no way to tell which page it means.

**Ash should correct anything that reads wrong to a Chinese operator; nothing
in the code depends on the exact phrasing.**
## Drafting the empty sections (2026-09-17)

**What this is.** §156. The product page editor's auto-fill fired once, in
silence, and only onto a page with nothing on it; its failures said nothing and
could not be retried. It becomes a named control the office can press —
*Draft the empty sections* — which reads the product's own description and
writes ONLY into sections that are still blank. A section carrying words is
never touched.

Register: **OPERATOR** (Rule 1) — the Product Page Editor's Shortcuts group.

**The verb is the decision, not decoration.** Every live string in this area
says `生成` — *generate*: `正在根据厨房描述生成板块…`, `已生成 N 个板块`,
`生成失败`. That word belongs to the pass §156 is retiring, the one that built a
page and REPLACED whatever was there. A control whose entire promise is *your
words are safe, I only fill the gaps* cannot be labelled with the verb for the
thing that overwrote them. So one word is minted and the feature is built on it.

### Composed — one word, six phrases

| Chinese | English | Built from | Rejected |
|---|---|---|---|
| `补全` | draft / fill in what is missing | minted | `生成` — live and everywhere in this feature, and wrong here: it is the word for the pass that replaced the page. `填充` — live in the products sheet as `智能填充`, but that names filling BOXES from text, a different gesture on a different screen |
| `补全空白板块` | Draft the empty sections | `补全` + the live `板块` | `生成空白板块` — lifts the live `生成板块` whole, and reads as *create a blank section*: the opposite of what the button does |
| `正在补全空白板块…` | Drafting the empty sections… | the live shape `正在根据厨房描述生成板块…` with the verb swapped and the source dropped | naming the source — it is no longer only the kitchen's description |
| `已补全 N 个板块` | Drafted N sections | the live `已生成 N 个板块` with the verb swapped; `个板块` is the editor's own live counter | `已生成` — see above |
| `已补全 ✓` | Drafted ✓ | `补全` + the live `✓` convention | `完成 ✓` — says the job finished, not that the gaps were filled |
| `没有空白板块需要补全` | Every section already has words | `补全` + the live `板块` | `所有板块都已填写` — true, and answers a question nobody asked; the operator pressed a button and wants to know why nothing moved |
| `没有可补全的板块` | Nothing to add | as above, shortened for the progress card | — |

Composed by Claude under the 2026-09-06 amendment. The tooltip
`根据本产品描述补全仍为空白的板块` is the same word in a sentence and is not
counted separately.

### Reused verbatim — six

| Chinese | English | Lifted from |
|---|---|---|
| `请先填写描述` | Add a description to this product first | `09-products.js` `pdAutoComposeBlocks` — `请先填写描述,再开启设计`, cut at the comma. What is dropped is *then turn on the design*, which is the other screen's next step and false in the editor |
| `生成失败` | Couldn't draft | `09-products.js` — the live failure word for this exact call, seven live uses |
| `请重试` | try again | the admin's standing retry line, ninety-two live uses |
| `个板块` | N sections | `product-editor.html` — its own live counter, `已添加 N 个板块 ✓` |
| `— 请审阅后保存 ✓` | — review and save ✓ | `product-editor.html` — the tail of the auto-fill toast this replaces, unchanged |
| `(已按品牌语气替你撰写中文,请校对)` · `(已按品牌语气替你撰写英文,请校对)` | drafted the 中文/English in the brand voice — give it a read | `product-editor.html` — carried over verbatim from the function being replaced |

**`生成失败` is kept although `补全` was minted**, and the inconsistency is
deliberate. The compose call is what failed, the same call the products sheet
makes and reports in those words; an operator who sees the sheet's failure and
the editor's failure should read one word for one event. The mint names the new
promise, not the old machinery.

### Not done

The five *Product details* boxes keep their own labels on the products sheet.
§156's third PR derives the plain description from the sections and retires
those boxes as a separate screen; whatever vocabulary that needs is a later
release, not this one.


## The colophon's line moves house (2026-09-17)

**What this is.** §158. No new Chinese, and the entry exists to say where an
existing pair now lives.

Register: **CUSTOMER** (Rule 1) — the mini app's product page sign-off.

`从土壤到灵魂` / *From Soil to Soul*, and the name `Roots · ESTD 2017` beside it,
were literals in `miniapp/pages/product/product.js`. That codebase is published
**once per tenant**, each under its own WeChat AppID, so the pair was being
printed onto every merchant's shopper-facing product page. It now comes from
`lib/brand-signature.js`, served per tenant on the storefront config, and a
tenant that is not Roots is served **an empty line rather than a translated
one** — a motto is not something a platform can compose on a merchant's behalf,
and Rule 4 has nothing to say about a string that must not exist.

### Nothing minted, nothing re-lifted

| Chinese | English | What happened to it |
|---|---|---|
| `从土壤到灵魂` | From Soil to Soul | moved, unchanged, from the mini-app page into `lib/brand-signature.js`; still Roots' locked pair, now served rather than compiled |

The English half of the colophon's name line carries no Chinese and never did —
`Roots · ESTD 2017` is a mark, not a sentence, and it is now a per-tenant value
rather than a literal.

### Not done

The product editor's own colophon strings were dealt with in v1.106 under §156.
Nothing in the storage box moved; §147's three lists keep their own vocabulary
until the PR that counts them per tenant.


## The storage box's vocabulary leaves the codebase (2026-09-17)

**What this is.** §159. No new Chinese, and no Chinese withdrawn. The entry
exists to say that a body of locked vocabulary is no longer in any file.

Register: **OPERATOR** (Rule 1) — the Product Page Editor's storage box.

§147 locked four lists here: the icons, the two card heads, the two unit tails
and the notes, each chosen by *most-used wins* over 218 live storage boxes, each
with its losing twin recorded rather than dropped. They were constants in
`public/product-editor.html`, which on a platform meant every tenant was offered
a Guangzhou vegan grocer's storage vocabulary.

They are counted per tenant now. **Every pair §147 locked is still Roots', still
offered to Roots, and still spelled the way §147 decided** — reproduced from the
products those decisions were measured against, which a test asserts. What
changed is only where the answer is read from.

### Nothing minted, nothing re-lifted, nothing withdrawn

| Was | Now |
|---|---|
| `PA_STORAGE_ICONS`, `PA_STORAGE_HEADS`, `PA_STORAGE_UNITS`, `PA_STORAGE_NOTES` in `public/product-editor.html` | counted from this tenant's own `storage_box` blocks by `GET /api/retail-admin/products/storage-usage` |

### The one rule that had to survive the move

§147's whole reason for existing was that **❄ and ❄️ look identical and are
not**. A count keyed on the exact string would have offered both and re-opened
the split. The counter folds U+FE0F before tallying and returns the commoner
spelling, so the vocabulary decision §147 made is enforced by arithmetic now
instead of by a list somebody maintains.

### Not done

`PA_TEMPLATES` and the editor's preview samples still carry food wording. Those
are starting designs an operator picks and previews, not offered answers, and
their vocabulary is a later question.

## A copied page says where it came from (2026-09-17)

Rule 4's fourth amendment delegates composing to whoever is building, and
forbids composing *silently*. This section is that declaration. **One pair is
composed here. Nothing already locked is re-worded, re-lifted or retired.**

**What it is for.** Duplicating a product copies `description_blocks` — the
whole designed page — along with everything else. That is correct and stays: a
500g/1kg size variant wants the same page, and that is most of what duplicate is
for. What was missing is that the copy had no way to SAY so. Nothing in the
classic product form writes to `description_blocks`, so a copy whose name, price
and description had all been changed still opened the Product editor holding the
original's words, with no mark anywhere and nothing on screen to explain it.
Reported by Ash, 2026-09-17. §161 records the decision.

| Key | EN | CN | Source | What it says |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `designFromProduct` | Designed page came from *name* | 设计页来自 *name* | **COMPOSED by Claude, 2026-09-17**, from two declared parts — see below. **Ash should correct this if it reads wrong.** | The warning bar above the description, when the product's sections were copied from another product and nothing has yet written over them. The Rebuild button beside it regenerates them from this product's own description, which clears the mark. |

### Where both halves come from

- **`设计页`** — lifted verbatim from the same bar's existing line, `Designed
  page is out of date — content changed since it was built · 设计页已过期 —
  内容已更改`, live in `public/retail-admin.html`. The two lines share a bar and
  now share a subject, which is the point: an operator reading one and then the
  other should not have to work out that they are about the same thing.
- **`来自`** — lifted from `From the warehouse · 来自仓库`, locked in *Stock
  movement — picking and receiving* (2026-09-03), Ash's own words.
- **The product's name carries itself.** Per the standing preference, a name
  often needs no label, and it is printed in whichever language the reader is
  already in.

### Live candidates considered and rejected

- **`设计页已过期 — 内容已更改` reused as-is** (the bar's existing line, no new
  Chinese at all). Rejected: its second clause is *false* for a copy. Nothing
  changed — the page was never this product's. An operator who had just
  duplicated something and typed nothing would read "content changed" and go
  looking for the change they had not made.
- **`已复制` / `副本`** (copy, duplicate), live around the duplicate control.
  Rejected: they describe the PRODUCT as a copy, which is not what is being
  said. The product is a new product; it is the page that was copied. Using
  them here would mislabel the thing the bar is about.
- **Composing a second clause on the model of the existing one** (e.g. "— 该页
  属于另一商品"). Rejected under the standing preference for restructuring a
  label over minting: naming the source product says the same thing with a word
  the operator can act on, and needs one lifted term instead of a new clause.

### The compact row keeps the locked pair

The dock's Shopper page summary still reads `Designed page — out of date ·
已设计页面 — 已过期` for both reasons. That row is a status, not an
explanation, and a copied page IS out of date in the sense it means. The bar
below it is where the two reasons are told apart and the source is named.

## A required choice is part of what the product costs (2026-09-17)

Rule 4's fourth amendment delegates composing to whoever is building, and
forbids composing *silently*. This section is that declaration. **Two pairs are
composed here. Nothing already locked is re-worded, re-lifted or retired.**

**What it is for.** Ash, 2026-09-17, on Beet Salad: the customer picks one of
two dressings and staff had put BOTH into the recipe, so every unit carried the
cost of a dressing it never shipped — ¥11.43 where the true figure is ¥8.31 or
¥9.33, on every sale and not merely on the wasted ones. The fix is to model the
choice as a choice: the recipe holds the salad without dressing and each
dressing becomes a stock-linked option choice. That moves the dressing's cost
OUT of the recipe, and the margin in the product editor would then read *better*
than the product really is — the one direction that gets a price cut. So the
editor's margin now adds what the product's REQUIRED choices cost, and needs two
lines the tree did not have: one naming that addition, and one for when a
required choice cannot be priced at all.

| Key | EN | CN | Source | What it says |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `marginInclRequiredChoice` | incl. *¥X* required choice | 含必选项 *¥X* | **COMPOSED by Claude, 2026-09-17**, from three live parts — see below. **Ash should correct this if it reads wrong.** | The trailing clause on the product editor's margin line, when a required choice adds to what the product costs us. Absent entirely when it adds nothing, which is nearly every product. |
| `marginRequiredChoiceUncosted` | Margin — a required choice has no cost yet | 毛利 — 必选项暂无成本 | **COMPOSED by Claude, 2026-09-17**, from three live parts — see below. **Ash should correct this if it reads wrong.** | Replaces the margin line outright when a required group holds a stock-linked choice nobody has priced. The floor is then unknown rather than free, and a margin drawn without it would be confidently too good. |

### Where every half comes from

- **`毛利`** — live in this very file at `09-products.js:1134`, and already
  declared as a lifted term in `21-product-build.js`'s header, which names it
  among the seven labels copied from live markup rather than retyped.
- **`必选`** — lifted verbatim from the checkbox that MAKES a group required, on
  this same screen: `Required · 必选` (`09-products.js`, the options editor).
  The word the operator ticks is the word the margin line answers with.
- **`选项`** — live on the same options editor, in `Remove this option ·
  移除此选项`, and in `10-extras.js`'s `个选项`. **`必选项` is the only thing
  assembled here**, out of those two sharing their `选` — the ordinary
  contraction, not a coinage.
- **`暂无成本`** — lifted verbatim from the waste screen's own words for exactly
  this fact, `No cost · 暂无成本` (`20-waste.js`, both the totals strip and the
  row). A product nobody has priced reads the same way in both places, which is
  the point: one vocabulary for one fact.
- **`含`** — live in `03-dashboard.js` as `(含其中的最近使用)`, "including the
  recent-use list among them". Same sense, same function word.
- **The money carries itself.** Per the standing preference, a numeral often
  needs no label, and `paMoney` prints it in the tenant's own currency.

### Live candidates considered and rejected

- **`必填`** (required, of a form field), the commoner word in Chinese UI.
  Rejected: it is not live anywhere in this tree, and the screen has already
  chosen `必选` on the control that sets the very flag being read. Two words for
  one flag on one screen is the drift Rule 4 exists to stop.
- **`无成本`**, live in `21-product-build.js` for an unpriced build line.
  Rejected in favour of the waste screen's `暂无成本` for the same fact: the
  build-list line describes a material that may never have a cost, while `暂`
  ("as yet") is what the margin means — somebody can go and price it, and the
  English half says *yet* for the same reason.
- **`加价`** (price increase), live around option `price_delta`. Rejected, and
  it is the trap worth naming: `price_delta` is what the CUSTOMER pays extra and
  this line is what WE pay. Borrowing the price word for a cost would put the
  two figures under one label on a screen that shows both.
- **Saying nothing and folding the figure in silently.** Rejected: a margin
  computed on a cost the operator cannot see in the Cost field beside it is a
  number they cannot check, and an unexplained difference is how a correct
  figure gets overridden by a wrong one.

## The alerts say what happened, not what the code did (2026-09-17)

§162. Ash, reading the system-alert group on a phone — *"I don't understand what
this message means."* The alert was this, and it is the ordinary one, not an edge
case:

```
🟠 饿了么 tianhe 订单 EL20260917997276:1 项库存不足,已按 0 计:Falafel Wrap 需要 1,剩余 0。请检查
Eleme tianhe order EL20260917997276 — 1 line(s) exceeded on-hand and were clamped at 0: Falafel Wrap wanted 1, had 0. Please check.
```

**The 中文 half was fine. The English half was the failure**, and the group's own
auto-translate proved it: WeCom rendered `库存不足,已按 0 计` as *"1 item out of
stock, counted as 0"*, which is what happened. Our English said `line(s)`
(a row in a platform payload), `on-hand` (a column name), `clamped` (what the
ingest did to it) and `(s)` (a programmer declining to pick a number) — four
pieces of our own vocabulary in nine words. **The machine translation of our
Chinese was clearer than the English we wrote ourselves.**

So this section locks no new Chinese and changes almost no Chinese at all. What
it changes is the register of the ENGLISH half, across every merchant-facing
alert: `lib/channel-line-report.js`, `scripts/proxy-watchdog.js` and the five
daemons. Say what happened to the shelf or the shop, then say the one thing to
go and do. The count is already carried by the 中文 half and by the names
themselves, so the English no longer repeats it and can never disagree with it.

Worth recording for whoever writes the next alert: **the reader reads the 中文
half too, translated.** A parenthetical of Latin identifiers inside a Chinese
sentence does not disappear on the way through WeCom — it arrives, untranslated
and unexplained, in the middle of an English sentence.

### Subtracted — declared, by Claude Opus 5, under Rule 4's 2026-09-06 delegation

| Before (live) | After | Why |
|---|---|---|
| `掉了门店绑定（acctId/区域丢失）` | `掉了门店绑定` | The Meituan store-binding alert (`scripts/meituan-daemon.js`). `掉了门店绑定` already states the fact completely; the parenthetical names the two internal fields whose loss we detect it by, which is a debugging note that had been left in a merchant page. It survives where it belongs — the sync log and the pm2 line both still carry the reason. **Nothing is minted**: every remaining character is the live string in its original order. **Rejected:** keeping it and translating it into the English half too — that spreads the defect rather than fixing it, and no shop has ever acted on an `acctId`. **Rejected:** replacing it with a plainer Chinese gloss such as `（登录信息丢失）` — that is minting a phrase to explain a detail the sentence does not need |

### Not a Chinese change

The other four daemons, the watchdog and both stock alerts keep their 中文
halves **exactly as locked**. `库存不足,已按 0 计`, `库存未扣减`,
`库存已为负数`, `可能是数错了`, `有客户订单等待`, `请检查` and the channel and
proxy labels are all untouched. This section is an English-register decision
with one Chinese subtraction, and that asymmetry is the finding, not an
oversight.

### Not done

**The proxy watchdog still pages the merchant group on every failover and
failback.** Those are self-healing events that ask nothing of a shop, and the
alerts now say so in words (`switched itself … nothing to do`), but saying
"nothing to do" in a group meant for things to do is still the wrong channel for
them. Whether they move to the platform audience is Ash's call, not a wording
one, and it is not made here.

## Trend stops being a line and four numbers (2026-09-17)

§163. The phone's Trend screen called `/dashboard?from=…&to=…`, read one key of
the reply — `trend`, the per-day buckets — and dropped the rest of the object on
the floor. What it dropped was the whole report: the deltas against the previous
window, the payment mix, the day×hour heat, the status funnel, top products and
customers, the cohort tables, and the per-store split. The screen then printed a
note saying the per-store breakdown was on desktop. That was true of the screen
and had never been true of the response it already held.

Four sections now open under the line, one tap each. **No new endpoint and no
second request** — every figure comes out of the call the screen was already
making. The vocabulary problem this creates is that the desktop has been naming
these same figures for a year, so almost nothing here should be new.

### Lifted whole — 19 pairs, every one of them the desktop's own words

| Customers | 客户 | LIVE — `public/retail-admin/03-dashboard.js` |

| Cancelled | 已取消 | LIVE — `public/retail-admin/03-dashboard.js` |
| Given away in vouchers | 优惠券让利 | LIVE — `public/retail-admin/03-dashboard.js` |
| People bought | 购买人数 | LIVE — `public/retail-admin/03-dashboard.js` |

| Top spender | 消费最高 | LIVE — `public/retail-admin/03-dashboard.js` |

| Units sold | 销量 | LIVE — `public/retail-admin/09-products.js` |

| vs prior N days | 对比前 N 天 | LIVE — `public/retail-admin/03-dashboard.js` (the Revenue card's prior-window note; the phone spaces the numeral, which is m.html's house convention throughout) |

The ten payment-method names (`微信支付`, `支付宝`, `美团`, `积分`, `现金`,
`银行转账`, `微信(线下)`, `支付宝(线下)`, `其他`, `未知`) and the seven weekday
names (`周日` … `周六`) are lifted as whole tables from `DASH_PAY_NAMES` and
`DASH_DOW_CN` in the same file, and are not re-listed here: they are the
desktop's tables, copied, and if either is ever corrected both halves must move
together.

### Composed — 5 pairs, declared by Claude Opus 5 under Rule 4's 2026-09-06 delegation

| EN | CN | built from |
|---|---|---|
| Quietest day | 最低的一天 | The live `最高的一天` (*Busiest day*) sits two tiles away in this same screen. One character moves. **Rejected:** `最冷清的一天` — reads as a judgement on the shop rather than a reading of the line, and does not pair with the tile beside it |
| Best day of the week | 一周中最旺的一天 | `一周` and `最旺` are both ordinary; the shape follows the live `最高的一天` above it so the two weekday facts rhyme. **Rejected:** `最佳星期几` — names the question, not the answer. **Rejected:** reusing `最高的一天`, which is already taken by a different fact on the same card and would make two tiles read identically |
| Started checkout, did not pay | 进入结算但未付款 | `进入结算` is lifted verbatim from the desktop's checkout funnel (`Reached checkout · 进入结算`); `未付款` is live across the order screens. **Rejected:** `弃单` — the trade word for it, and shorter, but it is the platform's vocabulary rather than the shop's, and this screen is read by people who do not have it |
| Unnamed store | 未命名门店 | `未命名` is live (`public/retail-admin/01-core.js`), `门店` is live everywhere. It shows only when a store row comes back with neither name filled in. **Rejected:** `(未命名)` alone, the voting page's form — inside a list of store names a bare parenthetical does not read as a store |
| Hover charts, the cohort grid and the written read are on desktop. | 悬停图表、留存矩阵与文字解读在电脑端。 | Replaces the retired note below. `在电脑端` is the phone's standing formula for this sentence and is untouched; `悬停图表` and `留存矩阵` name the two things that genuinely do not fit a thumb, and `文字解读` the AI read. **Rejected:** keeping `分章` (*chapters*) — the phone now has sections of its own, so the word had stopped distinguishing the two screens |

### Retired — 1 pair

| EN | CN | why |
|---|---|---|
| Charts, chapters and the per-store breakdown are on desktop. | 图表、分章与分门店明细在电脑端。 | v1.104. The per-store breakdown is on the phone as of this commit, so the sentence made a promise the screen had stopped keeping. Retired, not re-worded: the replacement above names a different set of things |

### What the money gate does to all of this

A warehouse, dispatch or customer-service account sees none of the rows that
carry an amount — the payment mix, the voucher giveaway, the top spender, each
store's takings — and **a section left with no rows is not drawn at all**, so
there is no empty card headed *Sales* for those accounts. The share-of-revenue
row goes with them although it prints a percentage and no `¥`: it is a fact
about revenue, and the gate is about revenue rather than about the currency
sign. Nothing here is blurred, zeroed or shown-then-hidden.

### Not done

**The previous window's own curve is not drawn.** The delta pill states the
change as a number, which is what the question needs, but the dotted comparison
line behind the curve is not there: `/dashboard` returns `deltas` and
`prev_window`'s dates, not the previous window's per-day buckets, and `baseline`
is computed only for single-day windows. Drawing that line means a second
request per range change, which is the one thing this whole section was able to
avoid. It is a small follow-up if Ash wants it, and a deliberate absence until
then.

## Opening a recipe and reading what it costs (2026-09-17)

§164. `recipes:view` was the only view key in `lib/capabilities.js` carrying
`money: 'shows'`. Its justification, in MONEY_NOTES, was `A recipe is a cost. ·
食谱本身就是成本。` — and that sentence was TRUE of the screen as it stood, which
is exactly why the coin was on the wrong KEY rather than simply wrong. With
nothing else to carry it, opening Recipes at all was seeing money: the list's
Cost and Cost-per-gram columns, every ingredient line's cost, the record's
summary strip, the whole Cost & Pricing tab, the linked material's rate, the
margin beside each product, and the costs inside version history.

So a kitchen operator could not be given what a thing is made from, in what
quantities, in what order — without also being given what it all costs. Ash
asked for the cost section to be reachable only by role. `recipes:costs` is that
key, and it is modelled line for line on the `products:costs` that already does
this job one screen over.

### Composed — 2 pairs, declared by Claude Opus 5 under Rule 4's 2026-09-06 delegation

| EN | CN | built from |
|---|---|---|
| See recipe cost & margin | 查看食谱成本与毛利 | The live `products:costs` pair — `See product cost & margin · 查看产品成本与毛利` (`lib/capabilities.js`) — with its one noun swapped for the noun `recipes:view` already carries (`查看食谱`). Every character is lifted: `查看` from five sibling view keys, `食谱` from `recipes:view`, `成本与毛利` from `products:costs` untouched. **Rejected:** `成本与定价`, the record's own Cost & Pricing sub-tab pair — it names a TAB, and this key is not the tab (it also gates the list's columns, the ingredient lines and the summary strip); and `定价` claims a pricing write the key does not grant |
| Recipes show batch cost, cost per gram and margin. | 食谱显示整批成本、每克成本与毛利。 | The MONEY_NOTES sentence that justifies the coin. `显示` and the sentence shape come from `products:costs`' own note (`产品列表显示单位成本与毛利。`); `整批` and `每克成本` are the Recipes list's and the cost breakdown's own live column headings, so the note names the figures in the words the screen already prints them in. **Rejected:** reusing `食谱本身就是成本。` — it is the retired sentence below, and it argues that a recipe IS a cost, which is the premise this split withdraws |

### Retired — 1 pair

| EN | CN | why |
|---|---|---|
| A recipe is a cost. | 食谱本身就是成本。 | The justification for `recipes:view`'s money mark. The mark has moved to `recipes:costs`, and the sentence went with it re-worded, because the claim it makes — that opening a recipe is inherently seeing money — is the thing §164 stops being true. Retired, not re-pointed: the replacement above names three figures rather than asserting an identity |

### What the money gate does to the screen

Every figure goes; every count and every cause stays. The list loses its two
money columns **outright — header and cells — rather than dashing them**,
because a dash on that screen means *no figure could be worked out for this
recipe*, which is a real state the list exists to surface; reusing it for *you
may not see this* would make a priced recipe and a broken one identical on the
screen somebody is meant to fix the first from. The Cost & Pricing tab is not
drawn at all rather than drawn empty. The record's summary row of figures is not
built, but the warning pills beneath it — `N ingredients missing cost` and the
locked `UNIT MISMATCH · 单位不符` — still paint, because they carry a count and a
cause and never an amount. Hiding those is how a fault stays unfixed, which is
the call `18-materials.js` makes in the same words.

**The product's SELL PRICE stays.** It is what a customer is charged — it is on
the shopfront, the menu and the receipt — and it is not what this key withholds.
The margin beside it goes, because a margin is the sell price minus this
recipe's cost and printing the two together hands the cost back by subtraction.
Exactly the split `lib/product-cost-visibility.js` already makes.

### Not done

**The server still sends the figures.** `lib/cap-routes.js` opens every GET, so
`/api/retail-admin/recipes` returns `batchCost`, `costPerGram` and
`costPerServing` to anyone who can read the list, and this gate decides layout
rather than what is knowable. Its three siblings go further — the materials
route declines to SEND a price without the capability. §164's *what this does
not decide* records the gap deliberately: whether the API should withhold cost
fields per capability is its own decision, and answering it in passing would
gate a dozen live readers on a choice nobody made.

## Three numbers, three populations (2026-09-17)

§165. Three labels on the Trend screen described something other than the figure
under them. None of the arithmetic was wrong; all three names were.

1. **`Top seller`** sat on a list the server returns `ORDER BY revenue DESC`. Row
   one is the biggest EARNER, and its unit count can be lower than the row below
   it — a costly product shifting seventeen units outranks a cheap one shifting
   four hundred. The desktop carries the same fault twice over: its panel is
   headed `热销商品` with the meta `件数`, which together promise a volume
   ranking this has never been.
2. **`WeChat Pay share of revenue`** divided by order revenue alone. The
   wholesale ledger and top-up cash are folded into the headline total four rows
   above it and into neither side of that percentage, so the two figures could
   not be reconciled by anybody reading them together.
3. **`Revenue`, `Orders` and `Visits`** sit in one chip row and come from three
   different populations. Revenue and Orders are every channel; `retail_visits`
   has exactly one writer, the mini app's tracker, so Visits is one channel's.
   The customer figures below are keyed on a `customer_id` that
   `routes/meituan-internal.js` and `routes/eleme-internal.js` never write, so
   every marketplace buyer is invisible to them.

The fix is naming, not re-plumbing. **Not one figure changes value.** Re-sorting
the product ranking by units would change what the desktop has shown for a year;
the ranking is useful and was only ever named wrong.

### Composed — 7 pairs, declared by Claude Opus 5 under Rule 4's 2026-09-06 delegation

| EN | CN | built from |
|---|---|---|
| Top earner | 营收最高 | `营收` is live throughout (`public/retail-admin/03-dashboard.js`, `public/m.html`), `最高` from the live `最高的一天` (*Busiest day*). **Rejected:** `最赚钱` — plain, and the register is a shop's rather than a report's. **Rejected:** keeping `最热销` and re-sorting by units, which changes a year-old ranking to rescue a word |
| share of order revenue | 占订单营收 | `占订单` is live (`03-dashboard.js`, the refund-rate fact `占订单 N%`), `营收` likewise. **Rejected:** `占总营收` — the opposite of true; the total is exactly what this share does NOT divide by. **Rejected:** folding the wholesale ledger and top-ups INTO the denominator, which would invent a payment method for money that arrived without one |
| Mini app only — the marketplaces do not report visits. | 仅小程序 —— 外卖平台不提供访问数据。 | `小程序` live (`public/m.html` CHAN_META), `外卖平台` live (`02-settings-ops.js`), `访问` is the screen's own Visits label. **Rejected:** `仅微信小程序` — the tenant's channel is named `小程序` everywhere else and the platform word adds nothing |
| Mini app, web and in-store only — Meituan and Eleme orders carry no customer. | 仅小程序、网页与门店 —— 美团、饿了么订单没有顾客。 | Every noun is live in `public/m.html`'s own channel table (`小程序`, `网页`, `门店`, `美团`, `饿了么`), and the enumerating `、` follows the live line `各渠道的新订单都会出现在这里 — 小程序、网页、美团。`. **Rejected:** `没有顾客档案` — `档案` is a records-system word that appears nowhere a shop reads. **Rejected:** naming it a limitation of ours; it is the platforms', and the sentence says which ones |
| Wholesale pickups are not counted here. | 不含批发提货。 | `批发` live (`22-finance.js`, `04-orders.js`), `提货` live (`public/m.html`, *View pickups · 查看提货记录*). **Rejected:** `不含 gzgrocery` — the channel's internal name, which no shop floor uses |
| Top products · 商品排行 | (desktop panel heading) | `商品` is live throughout the admin; `排行` is live only inside `排行榜` (*Leaderboard*, `public/shop.html`) — a customer-facing file, so the bare noun is the composed part. Neutral on purpose: the heading no longer claims an ordering, and the meta beneath states it. **Rejected:** `商品排行榜` — a leaderboard is a customer-facing entertainment, and this panel is a report |
| by revenue, showing units · 按营收排序，显示件数 | (desktop panel meta) | `按…排序` follows the live `按销量` (`09-products.js`, *Set badge to Auto (by sales) · 设置标签为自动(按销量)*); `件数` is the retired meta's own word, kept because it is what the column still shows. Says both facts because the panel has both: ranked on one number, printing another |

### Retired — 4 pairs

| EN | CN | why |
|---|---|---|
| Top seller | 最热销 | v1.114 (phone) and long-standing on desktop. `热销` is a volume claim; the ranking is by revenue |
| Top products | 热销商品 | Same word, same fault, on the desktop panel heading |
| units | 件数 | Retired as a *meta* — it described the list's ordering, which it never was. The word survives inside the new meta, where it describes the column instead |
| share of revenue | 占营收 | v1.114. Named a total it does not divide by |

### Not a Chinese change

The desktop's Overview headline for this chapter moves from `${nm} 最热销（N 件）。`
to `${nm} 营收最高（¥X，N 件）。` — the retired pair above replaced by the
composed one, and the money it was ranked on added ahead of the units. No new
characters beyond `营收最高`.

### Not done

**The populations are named, not reconciled.** Meituan has given us a stable
`channel_customer_id` since 2026-09-15 and a `channel_customer_orders` count
beside it, both unread by any screen; gzgrocery carries a real unmasked phone;
Eleme's own payload has never been audited for a user id, and the raw detail it
sends us is discarded at the ingest route without being looked at. Counting
those buyers is a change to what the figures MEAN on both the phone and the
desktop, and it belongs in the decisions record before it belongs in code.

## Recipes reach the phone, read-only (2026-09-17)

§164's screen, stage one. The desktop record is four sub-tabs with autosave and
version history behind it; what crosses is the half a person standing at a bench
needs. **No money on it for anybody** — that is what §164's split was FOR, so
the phone draws the half that was being withheld and none of the other.

Almost nothing had to be minted. The desktop Recipes screen has been bilingual
since §29, so its words were already the shop's words for these things:

`食谱` · `原料` · `步骤` · `分类` · `批量大小` · `份数` · `自制` · `分装` ·
`储存方式` · `保质期` · `备注` · `冷冻` · `冷藏` · `常温` · `暂无食谱` ·
`没有匹配项` · `无` — all LIFTED WHOLE from
`public/retail-admin/19-recipes.js`, and asserted as that file's own characters
in `scripts/test_mobile_recipes.js` so a retyped variant fails rather than
passing as close enough. The note at the foot of both screens is the phone's
existing `Best edited on desktop · 建议在电脑端编辑`, untouched.

### Composed — 2 pairs, declared by Claude Opus 5 under Rule 4's 2026-09-06 delegation

| EN | CN | built from |
|---|---|---|
| Search recipes | 搜索食谱 | The phone's own `Search products · 搜索商品` (`screenLogBatch`, `public/m.html`) with its one noun swapped for the noun the desktop already carries: `商品` → `食谱`. Both characters of `搜索` and both of `食谱` are live. **Rejected:** `查找食谱` — `查找` appears nowhere on this surface, and a second word for *search* inside one app is how two screens come to feel like two apps |
| Could not load recipes. | 无法加载食谱。 | The phone's own `Could not load waste records. · 无法加载损耗记录。` (`loadWaste`, same file) with `损耗记录` → `食谱`. §1.12's sentence shape, unchanged, because it is the same failure. **Rejected:** `食谱加载失败。` — the passive reads as a system report rather than as the app saying what it could not do, and every other failure line on this surface opens with `无法` |

### What is NOT on this screen, and why no words were needed for it

The cost tab, the batch cost, the cost per gram, the margin and the per-line
rates. §164 split `recipes:costs` out of `recipes:view` so the kitchen could be
handed the ingredients and the steps **without** them; a phone that printed them
would hand back exactly what the split withheld, and to the operator the split
was made for. So this screen has no money vocabulary at all — not hidden behind
a capability, simply not part of what it is.

`scripts/test_mobile_recipes.js` asserts that as a fact about the source rather
than as a permission: twelve money field names, and the phone's money helpers,
must not appear in the two screens' code at all.

### Not done

**Stage two — making a batch from the recipe — is not built.** Scaling, an
ingredient checklist, a step-at-a-time mode and the hand-off into *Log a batch*
were drawn as option C of the mockups and are a separate decision. Nothing here
prejudges them, and none of their vocabulary is minted: a dozen pairs would be
needed and composing them ahead of the screen would lock words to a design
nobody has chosen yet.

§167, stage two of the Recipes screen. Scale the batch, gather the lines, work
the steps. **It writes nothing** — the scale and the ticks are facts about this
afternoon — so there is no save, no confirmation and no receipt to name.

Two live words joined. **Rejected:** `制作` alone — as a button it reads as a noun, *production*, rather than as an instruction to begin one. **Rejected:** `开始烹饪` (*start cooking*) — a repacked recipe is not cooked, and 27 of them back a material rather than a dish |

The mockup drew a per-step ingredient list (*"This step uses…"*) and a rest

cannot support is how a vocabulary file comes to describe an app that does not
exist; §167 records the same absence on the decisions side.

## Buyers, by the channel that can count them (2026-09-17)

§169. One figure, *People bought*, counted `DISTINCT customer_id` — a column
`routes/meituan-internal.js` and `routes/eleme-internal.js` have never written.
So it was the storefront's number wearing an all-channel label, sitting four
tiles under revenue that counts every channel. Ash, reading it on the phone:
*"people bought I see low number it can't be all channels"*.

It is three rows now, and they are **never added together**: our `customer_id`,
Meituan's `wmUserId` and a wholesale phone are three identity spaces that do not
reconcile, so a total would count the same person twice the moment they order in
two places, with no join that could detect it.

the channel name. not wholesale"*. He is right: between two rows that answer
"where from", one answering "what sort" reads as a different question.

Worth recording beside it: that date moves earlier on its own.
`routes/meituan-internal.js` backfills the column with
`COALESCE(channel_customer_id, ?)` on every re-read, so orders older than the
column fill in as the daemon sees them again — which is why a live window can
show a date well before 2026-09-15, when the column shipped. The screen reads
the date from the data rather than pinning it to that day.

## Where the order came from (2026-09-18)

Ash, on Trend's Sales card: *"sales show revenue only of mini app, why not
showing each channel revenue?"*.

It never showed only the mini app — `WeChat Pay share of order revenue` is every
channel, split by how the money ARRIVED, and Meituan and Eleme sit in that same
denominator under their own payment methods. But the question underneath it was
fair: a split by where the order CAME FROM existed nowhere for a window.
`/sales/summary` has one and is pinned to today; `/dashboard` had splits by
store, by delivery mode and by payment method, and none by channel.

### Lifted whole — 7 pairs, every one of them already this app's

| EN | CN | provenance |
|---|---|---|
| Channels | 渠道 | LIVE — `public/retail-admin.html` |
| Mini app | 小程序 | LIVE — `public/m.html`, CHAN_META |
| Web store | 网店 | LIVE — `public/m.html`, CHAN_META |
| Meituan | 美团 | LIVE — `public/m.html`, CHAN_META |
| Eleme | 饿了么 | LIVE — `public/m.html`, CHAN_META |
| gzgrocery ERP | gzgrocery | LIVE — `public/m.html`, CHAN_META |
| In store | 门店 | LIVE — `public/m.html`, CHAN_META |
| Manual | 手动 | LIVE — `public/m.html`, the Orders screen's own channel filter. NOT in CHAN_META, which carries the six channels a shop switches on and off; a typed-in order is not one of those. It is a channel HERE, because this card asks where the money came from and a typed-in order is somewhere |

The names are read out of CHAN_META by the same key the server groups on, so a
channel is named identically here and on the Sales board, and a rename in that
one table moves both. **A channel the server reports and the table does not know
prints the server's own key** rather than being dropped: an unnamed row is a
question somebody can ask, a missing one is silence.

### Composed — 1 pair

| EN | CN | built from |
|---|---|---|
| Wallet top-ups (X) are in the total above but belong to no channel. | 钱包充值（X）计入上方合计，但不属于任何渠道。 | `钱包充值` is live (`public/m.html`, the activity feed's top-up row), `合计` is live as this very screen's *Total*, `渠道` as above. It exists because the rows genuinely do not add up to the headline: top-up cash is real money in, booked the day it lands, and a top-up is not an order on any channel. **Rejected:** saying nothing and letting the gap be found by adding the rows up. **Rejected:** folding the money into the mini app, which would make the sum look right and every row wrong |

### Not a Chinese change, and the reason the card is honest

A till sale and a typed-in phone order are **both** `channel='manual'`, and the
column cannot tell them apart — `/sales/summary`'s POS card says so in its own
comment and keys on `fulfillment_type` for that reason. Grouped by channel alone
the two merge into one row: two ways of selling under one name. Counter
fulfilment is split out, and what remains under `manual` keeps the Orders
filter's name.

To be exact about what that split does and does not buy: a till sale was never
going to be counted as a mini-app sale. `manual` is not empty, so it is never
defaulted to `wechat`. The split is worth making because two ways of selling
share a channel — not because either was being misattributed.

And the ranking follows the figure on show: the server sorts by revenue, but an
account that may not see money reads order counts, so the gate picks the
measure and the order, the bar and the value all follow it. A list ordered by a
number the reader is not allowed to see is the fault the product ranking carried
until v1.116.

## Striking a kitchen line off — purchasing (2026-09-18, §174)

The buyer's From-the-kitchen box now carries a ✕ per row: *we are not buying
this one*. **Register: OPERATOR** (Rule 1) — it is a control on the admin's
purchasing screen; the kitchen has no screen of its own for a sent request, so
no customer-register twin exists to keep in step.

**Two pairs are COPIED WHOLE and nothing about them is new.**

| key | EN | CN | provenance |
|---|---|---|---|
| `mrLineRemove` | Remove | 移除 | **COPIED** from the live `Remove · 移除` (staff removal, `public/retail-admin/02-settings-ops.js`) |
| `mrLineRemoved` | Removed ✓ | 已移除 ✓ | **COPIED** from the live `Removed · 已移除`, the same control's own past tense |

**Four are COMPOSED, by Claude, under Rule 4's standing delegation.** All four
are assembled from live fragments — `移除`, `已移除`, `该行`, `采购单`, `采购`,
`厨房申请` — rather than minted; they are declared here because an assembled
sentence is still a composed one.

| key | EN | CN | provenance |
|---|---|---|---|
| `mrLineRemoveTitle` | Remove this line? | 移除该行？ | **COMPOSED** — live `移除` + live `该行` (`public/retail-admin/16-purchasing.js`, returns and receiving) |
| `mrLineRemoveBody` | The kitchen asked for it and it will not be bought. It leaves this list for good — if it is still needed, the kitchen asks again. | 厨房申请的该物料将不会采购，并永久离开此列表。如仍需要，请厨房重新申请。 | **COMPOSED** — live `厨房申请` (the box's own title), `物料`, `采购`, `该行` |
| `already_sourced` | That line is already on a purchase order. | 该行已在采购单上。 | **COMPOSED** — live `该行` + live `采购单` |
| `line_declined` | That line was removed and is no longer being bought. | 该行已移除，不再采购。 | **COMPOSED** — live `已移除` + live `采购` |

**THE BODY SENTENCE CARRIES THE WHOLE DECISION AND IS WHY IT IS NOT SHORTER.**
Striking a line off is one-way (§174): the ask belongs to the kitchen, so there
is no un-strike, and the kitchen raises it again if it is still needed. A confirm
that said only *移除该行？* would be asking about an undoable delete in the words
this product uses for reversible ones. The second clause is the warning, and it
is the reason the pair is composed rather than copied from a shorter live one.

**THREE LIVE CANDIDATES WERE CONSIDERED AND REJECTED**, recorded so the search is
not repeated:

- **`删除`** (live: `Delete draft · 删除草稿`, same file) — the product's word for
  destroying **something you made**. A buyer does not own the kitchen's ask, and
  the row is not deleted: it stays in the record, stamped with who refused it.
- **`作废`** (live: `Void · 作废`, waste and referrals) — voiding reverses an entry
  that already moved stock or money. Nothing has moved here; a line that was
  never bought has nothing to reverse.
- **`撤回`** (live: `Recall · 撤回`, broadcasts; `Withdrawn · 已撤回`, orders) — this
  is the right word for withdrawing the WHOLE request, which `archived_at`
  already does. Using it for one line would blur the two grains §174 exists to
  separate. **It stays reserved for the request.**

**Ash should correct any of the four if they read wrong to a Chinese operator;
nothing depends on the exact phrasing.**

## What became of what you asked for — the kitchen's phone (2026-09-18, §175)

The cook's new screen, *Your buying requests*: what happened to each line of a
material request after it was sent. **Register: CUSTOMER is not involved — this
is the OPERATOR register** (Rule 1), on the staff phone app, and it is the same
register as the buyer's box §174 added, which is why the three state words are
that screen's words and not a second vocabulary for the same three facts.

**Four pairs are COPIED WHOLE, and three of them are the point.** A line that
reads `已移除` to the buyer and something else to the cook would be two screens
disagreeing about one event.

| key | EN | CN | provenance |
|---|---|---|---|
| `mbsWaiting` | Waiting | 待处理 | **COPIED** from the live `Waiting · 待处理` (the Ready-from-the-kitchen tabs, `public/m.html`) |
| `mbsOrdered` | Ordered | 已下单 | **COPIED** from the live `已下单` (`Mark ordered · 标记已下单`, `public/retail-admin/16-purchasing.js`) |
| `mbsRemoved` | Removed | 已移除 | **COPIED** from §174's own pair, which is the whole point: one event, one word on both screens |
| `mbsWithdrawn` | Withdrawn | 已撤回 | **COPIED** from the live `Withdrawn · 已撤回` (`public/retail-admin/04-orders.js`), and it is the request-level word §174 reserved for exactly this |

**Four are COMPOSED, by Claude, under Rule 4's standing delegation**, each
assembled from live fragments rather than minted.

| key | EN | CN | provenance |
|---|---|---|---|
| `mbsTitle` | Your buying requests | 我的采购申请 | **COMPOSED** — live `我的` (`Me · 我的`) + live `采购申请` (the `askbuy` row's own Chinese) |
| `mbsEmptyTitle` | Nothing asked for yet | 还没有申请 | **COMPOSED** — live `还没有` (`No materials yet · 还没有物料`) + live `申请` |
| `mbsEmptyBody` | What you ask the buyer for shows up here, and what became of it. | 你向采购提交的申请及其进展会显示在这里。 | **COMPOSED** — modelled line-for-line on the live `向厨房下的申请在完成前会显示在这里。`, the same sentence for the kitchen's other waiting list |
| `mbsReadFailed` | That list could not be read. Try again. | 无法读取列表，请重试。 | **COMPOSED** — the live `无法读取物料列表，请重试。` with `物料` dropped, because here it is not the material list that failed |

**THE EMPTY BODY SAYS "AND WHAT BECAME OF IT" AND THAT CLAUSE IS THE SCREEN.**
Without it the sentence describes a list of things you sent, which the cook
already knows they sent. The screen exists for the second half — ordered,
waiting, or refused by a named person — so the empty state promises that and not
just a history.

**ONE LIVE CANDIDATE WAS CONSIDERED AND REJECTED.** **`等待`** is live and is the
ordinary word for waiting, but `待处理` is the pair already shipping on the
kitchen's other "what happened to my request" screen, one row up in the same
menu group. Two words for one state across two adjacent screens is the defect
Rule 1 names; the live pair wins.

**Ash should correct any of the four composed pairs if they read wrong to a
Chinese operator; nothing depends on the exact phrasing.**

## Moving a menu to another location — the shop menu (2026-09-18, §177)

The `Move products` exit `public/retail-admin/28-shops.js` gained under
`docs/tacet-consolidation-decisions.md` §177, with its two dialogs and the
three refusals `TD_ERR` answers them with. Register: OPERATOR (Rule 1)
throughout — every string here is read by an operator in the admin, and
nothing on this path is customer-facing. The public QR menu gains no string at
all: a moved-away shop resolves to nothing and serves the not-found page it
already had.

**Four pairs are verbatim, four lift a live term, four are composed.** The
composed four are the ones nothing in this product has ever had to say: that a
menu is about to leave the location it is on, and what that costs in printed
paper.

### Taken verbatim (no new Chinese)

`Location · 门店` (the picker's field label — the same pair this file's own
column heading uses), `Cancel · 取消` and `Saving… · 保存中…` (both already on
this screen), and `Moved · 已移动` — **the success toast, taken from
`catMoveProduct` in `01-core.js`, which moves a product between shelves.** That
is the nearest live act in the product to this one, and an operator who has
moved a product between shelves meets the same word when they move a menu
between locations.

### A live TERM, a new pair

| key | EN | CN | the live source of the term |
|---|---|---|---|
| `shpMoveBtn` | Move products | 移动商品 | `移动到其他分类` (Move to another shelf) for 移动; 商品 from this screen's own `+ 添加商品` and its title 门店商品 |
| `shpMoveVerb` | Move | 移动 | the same 移动 — the bare verb on both dialogs' commit button |
| `shpMoveWhere` | Move products to another location? | 移动商品到其他门店？ | `改到其他门店` (`04-orders.js`, changing which location a pickup happens at) supplies 其他门店 |
| `nothing_to_move` | There is nothing on this menu to move. | 没有可移动的商品。 | `没有可迁移的角色` (`02-settings-ops.js`, "there is nowhere to move them") gives the 没有可…的 shape, with this flow's own 移动 standing where that one has 迁移 |

**The button says `Move products`, not `Move menu`, and the Chinese says
商品 rather than 门店商品 for the same reason.** §177 refuses to move the shop
record: the listings move and the shop and its code stay where they were. A
button reading `Move menu` would promise the thing the section refuses, and the
operator would learn otherwise from a dead QR code.

**One verb, not two.** 移动 and 迁移 both ship in this admin for moving things.
This flow uses 移动 in all four places — button, both commit buttons, and the
refusal — because the toast it ends on is the live `已移动`, and a flow whose
refusal and whose success used different verbs would read as two features.

### Composed

| key | EN | CN |
|---|---|---|
| `shpMoveCount` | Move N products to X? | 移动 N 个商品到 X？ |
| `shpMoveCost` | This menu is emptied and this shop switches off, so the QR already printed here stops working. X has its own code, so a new QR has to be printed there. | 此门店的商品将被清空,门店将停用,已打印的二维码将失效。X 有自己的编码,需要重新打印二维码。 |
| `destination_has_menu` | That location already has a menu of its own. | 该门店已有商品,请选择其他门店。 |
| `same_location` | The menu is already at that location. | 商品已在该门店。 |

**`shpMoveCost` is assembled from live clauses rather than written fresh, and
that is what keeps a four-clause warning readable.** 将被清空 is lifted whole
from `16-purchasing.js`'s undo-receiving warning (`实收与破损数量将被清空`),
which is the same kind of sentence — here is what this button empties. 停用 is
the state word this very screen already prints as `已停用` under its Inactive
pill, put in the future tense for a thing about to happen. 二维码, 编码 and
重新打印 are all live in this admin. **Nothing in the Chinese sentence is a
word an operator has not already read somewhere in this product**; what is
composed is the order they come in.

**`shpMoveCount` and `shpMoveCost` are INTERPOLATED and must not be glued.**
The count and the destination's name are values, and each language places them
itself — `移动 N 个商品到 X？` puts the name against 到 with no space, where the
English needs one. Same terms as `categorySubtotals` in the Finance section.

**The English plural is chosen and the Chinese has none**: `Move 1 product` /
`Move 14 products` against one 个商品 for both, which is the house treatment of
a counted noun everywhere else in this file.

**Two live candidates were considered and rejected, and both rejections are
recorded so the search is not repeated.** `没有可迁移的角色` was the obvious
source for the empty-handed refusal and is NOT reused as-is: it names roles,
and swapping the noun would have left 迁移 in a flow whose other four strings
say 移动 (see above). And `暂无其他门店开通同城配送。` (`04-orders.js`) was
considered for a "nowhere to move this" message and is not needed at all — the
control is hidden when there is no eligible destination, so the sentence has no
place to appear. **A string that no longer has a screen to sit on is better
than a string that explains why a button did nothing.**

## Picking a replacement — the late-parcel case (2026-09-18, §98 addendum 8)

The sheet behind `Send replacement` on both surfaces: which lines of the late
parcel go back out, how many of each, and what the freight costs us. **Register:
CUSTOMER is not involved — this is the OPERATOR register** (Rule 1), and it is
the register the whole §98 panel is already written in.

therefore words this panel already shipped.

| `dcrQty` | Qty | 数量 | **COPIED** from the live items-table header (`public/retail-admin/04-orders.js`) |
| `dcrIn` | In this parcel | 此包裹内含 | **COPIED** from the live parcel-contents strip, one card below |
| `dcrBadQty` | Every line needs a quantity of 1 or more. | 每行数量需为 1 或以上。 | **COPIED** from the live `invalid_item` refusal — the same fault, so the same sentence |

**One pair is RESTRUCTURED**, which is Rule 4's first preference taken over
composing: the live confirm minus the clause that stopped being true.

| key | EN | CN | provenance |
|---|---|---|---|
| `dcrBody` | This creates a no-charge order and takes the goods off the shelf. | 将新建一笔零元订单,并从库存中扣除。 | **RESTRUCTURED** from the live `将新建一笔零元订单发出同样商品,并从库存中扣除。` — `发出同样商品` is dropped because a partial replacement is no longer the same goods |

**Five are COMPOSED, by Claude, under Rule 4's standing delegation**, each
assembled from live fragments rather than minted.

| key | EN | CN | provenance |
|---|---|---|---|
| `dcrShipCost` | Shipping cost | 运费成本 | **COMPOSED** — live `运费` (the order's fee line) + live `成本` (this panel's own cost bit). The two words together are the distinction: `运费` is what a customer is charged, `运费成本` is what the parcel costs us |
| `dcrBadShip` | Enter a shipping cost of 0 or more. | 运费成本需为 0 或以上。 | **COMPOSED** — the live `每行数量需为 1 或以上。` with its subject and its number swapped |
| `dcrPickOne` | Pick at least one line. | 请至少选择一行。 | **COMPOSED** — the live `请至少添加一行。` with `添加` swapped for the live `选择` (`请先选择门店。`), because the lines already exist and are being chosen between |
| `dcrBadItem` | That line is not in this parcel. | 该商品不在此包裹内。 | **COMPOSED** — live `该商品` (`该商品已不存在。`) + the live `不在…内` shape (`该地址不在任何配送区域内。`) + live `此包裹` |
| `dcrTooHigh` | A replacement cannot send more than went out. | 补发不能超过已发出的数量。 | **COMPOSED** — live `补发` + the live refusal shape `退货不能超过已接收的数量。` with `退货`→`补发` and `已接收`→`已发出` (live, from the confirm restructured above) |

**THE THREE REFUSALS ARE WORDED FOR A SCREEN THAT SHOULD NEVER SHOW THEM.** The
sheet checks all three before it posts, so reaching one means a page that has
not been reloaded since the deploy, or a hand-made request. They are written
anyway, because a silent refusal on the one control in this product that sends
real stock is the worse failure.

**`运费` IS NOT RETIRED AND MUST NOT BE.** It is the fee on a customer's order
and it stays exactly where it is; this sheet needed the other half of the pair,
which is why the composed word is two characters longer rather than the same
word used for two different facts.

**Ash should correct any of the five composed pairs if they read wrong to a
Chinese operator; nothing depends on the exact phrasing.**

|---|---|---|---|

| key | EN | CN | provenance |
|---|---|---|---|
| `desc` | Suggestions at the top of a purchase order: the products that store is running low on, each with an Add button. | 采购单顶部的推荐:该门店库存偏低的商品,可直接添加。 | **COMPOSED** — live `采购单` (`16-purchasing.js` `一张采购单就是…`) + live `顶部` (`10-extras.js` `卡片顶部`) + live `库存偏低的…推荐` (the very sentence restructured above) + live `可直接修改。` with the live verb `添加` in place of `修改`. **Rejected:** `推荐补货` — `补货` is the Reorder tab's name and this strip is not that tab |

**Nothing is retired.** The two long empty-state sentences stay live and are

## A parcel is packed

§180. The per-parcel prepared step. Every pair below is lifted; nothing on this
screen is composed.

| where | EN | CN | source |
|---|---|---|---|
| parcel pill | Prepared | 已备货 | `reference/roots_v1.html` warehouse status labels |
| parcel button | Mark this parcel prepared | 标记此包裹已备货 | the live `标记此包裹已发货` in the same card, one word swapped |

Rejected, and why:

- **`待发货`** — live, but spoken for: it is the order list's *to ship* count in
  both back offices. It also names a queue position, not the box's own state, so
  a packed box and an untouched one would wear the same word.
- **`已打包`** — live twice, but it names the physical wrapping, and in a shop
  that sells food it reads as takeaway packaging.

The button appears only on a parcel that is still here, is not supplier-direct,
and sits on an order that is `paid`, `preparing` or `dispatched`.

**AMENDED BY §187 (2026-09-19): `awaiting_restock` NOW APPEARS IN THAT LIST, and
the sentence this replaces is recorded rather than deleted** — it read *"On
`awaiting_restock` it is absent by design (§136 moves no stock; §180 says
why)."* That was right about the danger and wrong about the unit. The goods that
have not landed are the SHORT ones; a parked order's OTHER parcels carry nothing
the shortage touches, and refusing them left TC000784's 陆运 box of four shelf
products with no control at all. The refusal now sits on the parcel
(`parcelHeldLines`), not the order: a box holding a short line is still refused,
and the commit behind the button still cannot take goods that have not landed.
**No word on this screen changed** — the button and the pill keep the pairs
above; only when they are reachable did.

for it either. This is the vocabulary for the switch that was missing and for
the state that was never named. Register: OPERATOR (Rule 1) throughout — every
string is read in the back office by whoever is opening or closing a shop.
